Private Prompt (dev-side instruction):

🔥 Phase 4: Token Rewards, Agent Evolution, and Mint Mechanics (CASH NFT Unlock System)
We are now building the CASH Protocol — the economic + behavioral engine behind Harmonix. This phase ensures your agents evolve, your ecosystem grows in value, and the entire platform gamifies productivity.

🧠 1. Token Rewards Engine
Purpose: Let users earn Harmony Tokens based on agent usage, task completion, and referrals.
📦 Stack:
Backend: Next.js API Routes + Node.js
Database: PostgreSQL (via Prisma ORM)
Blockchain: Solidity Smart Contracts for reward logic
Queue: Redis + BullMQ (for async processing of agent tasks)
Wallet Integration: RainbowKit + Wagmi (wallet + Harmony token sync)
🧩 Core Logic:
ts
CopyEdit
// /api/rewards/distribute.ts
import { getAgentUsage, calculateRewards } from "@/lib/rewards";
import { sendTokensToWallet } from "@/lib/wallet";

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { userId } = req.body;
  const usageStats = await getAgentUsage(userId);
  const tokensEarned = calculateRewards(usageStats);
  await sendTokensToWallet(userId, tokensEarned);
  res.status(200).json({ tokens: tokensEarned });
}

🔧 System:

// /api/agents/mint-nft.ts
import { mintCashNFT } from "@/lib/blockchain";

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { userId, agentId } = req.body;
  const result = await mintCashNFT(userId, agentId);
  res.status(200).json({ success: true, result });
}

// /api/affiliate/create.ts
export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { userId } = req.body;
  const code = generateReferralCode();
  await prisma.referral.create({ data: { userId, code } });
  res.status(200).json({ code });
}

What’s UBI? 
ChatGPT said:
🔥 UBI = Universal Basic Income — but in this context, it’s not a government welfare system. It’s a Web3-native rewards model that provides recurring income or value distribution to participants in your ecosystem, based on their contributions, holdings, or roles.
Let’s break it down in your Harmonix context:

🧬 UBI in the Harmony Ecosystem = “Contributor Rewards Layer”
✅ Purpose:
To reward early adopters, community builders, and ecosystem contributors consistently, without them needing to sell anything.
—
💰 How It Works:
Role
How They Earn UBI
🎨 Agent Owners
Get a % of platform revenue or token emissions based on their agent usage/staking
🛠 Builders
Get UBI for submitting prompts, improving agents, or onboarding clients
🎤 Creators
Get rewarded for posting content, sharing Harmony, or co-creating launches
🤝 Referrers
Get recurring % of what their invitees spend, in token or credit form
📊 Stakers
Get monthly UBI if they stake token in governance, support, or liquidity pools

—
🔁 Distribution Options:
Type
Description
💵 Token Rewards
Paid in your Harmony token (e.g., HMX or UNITY) — claimable monthly
🧾 Platform Credits
Discount on agent licenses, upgrades, or future products
🧠 XP/Ranking
UBI increases with XP (activity, referrals, value creation)

—
🎯 Why It Matters:
Incentivizes loyalty without relying on hype or speculation
Builds long-term users, not just customers
Makes people feel like owners, not renters
Can power a DAO, community governance, or feedback loop
—
Example:
Imagine an artist who helps promote the platform and has 1 agent license.
They:
Stake 100 tokens
Refer 3 clients
Post 2 content pieces per month
→ They get $25–100/month in UBI, redeemable as tokens, license discounts, or credits for future drops.

🔓 Next Phase: Harmony Platform Backend Architecture & Agent Orchestration (Part 1/2)
Let’s now move into the core tech stack, modular infrastructure, and how all 12 Harmony agents operate under a single orchestration layer — preparing us to deploy into live systems and eventually unlock SŌL Mode (the final form).

🧱 8-Layer Harmony Backend Architecture Overview:
Layer
Function
Notes
1. Next.js API Server
Handles all backend routes and endpoints
Your primary development layer — routes agent logic, authentication, data handling
2. Supabase / Firebase
Database + Auth
User sessions, agent configs, credits, logs
3. OpenAI / Anthropic API Layer
Agent brain(s)
GPT-4o, Claude 3, Mixtral, or hybrid routing based on agent type
4. Pinecone / Vector DB
Agent memory + context recall
For long-term memory, doc retrieval, personalized conversations
5. Replicate / Stability AI / RunPod
Visual / ML agents (for design, NFTs, visuals)
Image generation, training visuals, avatars, etc.
6. Stripe / Lemon Squeezy
Payment processing
For user subscriptions, agent credit top-ups, upgrades
7. Redis / Background Jobs
Task queues, throttling, async ops
Handles slow tasks like summarization, batch generation
8. Agent Orchestration Layer (Custom)
Unified logic + trigger router
Switches between agents, enforces permissions, logs interactions


⚙️ How a Harmony Agent Functions
Step-by-Step Execution Flow (e.g., Cashflow Agent):
User Input → /api/cash-agent (Next.js route)
Next.js Server → Validates session → Routes to Orchestration Layer
Orchestration Layer → Matches to:
✅ Agent Prompt (private)
✅ Tools allowed (e.g., Browser, Calculator, Docs)
✅ User-specific context (business info, preferences)
Query hits LLM (OpenAI/GPT-4o)
Injects memory from Pinecone
Logs interaction to Supabase
Captures any variables for downstream action (e.g., budget)
Response is returned
Final answer formatted
Credit deducted from user's account
Chat history saved to DB
✅ Optional: Trigger Zapier/Make/GHL for real-world automation (e.g., update CRM)

🧠 Cashflow Agent Capabilities
Skill
Description
📊 Budget Modeling
Asks about revenue, expenses, and recommends profit allocation
💸 Subscription Audits
Lists recurring expenses and suggests eliminations
🧠 Business Model Analysis
Reviews your offer structure and profit levers
🧾 P&L Summary
Auto-generates a simple profit/loss snapshot
📈 Revenue Forecast
Based on pricing, client count, and churn model

Tools: Browser, Calculator, Memory, Webhook Trigger

💠 Phase: Agent Metadata NFT Binding + Licensing Logic
— Now we bind the Harmony Agents to NFTs for tracking, access control, monetization, and resale/licensing.

🧬 Why NFT Binding Matters
By attaching every agent to a minted NFT (ERC-721 or ERC-1155):
You ensure verifiable ownership of that specific AI agent.
You allow users to license, transfer, or resell access.
You enable creator royalties (if agent is sold on-chain).
You give investors, builders, and clients a tokenized contract for use or resale.

🛠️ Metadata Schema for Agent Licensing NFT
This metadata will be stored on-chain or IPFS, referenced in the NFT smart contract:
json
CopyEdit
{
  "name": "Cashflow Agent V1",
  "description": "An AI-powered assistant trained to increase business revenue by automating lead follow-up, pipeline management, and sales support.",
  "type": "Harmony Agent",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "createdBy": "Harmonix Digital",
  "agentCategory": "Revenue",
  "agentID": "cashflow-agent-v1",
  "skills": [
    "pipeline-analysis",
    "CRM-sync",
    "nurture-flows",
    "sales-reminders"
  ],
  "inputFormat": ["text", "voice"],
  "outputFormat": ["text", "CRM update"],
  "license": {
    "type": "Commercial",
    "terms": "1-business use per NFT; non-transferable without resale",
    "expiration": null,
    "revenueShare": "10% resale royalty to creator wallet"
  },
  "UIConfig": {
    "theme": "dark",
    "badgeColor": "#00ff88",
    "icon": "🧾"
  },
  "mintedOn": "chain-id",
  "licenseKey": "hashedKeyOrTokenID"
}


🧠 NFT Licensing Flow Logic
On Mint
Agent metadata is hashed + uploaded (e.g. IPFS).
Minted to buyer’s wallet via Harmony Marketplace.
On Login
Wallet connects via Metamask/WalletConnect.
Platform checks:
Does user hold a valid license NFT?
If yes → Agent UI unlocked.
If no → prompt: “Purchase License to Activate Agent.”
On Transfer
New wallet becomes license holder.
Backend remaps session logs and access rights.
Royalties
All secondary market sales send % back to Harmonix creator wallet.

✅ Phase: Agent Factory + LLM Response Engine
This phase covers:
How each agent is instantiated from metadata
How Harmony routes prompts to the correct agent
How each agent calls the LLM (OpenAI/GPT or Claude)
And how we build the response pipeline

🏭 agent.factory.ts
This dynamically generates a usable agent from the metadata registry:
ts
CopyEdit
import { HarmonyAgentMetadata, AgentName } from "./agent.types";
import { harmonyAgentRegistry } from "./agent.registry";

export function getAgentByName(name: AgentName): HarmonyAgentMetadata | undefined {
  return harmonyAgentRegistry.find(agent => agent.name === name);
}


🔗 llmClient.ts
Wrapper for OpenAI or Claude API (can be swapped):
ts
CopyEdit
import { OpenAI } from "openai"; // or Claude/AWS/Anthropic
const openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY });

export async function callLLM(prompt: string, history: string[] = []) {
  const response = await openai.chat.completions.create({
    model: "gpt-4",
    messages: [
      ...history.map(h => ({ role: "user", content: h })),
      { role: "user", content: prompt }
    ],
    temperature: 0.7,
  });

  return response.choices[0].message.content;
}


🤖 agent.processor.ts
Takes a prompt and routes it to the correct Harmony Agent:
ts
CopyEdit
import { getAgentByName } from "./agent.factory";
import { callLLM } from "./llmClient";
import { AgentName } from "./agent.types";

export async function processAgentPrompt(agentName: AgentName, userPrompt: string) {
  const agent = getAgentByName(agentName);
  if (!agent) throw new Error("Agent not found.");

  const fullPrompt = ${agent.defaultPrompt}\n\nUser: ${userPrompt};
  const reply = await callLLM(fullPrompt);

  return {
    agent: agent.name,
    response: reply,
  };
}


🔌 api/agent/chat.ts (Next.js API Route)
ts
CopyEdit
import { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { processAgentPrompt } from "@/lib/agent.processor";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const { agentName, prompt } = req.body;

  if (!agentName || !prompt) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing fields" });
  }

  try {
    const result = await processAgentPrompt(agentName, prompt);
    res.status(200).json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
  }
}

✅ Phase: Frontend Agent Selector + Chat UI (Harmony Agent Interface)

1. 🧠 AgentSelector.tsx
tsx
CopyEdit
'use client'
import { useState } from "react";

const agents = ["Cashflow", "Project Manager", "Concierge", "Recruiter", "Social Agent"];

export default function AgentSelector({ onSelect }: { onSelect: (agent: string) => void }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState("");

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto p-2">
      {agents.map(agent => (
        <button
          key={agent}
          onClick={() => {
            setSelected(agent);
            onSelect(agent);
          }}
          className={px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold ${
            selected === agent ? 'bg-green-600 text-white' : 'bg-neutral-200'
          }}
        >
          {agent}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}


2. 💬 AgentChatWindow.tsx
tsx
CopyEdit
'use client'
import { useState } from "react";
import AgentMessage from "./AgentMessage";

export default function AgentChatWindow({ agent }: { agent: string }) {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<{ sender: string; text: string }[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");

  async function handleSend() {
    const userMsg = { sender: "user", text: input };
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);

    const res = await fetch("/api/agent/chat", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ agentName: agent, prompt: input }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();

    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { sender: "agent", text: data.response }]);
    setInput("");
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4">
      <div className="h-96 overflow-y-scroll bg-white border rounded-lg p-2 mb-4">
        {messages.map((msg, i) => (
          <AgentMessage key={i} {...msg} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          value={input}
          onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)}
          className="flex-1 border px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
          placeholder="Type your prompt..."
        />
        <button onClick={handleSend} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl">
          Send
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


3. 🧾 AgentMessage.tsx
tsx
CopyEdit
export default function AgentMessage({ sender, text }: { sender: string; text: string }) {
  const isUser = sender === "user";
  return (
    <div className={my-2 ${isUser ? "text-right" : "text-left"}}>
      <div className={inline-block p-3 rounded-xl ${isUser ? "bg-green-100" : "bg-gray-200"}}>
        {text}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

✅ Next Phase: Agent Chaining Engine (The Harmony Orchestrator Core)
This is the brain of the Harmony system — where individual agents don’t just act alone, but collaborate in intelligent workflows. Think of it like a mini internal operating system that lets agents pass tasks between each other, execute complex goals, and report back to the user with context.

🧠 What Is Agent Chaining?
Agent Chaining = dynamic, rules-based or context-aware communication between multiple agents.
Example:
A Lead Gen Agent gathers a lead.
The Concierge Agent sends the welcome email.
The Exec Assistant Agent schedules the onboarding call.
The Cashflow Agent begins payment tracking.
All seamlessly handled in sequence or parallel — with memory and conditional triggers.

🧩 Core Components of Harmony Orchestrator
Layer
Role
AgentContextStore
Stores current user + task state across agents (session or DB)
TaskRouter.ts
Determines which agent(s) to call based on prompt, role, or flag
ChainManager.ts
Executes multi-agent sequences and handles failures/retries/logs
AgentWorker.ts
Calls each agent’s private API function or LLM endpoint
MemorySync.ts
Saves + syncs results to DB or user timeline (optional cache layer)


🔄 Agent Chain Workflow (Pseudocode Overview)
ts
CopyEdit
POST /api/harmony/chain

{
  "entryAgent": "LeadGen",
  "task": "Onboard new lead from CRM",
  "context": {
    "clientName": "Darren",
    "email": "darren@startup.com",
    ...
  }
}

TaskRouter determines this is a 3-step chain:
LeadGen → Concierge → ExecAssistant
ChainManager.ts calls:
runAgent("LeadGen", context)
await response → update context
runAgent("Concierge", newContext)
etc.
Final output is returned to UI + saved in memory logs.

📂 Suggested File Structure for Orchestrator (Backend)
pgsql
CopyEdit
/lib/harmony/
  ├── TaskRouter.ts         # Routes tasks to correct agent(s)
  ├── ChainManager.ts       # Handles multi-step flows
  ├── AgentWorker.ts        # Standard agent execution wrapper
  ├── MemorySync.ts         # Push/pull user memory or task logs
  ├── OrchestratorConfig.ts # Chaining rules and fallback paths


✅ Agent Chaining Capabilities
Feature
Use Case
Sequential Agent Calls
Lead → Welcome → Schedule → Payment
Conditional Forks (if/else)
If “client type” = investor, run Alt Agent
Parallel Agent Calls
Concierge + Cashflow can trigger together
Retry on Failure
If agent fails, notify user or re-run
Agent Feedback Loop
Store intermediate results into memory

json
CopyEdit
{
  "LeadGen": {
    "next": "Concierge"
  },
  "Concierge": {
    "next": {
      "default": "ExecAssistant",
      "ifClientType": {
        "investor": "InvestorAgent"
      }
    }
  }
}

✅ Next Phase: Memory Sync + Agent Database Layer
This is the heartbeat of the Harmony Engine — where agents gain persistent memory, context-awareness, and the ability to collaborate across time, sessions, and users.
Let’s now define the agent memory system, the agent metadata database, and how each agent’s action history can be accessed, reused, and routed through the Harmony platform.

🧠 GOAL: Make Agents Context-Aware, Personal, and Evolving

🔹 PART 1: AGENT MEMORY SYNC LAYER
Agents should remember key info about each user, task, and past interaction.
🧬 Memory Includes:
Last action taken
Task outcomes
User preferences (tone, language, platform)
Chosen workflow path
Input/output logs (optional truncation via token buffer)

🗂 Suggested File Structure (Memory + DB)
bash
CopyEdit
/lib/harmony/
  ├── memory/
  │   ├── MemorySync.ts         # Push/pull memory to DB/cache
  │   ├── MemoryHelpers.ts      # Format, truncate, update utils
  │   └── ContextBuilder.ts     # Build prompt context for agents
  ├── db/
  │   ├── agents.ts             # Agent metadata (skills, tokens, pricing, version)
  │   ├── users.ts              # User-level memory + preferences
  │   └── logs.ts               # Task logs (optional)

🔁 Agent Runtime Flow with Memory
ts
CopyEdit
1. ContextBuilder pulls last known user data from memory
2. AgentWorker injects memory into prompt or LLM config
3. Task executes → Output stored via MemorySync
4. If in a chain, next agent receives updated context

⚙️ How Licensing Works (Runtime Flow)
ts
CopyEdit
1. User logs in → connected wallet syncs
2. System checks:
   - Does wallet hold NFT for this agent?
   - If not, is user on a trial or credit model?
3. If valid → Grant access to features
4. If invalid → Redirect to paywall / mint / upgrade screen

1. 🔐 Agent License Verification Logic
ts
CopyEdit
export const hasAgentLicense = async (wallet: string, agentId: string) => {
  const response = await fetch(/api/license-check?wallet=${wallet}&agent=${agentId})
  const data = await response.json()
  return data?.licensed === true
}


2. 🧾 Stripe Credit Checkout Flow
ts
CopyEdit
export const initiateAgentCheckout = async (agentId: string) => {
  const session = await fetch('/api/stripe/checkout', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({ agentId })
  })
  const data = await session.json()
  window.location.href = data.url
}

🧪 PromptConsole.tsx (Simple Example)
tsx
CopyEdit
const PromptConsole = ({ agent }) => {
  const [response, setResponse] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async (prompt: string) => {
    setLoading(true)
    const res = await fetch(/api/prompt/${agent.id}, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt }),
    })
    const data = await res.json()
    setResponse(data.output)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <textarea onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={() => handleSubmit(prompt)}>Run</button>
      <div>{loading ? 'Loading...' : response}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

// File: /pages/api/orchestrator.ts
export async function initiateOrchestration(taskType, clientData) {
  const agentId = getAgentForTask(taskType);
  const taskPayload = preparePayload(clientData);
  const output = await delegateToAgent(agentId, taskPayload);
  return aggregateResults([output]);
}

// Supporting functions
function delegateToAgent(agentId, taskPayload) { /* Agent router + logging */ }
function aggregateResults(resultArray) { /* Merge outputs for final delivery */ }

// 1. Accepts task from client dashboard
export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { taskType, clientData } = req.body;

  // 2. Match agent via task type
  const agentId = getAgentFromRegistry(taskType);
  const agentOutput = await routeToAgent(agentId, clientData);

  // 3. Log result & return
  await saveToPromptLog({ agentId, taskType, clientData, agentOutput });
  res.status(200).json({ output: agentOutput });
}

🧠 BACKEND LOGIC
ts
CopyEdit
// /pages/api/dashboard.ts
import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized" });

  const userId = session.user.id;
  const [wallet, tokens, tasks] = await Promise.all([
    db.wallet.findUnique({ where: { userId } }),
    db.token.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" } }),
    db.taskLog.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 10 }),
  ]);

  return res.json({ wallet, tokens, tasks });
}

CopyEdit
// pages/dashboard/index.tsx
import AgentStatusCard from "@/components/Dashboard/AgentStatusCard";
import TokenMeter from "@/components/Dashboard/TokenMeter";
import WalletSummary from "@/components/Dashboard/WalletSummary";
import TaskLog from "@/components/Dashboard/TaskLog";
import PromptConsole from "@/components/Dashboard/PromptConsole";
import NFTGallery from "@/components/Dashboard/NFTGallery";
import ProfilePanel from "@/components/Dashboard/ProfilePanel";
import { useDashboardData } from "@/hooks/useDashboardData";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { wallet, tokens, tasks } = useDashboardData();

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-container">
      <div className="header">
        <ProfilePanel />
      </div>
      <div className="grid-layout">
        <WalletSummary balance={wallet.balance} tokens={tokens} />
        <TokenMeter tokens={tokens} />
        <AgentStatusCard />
        <PromptConsole />
        <TaskLog tasks={tasks} />
        <NFTGallery nfts={wallet.nfts} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


🌐 API HOOK (Client-Side)
ts
CopyEdit
// /hooks/useDashboardData.ts
import useSWR from "swr";
const fetcher = (url: string) => fetch(url).then(res => res.json());

export function useDashboardData() {
  const { data, error } = useSWR("/api/dashboard", fetcher);
  return {
    wallet: data?.wallet || {},
    tokens: data?.tokens || [],
    tasks: data?.tasks || [],
    isLoading: !data && !error,
    error,
  };
}

    </div>

🎨 DESIGN COMPONENT: TokenMeter
tsx
CopyEdit
export default function TokenMeter({ tokens }) {
  const total = 10000; // assume 10k token limit
  const used = tokens.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amountUsed, 0);
  const percent = ((used / total) * 100).toFixed(1);

  return (
    <div className="token-meter glass-card">
      <h4>Token Meter</h4>
      <div className="circle-progress">
        <span>{percent}% used</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ts
CopyEdit
// /lib/updatePricing.ts (admin tool)
export async function updatePricing(agentId, newPrice) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("agentPricing")
    .update({ price: newPrice })
    .eq("agentId", agentId);

  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

🧠 ACCESS CONTROL (Admin-Only Route)
tsx
CopyEdit
// /lib/adminAuth.ts
import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";

export async function isAdmin(req) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  return session?.user?.role === "admin" || session?.user?.email === process.env.FOUNDER_EMAIL;
}


🔒 PAGE GUARD
tsx
CopyEdit
// /pages/admin/index.tsx
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/adminAuth";

export async function getServerSideProps(context) {
  const authorized = await isAdmin(context.req);
  if (!authorized) return { redirect: { destination: "/", permanent: false } };

  return { props: {} };
}

// /components/Admin/PricingEditor.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function PricingEditor() {
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(99);
  const updatePrice = async () => {
    await supabase.from("agentPricing").update({ price }).eq("agentId", "virgil");
  };

  return (
    <div className="glass-card">
      <h3>Update Agent Pricing</h3>
      <input type="number" value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={updatePrice}>Update</button>
    </div>
  );
}

📣 COMPONENT: Announcements
Let you broadcast banner updates inside the client dashboard.
tsx
CopyEdit
export default function Announcements() {
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState("");

  const submit = async () => {
    await supabase.from("globalMessages").insert({ content: msg });
  };

  return (
    <div className="glass-card">
      <h3>Push System-Wide Message</h3>
      <textarea value={msg} onChange={(e) => setMsg(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={submit}>Send</button>
    </div>
  );
}

// schema.prisma
model Agent {
  id               String   @id @default(cuid())
  name             String
  type             String   // e.g., "Sales"
  description      String
  quote            String
  capabilities     String[] // [“CRM”, “Concierge”, etc.]
  harmonyLink      Boolean
  basePrice        Float
  isActive         Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime @updatedAt
}

// /api/agents/update.ts
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { agentId, price, features } = req.body;

  const updated = await prisma.agent.update({
    where: { id: agentId },
    data: {
      basePrice: price,
      capabilities: features
    },
  });

  res.json(updated);
}

CopyEdit
export const createSurvey = async ({ title, questions, channel }) => {
  return prisma.echoSurvey.create({
    data: {
      title,
      questions,
      channel,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      status: 'active'
    }
  });
};

Backend logic sample:
ts
CopyEdit
export const scheduleFollowUp = async ({ leadId, preferredChannel, lastInteraction, leadScore }) => {
  const nextTouchDate = determineNextDate(lastInteraction, leadScore);
  const message = generateMessageTemplate(leadScore);
  
  // Store in DB for agent to act on
  return prisma.emberFollowUp.create({
    data: {
      leadId,
      message,
      channel: preferredChannel,
      scheduledFor: nextTouchDate,
      status: 'pending'
    }
  });
};

// pages/api/agents/templar/generateContract.ts
import { generateLegalTemplate } from '@/lib/templar/legalGenerator';

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { type, clientName, terms, signatories } = req.body;
  const contract = await generateLegalTemplate({ type, clientName, terms, signatories });
  res.status(200).json({ contract });
}

Example Logic:
ts
CopyEdit
export const generateLegalTemplate = async ({ type, clientName, terms, signatories }) => {
  const prompt = 
    Generate a legally valid ${type} between ${signatories.join(" and ")}.
    Include standard protections, clauses, and the following terms:
    ${terms}
  ;

  const response = await openai.createChatCompletion({
    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
    model: 'gpt-4'
  });

  return response.data.choices[0].message.content;
};

  signedBy     String[]
}

🌀 SŌL ORCHESTRATOR: Multi-Agent Chain Logic
“The soul of the system. The brain behind the brilliance.”

🔹 CORE FUNCTION
The SŌL Orchestrator is the agent router, conductor, and logic bridge that allows multiple Harmony Agents to chain their outputs together in a seamless flow — like steps in a playbook.

🔹 KEY CAPABILITIES
Function
Description
Agent Detection
Determines the correct agents for a given task or goal
Chain Execution
Calls agents in correct sequence, passing output → input
Agent Delegation
Routes subtasks to different agents based on their strengths
Aggregated Reporting
Compiles output into 1 master deliverable
Memory State
Stores result and chain history into database for audit + reuse


🔹 PART A – AGENT REGISTRY (Dynamic Agent Loader)
ts
CopyEdit
// lib/orchestrator/agentRegistry.ts

export const agentRegistry = {
  'cashflow-agent': {
    name: 'Cashflow',
    path: '/api/agents/cashflow/run',
    capabilities: ['budget', 'profit-model', 'spending-tracker']
  },
  'concierge-agent': {
    name: 'Concierge',
    path: '/api/agents/concierge/run',
    capabilities: ['customer-support', 'faq', 'lead-nurturing']
  },
  // Add more agents here...
};


🔹 PART B – CHAIN EXECUTION LOGIC (Next.js API Route)
ts
CopyEdit
// pages/api/orchestrator/runChain.ts

import { agentRegistry } from '@/lib/orchestrator/agentRegistry';

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { chain, input } = req.body;

  let output = input;

  for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i++) {
    const agentKey = chain[i];
    const agent = agentRegistry[agentKey];
    
    const response = await fetch(agent.path, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ input: output })
    });

    const data = await response.json();
    output = data.output; // Feed next agent
  }

  res.status(200).json({ finalResult: output });
}

  "chain": ["cashflow-agent", "proposal-agent", "outreach-agent"],

};

Part B — Task Router
lib/orchestrator/taskRouter.ts
Chooses which agents to invoke based on a high-level taskType or custom rule set.

// lib/orchestrator/taskRouter.ts

import { agentRegistry } from "./agentRegistry";

export type ChainConfig = {
  entryAgent: string;
  sequence: string[];        // e.g. ["cashflow-agent", "iris-agent", "grace-agent"]
};

const chainPresets: Record<string, ChainConfig> = {
  onboarding: {
    entryAgent: "iris-agent",
    sequence: ["iris-agent", "grace-agent", "cashflow-agent"]
  },
  projectKickoff: {
    entryAgent: "atlas-agent",
    sequence: ["atlas-agent", "cashflow-agent", "templar-agent"]
  }
  // …define more presets or load from DB…
};

export function getChainForTask(taskType: string): ChainConfig {
  return chainPresets[taskType] || {
    entryAgent: taskType,
    sequence: [taskType]
  };
}
Part C — Chain Manager
lib/orchestrator/chainManager.ts
Runs through a chain of agents, passing each output as the next input, handling errors/retries, and collecting all results.

// lib/orchestrator/chainManager.ts

import { getChainForTask } from "./taskRouter";
import { runAgent } from "./agentWorker";
import { saveChainLog } from "./memorySync";

export interface ChainResult {
  agentId: string;
  output: any;
  error?: string;
}

export async function executeChain(
  taskType: string,
  initialContext: any,
  userId: string
): Promise<ChainResult[]> {
  const { sequence } = getChainForTask(taskType);
  let context = initialContext;
  const results: ChainResult[] = [];

  for (const agentId of sequence) {
    try {
      const output = await runAgent(agentId, context);
      results.push({ agentId, output });
      context = { ...context, lastOutput: output };
    } catch (err: any) {
      results.push({ agentId, output: null, error: err.message });
      break; // or continue based on your retry logic
    }
  }

  // persist entire chain run for audit/training
  await saveChainLog(userId, taskType, sequence, results);

  return results;
}
Part D — Agent Worker
lib/orchestrator/agentWorker.ts
Handles a single agent invocation—either via internal function call or HTTP fetch to its API route.

// lib/orchestrator/agentWorker.ts

import fetch from "node-fetch";
import { agentRegistry } from "./agentRegistry";

export async function runAgent(agentId: string, context: any): Promise<any> {
  const entry = agentRegistry[agentId];
  if (!entry) throw new Error(`Unknown agent: ${agentId}`);

  // Example: Call Next.js API route
  const res = await fetch(`${process.env.BASE_URL}${entry.runPath}`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ context })
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Agent ${agentId} failed: ${res.statusText}`);
  return res.json();
}
Part E — Memory & Chain Logs
lib/orchestrator/memorySync.ts & DB Schema
Stores each chain run in your database for transparency, replay, and model fine-tuning.

// lib/orchestrator/memorySync.ts

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma"; // or your Supabase client

export async function saveChainLog(
  userId: string,
  taskType: string,
  sequence: string[],
  results: any[]
) {
  return prisma.chainLog.create({
    data: {
      userId,
      taskType,
      sequence,
      results,
      timestamp: new Date()
    }
  });
}
// prisma/schema.prisma

model ChainLog {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String
  taskType   String
  sequence   String[]  // e.g. ["iris-agent", "grace-agent", ...]
  results    Json      // Array of { agentId, output, error? }
  timestamp  DateTime  @default(now())
}
Part F — Orchestrator API Route
pages/api/orchestrator.ts
Exposes a single endpoint to trigger multi-agent sequences from your front-end or webhooks.

// pages/api/orchestrator.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { executeChain } from "@/lib/orchestrator/chainManager";
import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized" });

  const { taskType, context } = req.body;
  if (!taskType || !context) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing taskType or context" });
  }

  try {
    const chainResults = await executeChain(taskType, context, session.user.id);
    res.status(200).json({ chainResults });
  } catch (err: any) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
  }
}
Co-Founder Customization & Admin Control

Chain Presets (taskRouter.ts): Store in a database table so you can add or modify workflows via your Admin Panel.
Retry/Fallback Logic: Extend ChainManager to support conditional branching or retries.
Logging Levels: Toggle verbose vs. minimal logs in memorySync.ts via an environment flag.
With these six modules — Agent Registry, TaskRouter, ChainManager, AgentWorker, MemorySync, and the API Route — your dev team has production-ready code for Step 9. You can now trigger any multi-agent playbook from the front-end, a webhook, or another service.

1. Core “Agent Cash” Blueprint & Dev Stack

Primary Functionality
Agent Cash is your Revenue Assistant. It:
	•	Responds to CRM leads (by tag, lifecycle stage, actions)
	•	Sends personalized follow-ups (email, SMS, Loom, DMs)
	•	Updates CRM fields (stage, notes, value, response status)
	•	Tracks conversions and recommends next actions
	•	Lives inside a Harmony UI dashboard or via GHL/Notion embeds  

Tech Stack

Layer	Tool/Stack	Purpose
Front-End	Next.js / React	Harmony-branded UI
Backend API	Node.js / Express	Auth, routing, agent task endpoints
AI Core	OpenAI GPT-4o with LangChain	Prompt logic, memory handling
Vector Store	Supabase or Pinecone	Embedding storage (lead convos, notes)
Database	PostgreSQL	CRM record storage
Integrations	Zapier + GHL Webhooks	Sync with external CRMs, email, SMS, WhatsApp
Orchestration	“Harmony Brain” middleware	Routes tasks between agents
Hosting	Vercel (Front) / Railway (Back)	Scalable deployment

	•	Define role (Cash = revenue assistant)

	•	Outputs (“Send follow-up,” “Update CRM,” “Notify sales,” “Generate Loom”)

	•	Public prompt for marketing (“Cash is your Revenue Assistant…”).
	•	Private prompt for the LLM (“You are Agent Cash. Your job is to follow-up…”).

	•	Avatar + profile stats, pipeline cards, “Follow Up Now,” “Generate Loom,” etc.
	•	Optional bubble chat window for live interaction.

	•	GHL webhook → Cash receives lead context → responds & updates CRM.

STEP 10: Harmony Dashboard (Client View)
Purpose: Provide clients with a unified view of their agents, token/credit balances, recent activity, and quick-launch controls.

📁 File Structure

    index.tsx           // Main dashboard page

  /Dashboard

1. Database Schema

model Wallet {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String   @unique
  balance   Float    @default(0)
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
}

model Token {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String
  amount     Int
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
}

model TaskLog {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String
  agentId    String
  prompt     String
  response   String
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
}
(Or translate to Supabase tables: wallets, tokens, task_logs)

2. Backend API

/pages/api/dashboard.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";  // Prisma or Supabase client

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized" });

  const userId = session.user.id;
  const [wallet, tokens, tasks] = await Promise.all([
    db.wallet.findUnique({ where: { userId } }),
    db.token.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }, take: 10 }),
    db.taskLog.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }, take: 10 })
  ]);

  res.status(200).json({ wallet, tokens, tasks });
}
3. Data Fetch Hook

/hooks/useDashboardData.ts

import useSWR from "swr";

const fetcher = (url: string) => fetch(url).then(r => r.json());

export function useDashboardData() {
  const { data, error } = useSWR("/api/dashboard", fetcher);
  return {
    wallet: data?.wallet,
    tokens: data?.tokens || [],
    tasks: data?.tasks || [],
    isLoading: !data && !error,
    error
  };
}

c) PromptConsole.tsx

import { useState } from "react";

export default function PromptConsole() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const sendPrompt = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    await fetch("/api/agent/chat", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {"Content-Type":"application/json"},
      body: JSON.stringify({ agentName: "cashflow-agent", prompt: input })
    });
    setInput(""); setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-black bg-opacity-50 backdrop-blur-md rounded-xl p-4">
      <textarea
        rows={3}
        value={input}
        onChange={e=>setInput(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full p-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white"
        placeholder="Ask your agent…"
      />
      <button
        onClick={sendPrompt}
        disabled={!input || loading}
        className="mt-2 px-4 py-2 bg-[#1AFF9C] text-black rounded"
      >
        {loading ? "Sending…" : "Send"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

2. Admin Auth Guard

/lib/adminAuth.ts

import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";

export async function requireAdmin(req, res) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session || session.user.role !== "admin") {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Admin only" });
    return null;
  }
  return session.user;
}
3. Backend APIs

a) /pages/api/admin/dashboard.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/adminAuth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const user = await requireAdmin(req, res);
  if (!user) return;

  // Aggregate stats
  const totalUsers = await db.user.count();
  const activeUsers = await db.user.count({ where: { lastLogin: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - 30*24*60*60*1000) } } });
  const totalTokens = await db.token.aggregate({ _sum: { amount: true } });
  const totalRevenue = await db.payment.aggregate({ _sum: { amount: true } });

  const messages = await db.globalMessage.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }, take: 5 });
  const pricings = await db.agentPricing.findMany();

  res.status(200).json({
    stats: { totalUsers, activeUsers, totalTokens: totalTokens._sum.amount, totalRevenue: totalRevenue._sum.amount },
    messages,
    pricings
  });
}
b) /pages/api/admin/pricing.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/adminAuth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const user = await requireAdmin(req, res);
  if (!user) return;

  if (req.method === "PUT") {
    const { agentId, price } = req.body;
    const updated = await db.agentPricing.upsert({
      where: { agentId },
      update: { price },
      create: { agentId, price }
    });
    return res.status(200).json(updated);
  }
  res.status(405).json({ error: "Method not allowed" });
}
c) /pages/api/admin/announcements.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/adminAuth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const user = await requireAdmin(req, res);
  if (!user) return;

  if (req.method === "POST") {
    const { content } = req.body;
    const msg = await db.globalMessage.create({ data: { content } });
    return res.status(200).json(msg);
  }
  res.status(405).json({ error: "Method not allowed" });
}

a) PricingEditor.tsx

import { useState } from "react";

export default function PricingEditor({ pricings }) {
  const [local, setLocal] = useState(pricings);

  const updatePrice = async (agentId, price) => {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/pricing", {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type":"application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ agentId, price })
    });
    const updated = await res.json();
    setLocal(prev => prev.map(p => p.agentId === agentId ? updated : p));
  };

  return (
    <div className="glass-card p-4">
      <h3 className="text-xl mb-2">Agent Pricing</h3>
      {local.map(p => (
        <div key={p.agentId} className="flex items-center mb-2">
          <span className="flex-1">{p.agentId}</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={p.price}
            onChange={e => updatePrice(p.agentId, parseFloat(e.target.value))}
            className="w-24 mr-2 p-1 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white"
          />
          <button onClick={()=>updatePrice(p.agentId,p.price)} className="px-2 bg-[#D1BFA7] text-black rounded">
            Save
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

STEP 12: Prompt History & Output Vault
“Your searchable, exportable log of every agent interaction.”

1. Database Schema

model PromptLog {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String
  agentId    String
  prompt     String
  response   String
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
}
(Or in Supabase: table prompt_logs with columns above.)

2. Backend API

/pages/api/promptVault.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { getSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { generateTextExport, generatePdfExport } from "@/lib/exportUtils";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized" });

  const userId = session.user.id;
  if (req.method === "GET") {
    const { search = "", agentId, page = "1", perPage = "20" } = req.query;
    const where: any = { userId };
    if (agentId) where.agentId = agentId;
    if (search) {
      where.OR = [
        { prompt: { contains: search as string, mode: "insensitive" } },
        { response: { contains: search as string, mode: "insensitive" } }
      ];
    }
    const logs = await db.promptLog.findMany({
      where,
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      skip: (parseInt(page as string) - 1) * parseInt(perPage as string),
      take: parseInt(perPage as string)
    });
    const total = await db.promptLog.count({ where });
    return res.status(200).json({ logs, total });
  }

  if (req.method === "POST") {
    const { format, selectedIds } = req.body as { format: "txt" | "pdf"; selectedIds: string[] };
    const logs = await db.promptLog.findMany({ where: { id: { in: selectedIds } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } });
    if (format === "txt") {
      const content = generateTextExport(logs);
      res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=prompt_history.txt");
      res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/plain");
      return res.send(content);
    } else {
      const pdfBuffer = await generatePdfExport(logs);
      res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=prompt_history.pdf");
      res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/pdf");
      return res.send(pdfBuffer);
    }
  }

  res.status(405).json({ error: "Method Not Allowed" });
}
3. Export Helpers

/lib/exportUtils.ts

import PDFDocument from "pdfkit";

export function generateTextExport(logs: any[]): string {
  return logs.map(l => {
    const time = new Date(l.createdAt).toLocaleString();
    return `[${time}] Agent: ${l.agentId}\nPrompt: ${l.prompt}\nResponse: ${l.response}\n`;
  }).join("\n-----------------\n");
}

export function generatePdfExport(logs: any[]): Promise<Buffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const doc = new PDFDocument();
    const chunks: any[] = [];
    doc.on("data", chunk => chunks.push(chunk));
    doc.on("end", () => resolve(Buffer.concat(chunks)));

    logs.forEach(l => {
      doc.fontSize(10).text(`Time: ${new Date(l.createdAt).toLocaleString()}`);
      doc.fontSize(12).text(`Agent: ${l.agentId}`, { underline: true });
      doc.moveDown(0.5);
      doc.text(`Prompt:\n${l.prompt}`);
      doc.moveDown(0.5);
      doc.text(`Response:\n${l.response}`);
      doc.addPage();
    });
    doc.end();
  });
}
4. Data Fetch Hook

/hooks/useVaultData.ts

import useSWR from "swr";

type Query = { search?: string; agentId?: string; page?: number; perPage?: number };

export function useVaultData(query: Query) {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams({
    ...(query.search ? { search: query.search } : {}),
    ...(query.agentId ? { agentId: query.agentId } : {}),
    page: String(query.page ?? 1),
    perPage: String(query.perPage ?? 20)
  }).toString();

  const { data, error } = useSWR(`/api/promptVault?${qs}`, fetcher);
  return {
    logs: data?.logs || [],
    total: data?.total || 0,
    isLoading: !data && !error,
    error
  };
}

const fetcher = (url: string) => fetch(url).then(res => res.json());
5. Frontend Component

/components/PromptVault.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useVaultData } from "@/hooks/useVaultData";

export default function PromptVault() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const { logs, total, isLoading } = useVaultData({ search, page:1 });

  const toggle = (id: string) => {
    setSelected(sel => sel.includes(id) ? sel.filter(s => s!==id) : [...sel, id]);
  };

  const exportLogs = (format: "txt" | "pdf") => {
    fetch("/api/promptVault", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type":"application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ format, selectedIds: selected })
    }).then(res => res.blob())
      .then(blob => {
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = format === "txt" ? "prompts.txt" : "prompts.pdf";
        a.click();
      });
  };

  if (isLoading) return <div>Loading vault…</div>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          value={search}
          onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search prompts..."
          className="flex-1 p-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white"
        />
        <button onClick={() => exportLogs("txt")} disabled={!selected.length} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#D1BFA7] text-black rounded">
          Export TXT
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => exportLogs("pdf")} disabled={!selected.length} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#D1BFA7] text-black rounded">
          Export PDF
        </button>
      </div>

      <ul className="bg-black bg-opacity-50 p-4 rounded max-h-96 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 text-white">
        {logs.map(l => (
          <li key={l.id} className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={selected.includes(l.id)} onChange={()=>toggle(l.id)} />
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-300">{new Date(l.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
              <p><strong>{l.agentId}</strong></p>
              <p className="italic">Prompt: {l.prompt}</p>
              <p>Response: {l.response}</p>
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Total logs: {total}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

STEP 13: Webhook Output (Slack / Notion / Zapier / ClickUp)
“Post-agent results anywhere you need—team chat, CRM, docs, or task boards.”

1. File Structure

/pages/api/webhook
  dispatch.ts                # Next.js API endpoint
2. Environment Variables

Add to your .env:

SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL=…
ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL=…
NOTION_API_KEY=…
NOTION_DB_ID=…
CLICKUP_TOKEN=…
CLICKUP_LIST_ID=…
3. Webhook Dispatcher

/lib/webhookDispatcher.ts

import fetch from "node-fetch";

const slackUrl    = process.env.SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL!;
const zapierUrl   = process.env.ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL!;
const notionKey   = process.env.NOTION_API_KEY!;
const notionDbId  = process.env.NOTION_DB_ID!;
const clickupToken= process.env.CLICKUP_TOKEN!;
const clickupList = process.env.CLICKUP_LIST_ID!;

export type Destination = "slack" | "zapier" | "notion" | "clickup";

export async function dispatchWebhook(agentOutput: {
  agentId: string;
  response: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}, destination: Destination) {
  switch (destination) {
    case "slack":
      await fetch(slackUrl, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          text: `*${agentOutput.agentId}* responded:\n${agentOutput.response}`
        })
      });
      break;

    case "zapier":
      await fetch(zapierUrl, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(agentOutput)
      });
      break;

    case "notion":
      await fetch("https://api.notion.com/v1/pages", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Bearer ${notionKey}`,
          "Notion-Version": "2022-06-28",
          "Content-Type": "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          parent: { database_id: notionDbId },
          properties: {
            Agent:   { title:      [{ text: { content: agentOutput.agentId } }] },
            Response:{ rich_text:  [{ text: { content: agentOutput.response } }] }
          }
        })
      });
      break;

    case "clickup":
      await fetch(`https://api.clickup.com/api/v2/list/${clickupList}/task`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": clickupToken,
          "Content-Type":  "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name:    `Agent ${agentOutput.agentId} Output`,
          content: agentOutput.response,
          status:  "to do"
        })
      });
      break;
  }
}
4. API Route

/pages/api/webhook/dispatch.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from "next";
import { dispatchWebhook, Destination } from "@/lib/webhookDispatcher";

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const { agentOutput, destination } = req.body as {
    agentOutput: { agentId: string; response: string; [k: string]: any };
    destination: Destination;
  };

  if (!agentOutput || !destination) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing agentOutput or destination" });
  }
  try {
    await dispatchWebhook(agentOutput, destination);
    res.status(200).json({ success: true });
  } catch (err: any) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
  }
}
5. Usage in Orchestrator

After an agent finishes processing, pipe its output into your dispatcher:

import { dispatchWebhook } from "@/lib/webhookDispatcher";

const result = await runAgent("cashflow-agent", context);
// …handle result…

STEP 9: SŌL Orchestrator (Multi-Agent Chaining)

// File: /pages/api/orchestrator.ts

import { getAgentForTask, preparePayload, delegateToAgent, aggregateResults } from '@/lib/orchestratorUtils';

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  try {
    const { taskType, clientData } = req.body;
    const agentId = getAgentForTask(taskType);
    const payload = preparePayload(clientData);
    const output  = await delegateToAgent(agentId, payload);
    const result  = aggregateResults([output]);
    res.status(200).json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
  }
}
// File: **/lib/orchestratorUtils.ts**
export function getAgentForTask(taskType: string): string {
  const map = {
    onboarding:  'intake-agent',
    followUp:    'sales-agent-cashflow',
    recruitFlow: 'hr-agent',
    // …etc
  };
  return map[taskType] || 'concierge-agent';
}

export function preparePayload(clientData: any): any {
  // massage incoming data into agent prompt schema
  return { context: clientData };
}

export async function delegateToAgent(agentId: string, taskPayload: any): Promise<any> {
  // call internal Agent API or microservice
  const resp = await fetch(`${process.env.AGENT_API_URL}/run/${agentId}`, {
    method:  'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body:    JSON.stringify(taskPayload),
  });
  return resp.json();
}

export function aggregateResults(results: any[]): any {
  // merge, dedupe, format final output
  return results.flat();
}
STEP 10: Harmony Dashboard (Client View)

// File: /pages/dashboard.tsx

import useSWR from 'swr';
import AgentStatusCard   from '@/components/AgentStatusCard';
import CreditsCounter    from '@/components/CreditsCounter';
import PromptConsole     from '@/components/PromptConsole';
import OutputVaultPreview from '@/components/OutputVaultPreview';

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { data: stats, error } = useSWR('/api/dashboardStats', url => fetch(url).then(r => r.json()));

  if (error) return <div>Failed to load dashboard.</div>;
  if (!stats)  return <div>Loading…</div>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 p-6">
      <AgentStatusCard data={stats.currentAgent} />
      <CreditsCounter  balance={stats.tokenBalance} />
      <PromptConsole   recentPrompts={stats.recentPrompts} />
      <OutputVaultPreview items={stats.recentOutputs} />
    </div>
  );
}

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/prompts')
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(setLogs);
  }, []);

  function exportLogs() {
    // convert logs -> .txt or .pdf and trigger download
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <h2>Prompt History</h2>
      <ul>
        {logs.map(log => (
          <li key={log.id}>
            <strong>{new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString()}:</strong> {log.prompt}
          </li>
        ))}

    </div>

STEP 13: Webhook Output (Slack / Notion / Zapier)

// File: /lib/webhookDispatcher.ts

export async function dispatchWebhook(agentOutput: any, destination: string) {
  switch (destination) {
    case 'Slack':
      await postToSlack(agentOutput);
      break;
    case 'Zapier':
      await triggerZap(agentOutput);
      break;
    case 'Notion':
      await appendToNotionDB(agentOutput);
      break;
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unsupported destination: ${destination}`);
  }
}

async function postToSlack(payload: any) {
  await fetch(process.env.SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ text: JSON.stringify(payload) }),
  });
}
async function triggerZap(payload: any) { /* … */ }
async function appendToNotionDB(payload: any) { /* … */ }
Call dispatchWebhook(output, dest) wherever you finalize each agent’s response.

STEP 16: Billing + Stripe Integration

// File: /pages/billing.tsx

import { loadStripe } from '@stripe/stripe-js';
import useSWR from 'swr';

export default function BillingPage() {
  const stripePromise = loadStripe(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_KEY);
  const { data: products } = useSWR('/api/creditPacks');

  async function handlePurchase(priceId: string) {
    const stripe = await stripePromise;
    const { sessionId } = await fetch('/api/createCheckoutSession', {
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ priceId }),
    }).then(r=>r.json());
    await stripe.redirectToCheckout({ sessionId });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 p-6">
      <h2>Buy Credit Packs</h2>
      {products?.map(p => (
        <div key={p.id} className="flex justify-between">
          <span>{p.name} — ${p.price}</span>
          <button onClick={()=>handlePurchase(p.priceId)}>Buy</button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

Below is an expanded, granular breakdown of Steps 9–19, with every sub-task, file path, schema, and acceptance criterion spelled out. You can copy each section into your sprint board or ticketing system and hand it directly to your developers.

🌀 STEP 9 – SŌL Orchestrator (Multi-Agent Chaining)

Goal: Central router that takes a high-level task and fans it out to one or more agents, then merges their outputs.

9.1 API Endpoint
File: pages/api/orchestrator.ts
Method: POST /api/orchestrator
Request Body:
interface OrchestratorRequest {
  taskType: string;        // e.g. "followUp", "onboarding"
  clientData: Record<string, any>;
}
Response:
type OrchestratorResponse = any[];  // array of agent outputs
Tasks:
Validate taskType against a white-list (enum).
Return HTTP 400 if missing/invalid.
9.2 Utility Module
File: lib/orchestratorUtils.ts
Exports:
getAgentForTask(taskType: string): string
preparePayload(clientData: any): { context: any }
delegateToAgent(agentId: string, payload: any): Promise<any>
aggregateResults(results: any[]): any[]
Details:
getAgentForTask: Map each taskType to an agent ID. Provide defaults.
preparePayload: Wraps clientData into the shape your Agent API expects ({ context, metadata }).
delegateToAgent: fetch → process.env.AGENT_API_URL → JSON.
aggregateResults: Flatten, dedupe (by id), preserve order.
9.3 Logging & Metrics
File: lib/logger.ts (using Winston or Pino)
Instrument every step:
logger.info('Orchestrator received task', { taskType, clientId });
logger.error('DelegateToAgent failed', { agentId, error });
Expose Prometheus counters:
orchestrator_requests_total
orchestrator_errors_total
9.4 Error Handling
Wrap every internal call in try/catch.
Return HTTP 500 + { error: string } on failures.
Include correlation ID in logs and response headers.
9.5 Tests
File: __tests__/orchestrator.test.ts
Scope:
Valid taskType → calls correct agent.
Invalid taskType → 400.
Simulate Agent API error → 500.
aggregateResults merges correctly.
🎛️ STEP 10 – Harmony Dashboard (Client View)

Goal: Build the main user dashboard showing current agent, token balance, recent prompts & outputs.

10.1 API: /api/dashboardStats
File: pages/api/dashboardStats.ts
Returns:
interface DashboardStats {
  currentAgent: { id: string; name: string; portraitUrl: string; tokensUsed: number; tasksCompleted: number; };
  tokenBalance: number;
  recentPrompts: { id: string; prompt: string; timestamp: string; }[];
  recentOutputs: { id: string; summary: string; timestamp: string; }[];
}
10.2 React Page
File: pages/dashboard.tsx
Use useSWR<DashboardStats>('/api/dashboardStats')
Handle loading / error states.
10.3 Components
components/AgentStatusCard.tsx
Props: currentAgent
Shows: Agent portrait, name, small halo indicating “active.”
components/CreditsCounter.tsx
Props: balance: number
Displays large number + “Recharge” button.
components/PromptConsole.tsx
Props: recentPrompts
Table/list with timestamp + prompt text.
components/OutputVaultPreview.tsx
Props: items
Similar list of outputs, clickable to full vault.
Styling: Use Tailwind CSS or existing design system tokens. Ensure mobile responsiveness.
10.4 Acceptance Criteria
Dashboard shows non-zero mock data.
Clicking “Recharge” opens billing modal.

9.1 API & Backend
9.1.1 Endpoint: POST /api/orchestrator

// pages/api/orchestrator.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getAgentForTask, preparePayload, delegateToAgent, aggregateResults } from '@/lib/orchestratorUtils';
import logger from '@/lib/logger';

interface OrchestratorRequest {
  taskType: 'multiAgentFlow' | 'followUp' | /*…*/;
  clientData: {
    userId: string;
    flowConfig?: any;       // from agent-builder
    context?: Record<string,any>; // e.g. CRM data
    webhookDestination?: string;
  };
}

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).end();
  const { taskType, clientData }: OrchestratorRequest = req.body;

  if (!taskType || !clientData?.userId) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing taskType or userId' });
  }

  logger.info('Orchestrator received task', { taskType, userId: clientData.userId });

  try {
    const agentId = getAgentForTask(taskType);
    const payload = preparePayload(clientData);
    const output = await delegateToAgent(agentId, payload);
    const results = aggregateResults(Array.isArray(output) ? output : [output]);

    // dispatch to webhooks in background
    if (clientData.webhookDestination) {
      import('@/lib/webhookDispatcher').then(m => m.dispatchWebhook(results, clientData.webhookDestination));
    }

    return res.status(200).json(results);
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.error('Orchestrator error', { err, taskType });
    return res.status(500).json({ error: err.message || 'Orchestrator failure' });
  }
};

export default handler;
9.1.2 Utility Module

// lib/orchestratorUtils.ts
import fetch from 'node-fetch';
import logger from './logger';

export function getAgentForTask(taskType: string): string {
  const map: Record<string,string> = {
    multiAgentFlow: process.env.AGENT_MULTIFLOW_ID!,
    followUp:         process.env.AGENT_CASH_ID!,
    // …
  };
  return map[taskType] || map['followUp'];
}

export function preparePayload(clientData: any) {
  return {
    context: clientData.context,
    flowConfig: clientData.flowConfig,
    metadata: {
      userId: clientData.userId,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    }
  };
}

export async function delegateToAgent(agentId: string, payload: any) {
  const url = `${process.env.AGENT_API_URL}/agents/${agentId}/run`;
  const resp = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.AGENT_API_KEY}` },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });
  if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`Agent ${agentId} failed: ${resp.statusText}`);
  return await resp.json();
}

export function aggregateResults(results: any[]) {
  const seen = new Set();
  return results
    .flat()
    .filter(r => {
      if (seen.has(r.id)) return false;
      seen.add(r.id);
      return true;
    });
}
9.2 Metadata Model
Every orchestrator output item should include:

interface OrchestratorOutput {
  id: string;                   // unique per task
  agentId: string;
  content: string;              // agent response
  confidenceScore?: number;     // 0–1
  executionTimeMs: number;
  timestamp: string;            // ISO
}
Store these in your vector DB or logs for later retrieval.

async function runFlow() {
  setLoading(true);
  const resp = await fetch('/api/orchestrator', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({
      taskType: 'multiAgentFlow',
      clientData: {
        userId: user.id,
        flowConfig: builderConfig,
        context: { /* any CRM data */ },
        webhookDestination: 'Slack'
      }
    })
  });
  const results = await resp.json();
  setLoading(false);
  showFlowResults(results);  // modal or inline panel
}

10.3.1 Dashboard Page

// pages/dashboard.tsx
import useSWR from 'swr';
import AgentStatusCard from '@/components/AgentStatusCard';
import CreditsCounter  from '@/components/CreditsCounter';
import PromptConsole   from '@/components/PromptConsole';
import OutputVault     from '@/components/OutputVault';

const fetcher = (url: string) => fetch(url).then(r => r.json());

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { data: summary } = useSWR<DashboardSummary>('/api/dashboard/summary', fetcher);
  const { data: agents  } = useSWR<AgentStatus[]>('/api/dashboard/agents', fetcher);
  const { data: tasks   } = useSWR<RecentTask[]>('/api/dashboard/tasks?limit=10', fetcher);

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 p-6">
      {/* Summary Cards */}
      <CreditsCounter
        tokens={summary?.harmonyTokensRemaining}
        credits={summary?.creditsBalance}
        earnings={summary?.totalEarningsUSD}
        nfts={summary?.nftCount}
      />

      {/* Agent Status */}
      <div className="col-span-2 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {agents?.map(a => (
          <AgentStatusCard key={a.agentId} {...a}/>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Prompt Console */}
      <div className="col-span-1 lg:col-span-2">
        <PromptConsole defaultAgent={agents?.[0]?.agentId}/>
      </div>

      {/* Output Vault */}
      <div className="col-span-1 lg:col-span-3">
        <OutputVault />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

10.3.4 PromptConsole

// components/PromptConsole.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
export default function PromptConsole({ defaultAgent }: { defaultAgent?: string }) {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [output, setOutput] = useState<string|null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function submit() {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/dashboard/console', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt, agentId:defaultAgent })
    });
    const data: ConsoleResponse = await res.json();
    setOutput(data.output);
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded">
      <textarea value={prompt} onChange={e => setPrompt(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={submit} disabled={loading}>Run</button>
      {output && <pre className="mt-2 bg-white p-2">{output}</pre>}
    </div>
  );
}
10.3.5 OutputVault

// components/OutputVault.tsx
import useSWR from 'swr';
export default function OutputVault() {
  const { data: items } = useSWR<VaultItem[]>('/api/dashboard/outputs?limit=20&page=1', fetcher);
  return (
    <table className="w-full text-left">
      <thead>
        <tr><th>Prompt</th><th>Response</th><th>Agent</th><th>When</th></tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {items?.map(i => (
          <tr key={i.id}>
            <td>{i.prompt.slice(0,30)}…</td>
            <td>{i.response.slice(0,30)}…</td>
            <td>{i.agentName}</td>
            <td>{new Date(i.timestamp).toLocaleString()}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}

10.c.1 /api/dashboard/agents

Modify to include timeSavedSeconds per agent:

// pages/api/dashboard/agents.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { agentTimeBenchmarks } from '@/lib/agentTimeBenchmarks';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const userId = req.session.user.id;
  // fetch agent stats
  const agents = await db.agentStatus.findMany({ where:{ userId } });
  const enriched = agents.map(a => {
    const secondsPerTask = agentTimeBenchmarks[a.agentId] || 0;
    const timeSaved = a.completedTasks * secondsPerTask;
    return { ...a, timeSavedSeconds: timeSaved };
  });
  return res.json(enriched);
};

export default handler;
10.c.2 /api/dashboard/summary

Add total time saved:

// pages/api/dashboard/summary.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { agentTimeBenchmarks } from '@/lib/agentTimeBenchmarks';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const userId = req.session.user.id;
  const [wallet, stats] = await Promise.all([
    db.wallet.findUnique({ where:{ userId } }),
    db.agentStatus.findMany({ where:{ userId } })
  ]);
  const totalTimeSavedSeconds = stats.reduce((sum,a) => {
    const per = agentTimeBenchmarks[a.agentId]||0;
    return sum + (a.completedTasks * per);
  }, 0);
  return res.json({
    harmonyTokensRemaining: wallet?.tokens||0,
    creditsBalance: wallet?.credits||0,
    totalEarningsUSD: wallet?.earnings||0,
    nftCount: wallet?.nfts?.length||0,
    totalTimeSavedSeconds
  });
};

export default handler;

10.d.3 formatDuration Helper

// lib/formatDuration.ts
export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = seconds % 60;
  const parts = [];
  if (hrs) parts.push(`${hrs}h`);
  if (mins) parts.push(`${mins}m`);
  if (!parts.length) parts.push(`${secs}s`);
  return parts.join(' ');
}

11.1 Auth & Route Protection
Library: NextAuth.js with JWT
Helper:
// lib/adminAuth.ts
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
export async function requireAdmin(req, res) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session?.user?.isAdmin) {
    res.status(302).setHeader('Location','/signin').end();
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
Usage: call in every getServerSideProps or API handler.

4.1 TimeSavingCalculator Component
// components/TimeSavingCalculator.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { formatDuration } from '@/lib/formatDuration';

export default function TimeSavingCalculator({
  benchmarkSeconds,
  defaultTasks
}: {
  benchmarkSeconds: number;
  defaultTasks: number;
}) {
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState(defaultTasks);
  const totalSeconds = tasks * benchmarkSeconds;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded shadow-sm">
      <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Time-Savings Calculator</h3>
      <label className="block mb-1 text-sm">Tasks/week:</label>
      <input
        type="number"
        value={tasks}
        min={0}
        onChange={e => setTasks(+e.target.value)}
        className="border px-2 py-1 w-24"
      />
      <p className="mt-2 text-lg">
        You’ll save <strong>{formatDuration(totalSeconds)}</strong> per week
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

// lib/formatDuration.ts
export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const parts = [];
  if (hrs) parts.push(`${hrs}h`);
  if (mins) parts.push(`${mins}m`);
  if (!parts.length) parts.push('0m');
  return parts.join(' ');
}

Step 12 – Prompt History & Output Vault

12.0 Site-Map Changes
Under the authenticated /dashboard area we introduce:

12.1 Database Schema
-- Vault of user prompts & AI responses
CREATE TABLE prompt_logs (
  id              UUID     PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id         UUID     NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  agent_id        TEXT     NOT NULL,
  agent_name      TEXT     NOT NULL,
  prompt_text     TEXT     NOT NULL,
  response_text   TEXT     NOT NULL,
  tokens_used     INTEGER  NOT NULL,
  time_saved_sec  INTEGER  DEFAULT 0,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

-- Full‐text index for search
CREATE INDEX idx_prompt_logs_search
  ON prompt_logs
  USING GIN (
    to_tsvector('english', prompt_text || ' ' || response_text)
  );
12.2 API Endpoints
12.2.1 List & Filter Vault Items

GET /api/dashboard/outputs?limit=20&page=1&agentId=&search=&from=&to=
// pages/api/dashboard/outputs.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { user } = req.session;
  if (!user) return res.status(401).end();

  const {
    page = '1',
    limit = '20',
    agentId,
    search,
    from,
    to
  } = req.query;

  const where: any = { user_id: user.id };
  if (agentId) where.agent_id = agentId;
  if (from || to) {
    where.created_at = {};
    if (from) where.created_at.gte = new Date(from as string);
    if (to)   where.created_at.lte = new Date(to as string);
  }

  // Basic Prisma + full-text for search
  const items = await db.prompt_logs.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: { created_at: 'desc' },
    skip: (parseInt(page as string)-1)*parseInt(limit as string),
    take: parseInt(limit as string),
    ...(search ? {
      // Raw query for full-text
      where: {
        AND: [
          where,
          { 
            prompt_text: {
              contains: search as string,
              mode: 'insensitive'
            }
          }
        ]
      }
    } : {})
  });

  const total = await db.prompt_logs.count({ where });
  res.json({ items, total });
};

export default handler;
12.2.2 Single Vault Item & Export

GET /api/dashboard/outputs/[id]
// pages/api/dashboard/outputs/[id].ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.query;
  const { user } = req.session;
  if (!user) return res.status(401).end();

  const item = await db.prompt_logs.findUnique({
    where: { id: id as string }
  });
  if (!item || item.user_id !== user.id) return res.status(404).end();
  res.json(item);
};

export default handler;
GET /api/dashboard/outputs/[id]/export?format=pdf|txt
// pages/api/dashboard/outputs/[id]/export.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import PDFDocument from 'pdfkit'; // or similar
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.query;
  const { user } = req.session;
  const item = await db.prompt_logs.findUnique({ where:{ id:id as string } });
  if (!item || item.user_id !== user.id) return res.status(404).end();

  const format = (req.query.format as string) || 'txt';
  if (format === 'txt') {
    res.setHeader('Content-Type','text/plain');
    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition',`attachment; filename="${id}.txt"`);
    return res.send(`Prompt:\n${item.prompt_text}\n\nResponse:\n${item.response_text}`);
  } else {
    // PDF
    res.setHeader('Content-Type','application/pdf');
    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition',`attachment; filename="${id}.pdf"`);
    const doc = new PDFDocument();
    doc.pipe(res);
    doc.fontSize(16).text(`Agent: ${item.agent_name}`, { underline:true });
    doc.moveDown();
    doc.fontSize(12).text('Prompt:').moveDown(0.5).text(item.prompt_text);
    doc.moveDown().fontSize(12).text('Response:').moveDown(0.5).text(item.response_text);
    doc.end();
  }
};

export default handler;
12.3 React Pages & Components
12.3.1 Vault List Page

// pages/dashboard/outputs.tsx
import useSWR from 'swr';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function OutputVaultPage() {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState({ page:1, agentId:'', search:'', from:'', to:'' });
  const { data, error } = useSWR(() => `/api/dashboard/outputs?${new URLSearchParams(filters as any)}`, fetcher);

  if (error) return <p>Error loading vault</p>;
  if (!data)  return <p>Loading…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl mb-4">Prompt & Response Vault</h1>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
        <input placeholder="Search…" onChange={e=>setFilters({...filters,search:e.target.value, page:1})}/>
        <select onChange={e=>setFilters({...filters,agentId:e.target.value,page:1})}>
          <option value="">All Agents</option>
          {/* map over registry */}
        </select>
        <input type="date" onChange={e=>setFilters({...filters,from:e.target.value,page:1})}/>
        <input type="date" onChange={e=>setFilters({...filters,to:e.target.value,page:1})}/>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>When</th><th>Agent</th><th>Prompt</th><th>Response</th><th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {data.items.map(item => (
            <tr key={item.id} className="border-t">
              <td>{new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td>{item.agent_name}</td>
              <td>{item.prompt_text.slice(0,50)}…</td>
              <td>{item.response_text.slice(0,50)}…</td>
              <td>
                <Link href={`/dashboard/outputs/${item.id}`}>View</Link> |{' '}
                <a href={`/api/dashboard/outputs/${item.id}/export?format=txt`}>TXT</a> |{' '}
                <a href={`/api/dashboard/outputs/${item.id}/export?format=pdf`}>PDF</a>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      {/* Pagination */}
      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-between">
        <button disabled={filters.page<=1} onClick={()=>setFilters({...filters,page:filters.page-1})}>Prev</button>
        <span>Page {filters.page}</span>
        <button
          disabled={data.items.length < 20}
          onClick={()=>setFilters({...filters,page:filters.page+1})}
        >Next</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
12.3.2 Vault Detail Page

// pages/dashboard/outputs/[id].tsx
import { GetServerSideProps } from 'next';
import fetch from 'node-fetch';

export const getServerSideProps: GetServerSideProps = async ({ req, params }) => {
  const res = await fetch(`${process.env.APP_URL}/api/dashboard/outputs/${params!.id}`, {
    headers: { cookie: req.headers.cookie! }
  });
  if (res.status !== 200) return { notFound:true };
  const item = await res.json();
  return { props: { item } };
};

export default function OutputDetail({ item }) {
  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Vault Entry</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-600">{new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()}</p>

      <section className="mt-4">
        <h2 className="font-semibold">Prompt</h2>
        <pre className="bg-gray-100 p-3 rounded">{item.prompt_text}</pre>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-4">
        <h2 className="font-semibold">Response</h2>
        <pre className="bg-gray-100 p-3 rounded">{item.response_text}</pre>
      </section>

      <div className="mt-6 flex gap-4">
        <a
          href={`/api/dashboard/outputs/${item.id}/export?format=txt`}
          className="underline"
        >Download TXT</a>
        <a
          href={`/api/dashboard/outputs/${item.id}/export?format=pdf`}
          className="underline"
        >Download PDF</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Body

  agentId: string,
