}
This will look up all integration_configs for that (userId, agentId), then call each provider’s API/webhook.
13.3 Server-Side Logic
13.3.1 Integration Dispatcher

// lib/integrations/dispatcher.ts
import fetch from 'node-fetch';

export async function dispatchIntegrations(userId: string, agentId: string, eventType: string, payload: any) {
  // 1. Load configs
  const configs = await db.integration_configs.findMany({
    where: { user_id: userId, agent_id: agentId }
  });

  // 2. For each config, route to specific handler
  await Promise.all(configs.map(cfg => {
    switch (cfg.provider) {
      case 'slack':
        return sendToSlack(cfg.config_json, payload);
      case 'zapier':
        return triggerZapier(cfg.config_json, payload);
      case 'notion':
        return sendToNotion(cfg.config_json, payload);
      case 'hubspot':
        return sendToHubSpot(cfg.config_json, payload);
      case 'gohighlevel':
        return sendToGoHighLevel(cfg.config_json, payload);
      default:
        return Promise.resolve();
    }
  }));
}
13.3.2 Provider Helpers

// lib/integrations/providers.ts

// Slack
export async function sendToSlack(cfg: any, payload: any) {
  return fetch(cfg.webhookUrl, {
    method:'POST',
    headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ text: cfg.messageTemplate.replace('{{data}}', JSON.stringify(payload)) })
  });
}

// Zapier
export async function triggerZapier(cfg: any, payload: any) {
  return fetch(cfg.webhookUrl, {
    method:'POST',
    headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ event: cfg.eventName, data: payload })
  });
}

// Notion (append to database)
export async function sendToNotion(cfg: any, payload: any) {
  return fetch(`https://api.notion.com/v1/pages`, {
    method:'POST',
    headers:{
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${cfg.apiKey}`,
      'Notion-Version': cfg.version,
      'Content-Type':'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      parent: { database_id: cfg.databaseId },
      properties: {
        Title: { title: [{ text: { content: payload.title }}] },
        Data:  { rich_text: [{ text: { content: JSON.stringify(payload) }}] }
      }
    })
  });
}

// HubSpot (create engagement)
export async function sendToHubSpot(cfg: any, payload: any) {
  return fetch(`https://api.hubapi.com/engagements/v1/engagements`, {
    method:'POST',
    headers:{
      'Content-Type':'application/json',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${cfg.accessToken}`
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      engagement: { active: true, type: payload.eventType },
      associations: { contactIds: [payload.contactId] },
      metadata: { body: JSON.stringify(payload) }
    })
  });
}

// GoHighLevel (via API)
export async function sendToGoHighLevel(cfg: any, payload: any) {
  return fetch(`https://rest.gohighlevel.com/v1/contacts/`, {
    method:'POST',
    headers:{
      'Content-Type':'application/json',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${cfg.apiKey}`
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      phone: payload.phone,
      email: payload.email,
      customField: payload
    })
  });
}
13.3.3 Dispatch Endpoint

// pages/api/integrations/dispatch.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { dispatchIntegrations } from '@/lib/integrations/dispatcher';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { userId, agentId, eventType, payload } = req.body;
  try {
    await dispatchIntegrations(userId, agentId, eventType, payload);
    res.status(200).json({ success:true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    res.status(500).json({ success:false, error: err.message });
  }
};

export default handler;

14.1 Database Schema

-- Templates of available agent blueprints
CREATE TABLE agent_templates (
  id                TEXT    PRIMARY KEY,            -- e.g. 'cashflow-agent'
  name              TEXT    NOT NULL,
  default_bio       TEXT,
  default_avatar    TEXT,
  capabilities       TEXT[] NOT NULL
);

-- User‐created agents
CREATE TABLE agent_instances (
  id                UUID    PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id       TEXT    NOT NULL REFERENCES agent_templates(id),
  owner_id          UUID    NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  name              TEXT    NOT NULL,
  description       TEXT,
  avatar_url        TEXT,
  metadata_url      TEXT,                           -- IPFS JSON URL
  price_usd         NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
  price_tokens      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  max_supply        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  minted_count      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  royalty_pct       INTEGER NOT NULL,               -- 0–100%
  is_public         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

-- Track secondary sales & MRR
CREATE TABLE agent_sales (
  id                UUID    PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  instance_id       UUID    NOT NULL REFERENCES agent_instances(id),
  buyer_id          UUID    REFERENCES users(id),
  amount_usd        NUMERIC(10,2),
  amount_tokens     INTEGER,
  is_primary        BOOLEAN,                        -- first mint vs resell
  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

14.3 Smart Contract (Solidity)

// contracts/AgentNFT.sol
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.0;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC721/extensions/ERC721URIStorage.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/common/ERC2981.sol";

contract AgentNFT is ERC721URIStorage, ERC2981 {
    address public owner;
    uint256 public nextTokenId;
    mapping(uint256 => uint256) public maxSupply;
    mapping(uint256 => uint256) public mintedCount;

    constructor(string memory name, string memory symbol) ERC721(name, symbol) {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    function setRoyalty(uint256 tokenId, address recipient, uint96 pct) external {
        require(msg.sender == owner, "only owner");
        _setTokenRoyalty(tokenId, recipient, pct);
    }

    function mint(uint256 instanceId, address to, string memory tokenURI) external payable {
        require(mintedCount[instanceId] < maxSupply[instanceId], "sold out");
        nextTokenId++;
        mintedCount[instanceId]++;
        _safeMint(to, nextTokenId);
        _setTokenURI(nextTokenId, tokenURI);
        // royalty already set
    }

    // Admin: set supply before sales
    function setMaxSupply(uint256 instanceId, uint256 supply) external {
        require(msg.sender == owner, "only owner");
        maxSupply[instanceId] = supply;
    }

    // Overrides for ERC2981
    function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) public view override(ERC721, ERC2981) returns(bool) {
        return super.supportsInterface(interfaceId);
    }
}
14.4 React Pages & Components

14.4.1 Agent Creation Wizard
// pages/agents/create.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import StepOne from '@/components/agents/create/StepOne';
import StepTwo from '@/components/agents/create/StepTwo';
import StepThree from '@/components/agents/create/StepThree';
import StepFour from '@/components/agents/create/StepFour';
import StepFive from '@/components/agents/create/StepFive';

export default function AgentCreate() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>({});

  const next = (vals:any) => { setData({...data,...vals}); setStep(step+1); };
  const prev = () => setStep(step-1);

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      {step === 1 && <StepOne onNext={next} registry={agent_templates}/>}
      {step === 2 && <StepTwo onNext={next} onPrev={prev} />}
      {step === 3 && <StepThree onNext={next} onPrev={prev} />}
      {step === 4 && <StepFour onNext={next} onPrev={prev} />}
      {step === 5 && <StepFive onFinish={()=>{
        fetch('/api/agents/create',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)});
      }} onPrev={prev} summary={data}/>}
    </div>
  );
}
Each StepX collects:

Basic Info: name, description, avatarUrl
Capabilities: multi-select from agent_templates[].capabilities
Pricing & Supply: priceUsd, priceTokens, maxSupply, royaltyPct
Training: initial prompt / upload business docs
Review & Mint: summary + confirm → calls creation API & smart-contract mint

14.5 IPFS Metadata & Auto-Update

// lib/ipfs.ts
import { Web3Storage } from 'web3.storage';
const client = new Web3Storage({ token: process.env.WEB3_STORAGE_TOKEN! });

export async function uploadMetadata(meta: any) {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(meta)], {type:'application/json'});
  const cid = await client.put([blob]);
  return `ipfs://${cid}/metadata.json`;
}
On POST /api/agents/create → generate JSON:
{
  "name": "...", "description":"...", "image":"ipfs://…",
  "attributes":[{"trait_type":"Price USD","value":"50"},{"trait_type":"Royalty %","value":"10"}]
}
Upload via uploadMetadata(), write metadata_url in DB, call setTokenURI on contract.
Auto-Update Flow
PUT /api/agents/:id/update → recalc metadata (e.g. new version), re-upload, call setTokenURI(tokenId, newUrl) via Web3 call.
Scheduled Cron (Vercel Cron / GitHub Actions) daily:
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
const instances = await db.agent_instances.findMany({ where:{ auto_update:true } });
for (let ai of instances) { /* re-upload metadata with updated stats */ }
14.6 Royalty & MRR Logic

Primary sale: purchase → record in agent_sales, revenue to owner_id minus platform fee
Secondary sale: contract’s ERC-2981 ensures royalty sent to owner wallet
Subscription: if using credits or tokens → record monthly in agent_sales with is_primary=false

15.2 Smart Contracts

15.2.1 HarmonyToken.sol (ERC-20)
// contracts/HarmonyToken.sol
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.0;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol";

contract HarmonyToken is ERC20 {
    address public admin;

    constructor() ERC20("Harmony Token", "HMX") {
        admin = msg.sender;
        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000_000 * 10**decimals()); // initial supply
    }

    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {
        require(msg.sender == admin, "only admin");
        _mint(to, amount);
    }
}
15.2.2 HarmonyStaking.sol
// contracts/HarmonyStaking.sol
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.0;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";

contract HarmonyStaking {
    IERC20 public token;
    address public admin;
    uint256 public minStakeDays;      // e.g. 7 days

    struct Stake {
        uint256 amount;
        uint256 stakedAt;
        uint256 unlockAt;
    }

    mapping(address => Stake[]) public stakes;

    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount, uint256 unlockAt);
    event Unstaked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);

    constructor(address tokenAddr, uint256 _minStakeDays) {
        token = IERC20(tokenAddr);
        admin = msg.sender;
        minStakeDays = _minStakeDays;
    }

    function stake(uint256 amount) external {
        require(amount > 0, "amount>0");
        token.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
        uint256 unlockAt = block.timestamp + (minStakeDays * 1 days);
        stakes[msg.sender].push(Stake(amount, block.timestamp, unlockAt));
        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount, unlockAt);
    }

    function unstake(uint256 index) external {
        Stake memory s = stakes[msg.sender][index];
        require(block.timestamp >= s.unlockAt, "locked");
        _removeStake(msg.sender, index);
        token.transfer(msg.sender, s.amount);
        emit Unstaked(msg.sender, s.amount);
    }

    function _removeStake(address user, uint256 i) internal {
        stakes[user][i] = stakes[user][stakes[user].length - 1];
        stakes[user].pop();
    }

    // Admin: adjust min stake
    function setMinStakeDays(uint256 daysCount) external {
        require(msg.sender == admin);
        minStakeDays = daysCount;
    }
}
15.3 Off-Chain Reward Distribution

A cron job (via Vercel Cron or your scheduler) runs weekly:

// scripts/distributeRewards.ts
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { mint } from '@/lib/web3';  // wrapper for HarmonyToken.mint()

async function distribute() {
  const periodStart = /* last Monday midnight */;
  const periodEnd   = /* this Monday midnight */;

  // 1. Sum all stakes × duration to calculate share weight
  const stakes = await db.token_stakes.findMany({ where:{ unlock_at: { gt: periodEnd } } });
  const totalWeight = stakes.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.amount * (periodEnd.getTime() - s.stakedAt.getTime()), 0);

  // 2. For each user, calculate reward = UBI_RATE × (userWeight/totalWeight)
  const ubiRate = await db.staking_params.findUnique({ where:{ id:'ubi_rate' }});
  for (let s of stakes) {
    const userWeight = s.amount * (periodEnd.getTime() - s.stakedAt.getTime());
    const reward = (ubiRate.value * userWeight) / totalWeight;
    // record distribution
    await db.reward_distributions.create({
      data:{ user_id: s.user_id, period_start:periodStart, period_end:periodEnd, amount:reward }
    });
    // mint tokens to contract for future claims
    await mint(process.env.HMY_TOKEN_ADDRESS!, process.env.ADMIN_KEY!, /*contract address?*/ , reward);
  }
}

distribute();

GET /api/launchpad/status
// pages/api/launchpad/status.ts
export default async function handler(req,res) {
  const {user} = req.session;
  const balance = await db.token_balances.findUnique({ where:{ user_id:user.id } });
  const stakes  = await db.token_stakes.findMany({ where:{ user_id:user.id } });
  const pending = await db.reward_distributions.findMany({
     where:{ user_id:user.id, claimed:false }
  });
  res.json({ balance, stakes, pending });
}

15.5.2 StakeForm
// components/Launchpad/StakeForm.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function StakeForm({ onStaked }) {
  const [amt, setAmt] = useState(0);

  const handleStake = async () => {
    await fetch('/api/launchpad/stake', {
      method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ amount:amt })
    });
    onStaked();
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2">
      <input
        type="number" min="1" value={amt}
        onChange={e=>setAmt(+e.target.value)}
        className="border px-2 py-1 w-24"
      />
      <button onClick={handleStake} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 rounded">
        Stake HMX
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
15.5.3 StakesList & RewardsList
// components/Launchpad/StakesList.tsx
export default function StakesList({ stakes, onUnstaked }) {
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-2">
      {stakes.map(s => (
        <li key={s.id} className="flex justify-between">
          <span>{s.amount} HMX (unlocks {new Date(s.unlockAt).toLocaleDateString()})</span>
          <button
            disabled={Date.now() < new Date(s.unlockAt).getTime()}
            onClick={async()=>{
              await fetch('/api/launchpad/unstake',{ method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ stakeId:s.id }) });
              onUnstaked();
            }}
            className="text-red-600"
          >Unstake</button>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

// components/Launchpad/RewardsList.tsx
export default function RewardsList({ rewards, onClaimed }) {
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-2">
      {rewards.map(r => (
        <li key={r.id} className="flex justify-between">
          <span>{new Date(r.periodStart).toLocaleDateString()}–{new Date(r.periodEnd).toLocaleDateString()}: {r.amount} HMX</span>
          <button
            onClick={async()=>{
              await fetch('/api/launchpad/claim',{ method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ distributionId:r.id }) });
              onClaimed();
            }}
            className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-2 rounded"
          >Claim</button>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
15.6 Acceptance Criteria

Token & Staking
User stakes HMX, tokens lock for min_stake_days.
Unstake only after unlock date, tokens return to user wallet.
Reward Distribution
Cron job calculates weekly rewards, records in reward_distributions.
Token minting to staking contract funding pool.
Claiming
User can claim pending amounts → minted into their HMX balance.
Once claimed, cannot claim again.
UI/UX
Launchpad page shows real-time balance, stakes, pending rewards.
Forms validate input and disable while processing.
Smart Contract
HarmonyToken ERC-20 supports mint.
HarmonyStaking enforces maxSupply of stakes & unlock logic.
Security
All endpoints require authentication.
Amounts ≥1, distribution only unclaimed records.
Tests
Unit tests for stake/unstake handlers.
Integration test: stake → cron simulate → reward created → claim → balance increases.

GET /api/launchpad/integrations/requirements?templateId=…
// pages/api/launchpad/integrations/requirements.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { templateId } = req.query as { templateId: string };
  const userId = req.session.user.id;

  const required = await db.agent_template_integrations.findMany({ where:{ template_id: templateId } });
  const configs  = await db.integration_configs.findMany({
    where:{ user_id:userId, provider:{ in: required.map(r=>r.provider) } }
  });

  const statusMap = Object.fromEntries(configs.map(c => [c.provider, c.status]));
  res.json(required.map(r=>({
    provider: r.provider,
    status: statusMap[r.provider] || 'pending'
  })));
};

export default handler;

// components/Launchpad/IntegrationChecklist.tsx
import useSWR from 'swr';
import { signIn } from 'next-auth/react';

export default function IntegrationChecklist({ templateId }: { templateId: string }) {
  const { data } = useSWR(`/api/launchpad/integrations/requirements?templateId=${templateId}`, fetcher);

  if (!data) return <p>Loading integrations…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Required Integrations</h3>
      {data.map((i:{provider:string,status:string})=>(
        <div key={i.provider} className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 border rounded">
          <span className="capitalize">{i.provider}</span>
          {i.status === 'connected' ? (
            <span className="text-green-600">Connected</span>
          ) : (
            <button 
              className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded"
              onClick={()=>{
                // OAuth providers
                if (['google','twitter','facebook'].includes(i.provider)) {
                  signIn(i.provider, { callbackUrl: '/dashboard/launchpad/integrations' });
                } else {
                  // redirect to custom connect form
                  window.location.href = `/dashboard/launchpad/integrations?provider=${i.provider}`;
                }
              }}
            >
              {i.status==='failed' ? 'Reconnect' : 'Connect'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

16.1 Database Schema

-- Credit packs purchased via Stripe
CREATE TABLE credit_purchases (
  id               UUID      PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  stripe_session   TEXT      NOT NULL,
  credits          INTEGER   NOT NULL,
  amount_usd       NUMERIC   NOT NULL,
  status           TEXT      NOT NULL,               -- 'created','paid','failed'
  created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

-- Agent purchases via Stripe (fallback for non-NFT buyers)
CREATE TABLE agent_purchases (
  id               UUID      PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  instance_id      UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES agent_instances(id),
  stripe_session   TEXT      NOT NULL,
  amount_usd       NUMERIC   NOT NULL,
  payment_method   TEXT      NOT NULL,               -- 'stripe','token'
  status           TEXT      NOT NULL,               -- 'created','paid','failed'
  created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

Products & Prices in your Stripe Dashboard:

16.3 API Endpoints

16.3.1 Create Checkout Session
Credits

// pages/api/stripe/checkout-credits.ts
import Stripe from 'stripe';
const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, { apiVersion: '2022-11-15' });

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { packId } = req.body;
  // Map packId → priceId & credits
  const packMap = {
    buy100: { priceId: 'price_1XXX', credits:100 },
    buy300: { priceId: 'price_1YYY', credits:300 },
    buy700: { priceId: 'price_1ZZZ', credits:700 }
  };
  const pack = packMap[packId];
  if (!pack) return res.status(400).json({ error:'Invalid pack' });

  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types:['card'],
    line_items:[{ price: pack.priceId, quantity:1 }],
    mode:'payment',
    success_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/dashboard/billing?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}`,
    cancel_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/checkout/credits?cancelled=true`
  });

  // Record pending purchase
  await db.credit_purchases.create({
    data:{
      user_id: req.session.user.id,
      stripe_session: session.id,
      credits: pack.credits,
      amount_usd: session.amount_total! / 100,
      status: 'created'
    }
  });

  res.json({ url: session.url });
}
Agent Purchase

// pages/api/stripe/checkout-agent.ts
import Stripe from 'stripe';
const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, { apiVersion: '2022-11-15' });

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const { instanceId, priceUsd } = req.body;
  const priceInCents = Math.round(priceUsd * 100);

  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types:['card'],
    line_items:[{ price_data:{
        currency:'usd',
        product_data:{ name:`Agent ${instanceId}` },
        unit_amount: priceInCents
      },
      quantity:1 }],
    mode:'payment',
    success_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/agent/${instanceId}?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}`,
    cancel_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/marketplace?cancelled=true`
  });

  await db.agent_purchases.create({
    data:{
      user_id: req.session.user.id,
      instance_id: instanceId,
      stripe_session: session.id,
      amount_usd: priceUsd,
      payment_method: 'stripe',
      status: 'created'
    }
  });

  res.json({ url: session.url });
}
16.3.2 Stripe Webhook Handler
// pages/api/stripe/webhook.ts
import Stripe from 'stripe';
import { buffer } from 'micro';
const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, { apiVersion:'2022-11-15' });

export const config = { api: { bodyParser:false } };

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const buf = await buffer(req);
  const sig = req.headers['stripe-signature']!;
  let event: Stripe.Event;

  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(buf, sig, process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!);
  } catch(err) {
    return res.status(400).send(`Webhook Error: ${err.message}`);
  }

  switch(event.type) {
    case 'checkout.session.completed':
      const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;
      // Update credit_purchases or agent_purchases
      await handleSessionComplete(session);
      break;
    // … handle invoices.payment_failed, etc.
  }

  res.json({ received: true });
}

async function handleSessionComplete(session: Stripe.Checkout.Session) {
  // Try update credits
  const cp = await db.credit_purchases.findUnique({ where:{ stripe_session:session.id } });
  if (cp) {
    await db.credit_purchases.update({
      where:{ id:cp.id },
      data:{ status:'paid' }
    });
    // credit user
    await db.users.update({
      where:{ id:cp.user_id },
      data:{ credits: { increment: cp.credits } }
    });
    return;
  }
  // Try update agent purchase
  const ap = await db.agent_purchases.findUnique({ where:{ stripe_session:session.id } });
  if (ap) {
    await db.agent_purchases.update({
      where:{ id:ap.id },
      data:{ status:'paid' }
    });
    // add sale record
    await db.agent_sales.create({
      data:{
        instance_id:ap.instance_id,
        buyer_id:ap.user_id,
        amount_usd: ap.amount_usd,
        is_primary:true
      }
    });
    return;
  }
}

// pages/checkout/credits.tsx
import { useRouter } from 'next/router';
export default function BuyCredits() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const buy = async (packId:string) => {
    const { url } = await fetch('/api/stripe/checkout-credits',{
      method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ packId }), headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}
    }).then(r=>r.json());
    window.location.href = url;
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl">Buy Credits</h1>
      {['buy100','buy300','buy700'].map(id=>(
        <button key={id}
          onClick={()=>buy(id)}
          className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white py-2 rounded"
        >
          {id==='buy100'? '$19 → 100 credits' : id==='buy300'? '$49 → 300 credits' : '$99 → 700 credits'}
        </button>
      ))}
      {router.query.cancelled && <p className="text-red-500">Payment cancelled.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

17.0 Site-Map Changes

/team                         ← Team management dashboard

17.1 Database Schema

-- A team (workspace) owned by a user
CREATE TABLE teams (
  id           UUID      PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_id     UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  name         TEXT      NOT NULL,
  created_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Team members
CREATE TABLE team_members (
  id           UUID      PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  team_id      UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES teams(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id      UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  role         TEXT      NOT NULL,            -- 'admin' | 'member'
  invited_at   TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
  accepted     BOOLEAN   NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE
);

-- Which agents the team has access to (shared licenses)
CREATE TABLE team_agent_access (
  id               UUID      PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  team_id          UUID      NOT NULL REFERENCES teams(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  agent_instance_id UUID     NOT NULL REFERENCES agent_instances(id),
  max_seats        INT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,   -- how many simultaneous seats
  created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
17.2 API Endpoints

17.2.1 Create & Manage Teams
GET /api/team
Returns all teams the user owns or is a member of.
POST /api/team
// Body: { name: string }
// Creates new team with user as owner + admin
PUT /api/team/:teamId
// Body: { name?: string }
// Only team owner can rename
DELETE /api/team/:teamId
// Only team owner; cascade deletes members & access
17.2.2 Invite & Manage Members
POST /api/team/:teamId/invite
// Body: { email: string, role: 'admin'|'member' }
// Creates team_members entry with accepted=false, sends invite email
POST /api/team/:teamId/members/:memberId/accept
// Marks accepted=true if invite matches user
DELETE /api/team/:teamId/members/:memberId
// Remove a member (owner or admin only)
PUT /api/team/:teamId/members/:memberId/role
// Body: { role: 'admin'|'member' }
17.2.3 Team Agent Access
GET /api/team/:teamId/agents
List all agent instances shared with the team.
POST /api/team/:teamId/agents
// Body: { instanceId: UUID, maxSeats: number }
// Owner/admin only: grant team access
PUT /api/team/:teamId/agents/:accessId
// Body: { maxSeats: number }
DELETE /api/team/:teamId/agents/:accessId
Revoke team access.
17.3 NextAuth.js / ACL

In your Next.js API handlers:

// lib/authorize.ts
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
export async function requireTeamAdmin(req, teamId) {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  const userId = session?.user.id;
  const member = await db.team_members.findFirst({
    where:{ team_id:teamId, user_id:userId, accepted:true }
  });
  if (!member || (member.role !== 'admin' && member.user_id !== team.owner_id)) {
    throw new Error('Forbidden');
  }
}
Use requireTeamAdmin or requireTeamMember at start of each handler.

export default function TeamDashboard() {

17.4.2 TeamList
// components/Team/TeamList.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function TeamList({ teams, onCreated }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const create = async() => {
    await fetch('/api/team',{ method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ name }), headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} });
    setName(''); onCreated();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input 
          value={name} onChange={e=>setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="New team name"
          className="border px-2 py-1 flex-1"
        />
        <button onClick={create} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-1 rounded">Create Team</button>
      </div>
      <ul>
        {teams.map(t=>(
          <li key={t.id}>{t.name} {t.ownerId===session.user.id && '(Owner)'}</li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
17.4.3 MemberPanel
// components/Team/MemberPanel.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function MemberPanel({ team, onChange }) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const invite = async() => {
    await fetch(`/api/team/${team.id}/invite`,{
      method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({ email, role:'member' }),
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}
    });
    setEmail(''); onChange();
  };

  return (
    <div className="mt-4">
      <h3 className="font-semibold">Members</h3>
      <ul className="list-disc list-inside">
        {team.members.map(m=>(
          <li key={m.id}>
            {m.user.email} – {m.role} – {m.accepted ? 'Accepted' : 'Invited'}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
        <input 
          value={email} onChange={e=>setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="Invite by email"
          className="border px-2 py-1 flex-1"
        />
        <button onClick={invite} className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-1 rounded">Invite</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
17.4.4 AgentAccess
// components/Team/AgentAccess.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function AgentAccess({ team, onChange }) {
  const [instId, setInstId] = useState('');
  const [seats, setSeats] = useState(1);

  const grant = async() => {
    await fetch(`/api/team/${team.id}/agents`,{
      method:'POST',
      body:JSON.stringify({ instanceId:instId, maxSeats:seats }),
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}
    });
    setInstId(''); setSeats(1); onChange();
  };

  return (
    <div className="mt-4">
      <h3 className="font-semibold">Shared Agents</h3>
      <ul className="list-disc list-inside">
        {team.agentAccess.map(a=>(
          <li key={a.id}>
            Agent {a.agentInstanceId} – Seats: {a.maxSeats}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
        <input
          value={instId} onChange={e=>setInstId(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Agent Instance ID" className="border px-2 py-1 flex-1"
        />
        <input
          type="number" value={seats} min={1}
          onChange={e=>setSeats(+e.target.value)} className="border px-2 py-1 w-20"
        />
        <button onClick={grant} className="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-1 rounded">
          Grant Access
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
17.5 Enforcement in Agent Usage

Before allowing an agent prompt:

// lib/checkAccess.ts
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

export async function verifyAgentAccess(userId, instanceId) {
  // Direct owner?
  const inst = await db.agent_instances.findUnique({where:{id:instanceId}});
  if (inst.ownerId === userId) return true;
  // Team access?
  const memberships = await db.team_members.findMany({
    where:{ user_id:userId, accepted:true },
    select:{ team_id:true }
  });
  const access = await db.team_agent_access.findFirst({
    where:{
      team_id:{ in:memberships.map(m=>m.team_id) },
      agent_instance_id:instanceId
    }
  });
  return !!access;
}
Use verifyAgentAccess(...) at the top of your /api/agent/chat and other route handlers.

Team CRUD
Users can create, rename, delete teams.
Invitations
Invite by email → pending membership → acceptance flow.
Agent Sharing
Owner/admin grants agent instance with seat count.
Members can use prompts against that instance up to seat limits.
Access Checks
verifyAgentAccess blocks unauthorized calls.
UI
Team page lists teams, members, shared agents with clear roles/seats.
Invite and grant flows update live.
Tests
Unit tests for invite accept, authorize, access check.
Integration: member uses shared agent then revokes → call rejected.

18.0 Site-Map

/case-studies                ← List all case studies
/case-studies/[id]           ← Case study detail + interactive demo

18.1 Database Schema

-- Table of all case studies
CREATE TABLE case_studies (
  id            UUID         PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title         TEXT         NOT NULL,
  summary       TEXT         NOT NULL,
  media_type    TEXT         NOT NULL, -- 'video' | 'interactive' | 'mixed'
  media_url     TEXT,               -- e.g. YouTube embed, S3 link
  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Steps for each case study (ordered)
CREATE TABLE case_study_steps (
  id             UUID        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  case_study_id  UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES case_studies(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  step_order     INT         NOT NULL,
  type           TEXT        NOT NULL, -- 'video' | 'chat-demo' | 'task-list'
  payload        JSONB       NOT NULL, -- e.g. { url, initialMessages, tasks }
  created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
18.2 API Endpoints

18.2.1 List Case Studies
// pages/api/case-studies/index.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_, res) => {
  const studies = await db.case_studies.findMany({
    select: { id: true, title: true, summary: true, media_type: true, media_url: true }
  });
  res.json({ caseStudies: studies });
};

export default handler;
18.2.2 Get Case Study Detail
// pages/api/case-studies/[id].ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.query as { id: string };
  const study = await db.case_studies.findUnique({ where: { id } });
  if (!study) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });

  const steps = await db.case_study_steps.findMany({
    where: { case_study_id: id },
    orderBy: { step_order: 'asc' }
  });

  res.json({ caseStudy: study, steps });
};

export default handler;

18.3.2 Case Study Detail Page
// pages/case-studies/[id].tsx
import { useRouter } from 'next/router';
import useSWR from 'swr';
import VideoStep      from '@/components/CaseStudies/VideoStep';
import ChatDemoStep   from '@/components/CaseStudies/ChatDemoStep';
import TaskListStep   from '@/components/CaseStudies/TaskListStep';

export default function CaseStudyDetail() {
  const { query } = useRouter();
  const { data } = useSWR(query.id ? `/api/case-studies/${query.id}` : null, fetcher);
  if (!data) return <p>Loading…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">{data.caseStudy.title}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-700">{data.caseStudy.summary}</p>

      {data.steps.map((step: any) => {
        switch (step.type) {
          case 'video':
            return <VideoStep key={step.id} url={step.payload.url} />;
          case 'chat-demo':
            return (
              <ChatDemoStep
                key={step.id}
                initialMessages={step.payload.initialMessages}
                userReadOnly={true}
              />
            );
          case 'task-list':
            return <TaskListStep key={step.id} tasks={step.payload.tasks} />;
          default:
            return null;
        }
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

// components/CaseStudies/ChatDemoStep.tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import AgentMessage from '@/components/AgentMessage';

export default function ChatDemoStep({
  initialMessages,
  userReadOnly = false
}: {
  initialMessages: { sender: string; text: string }[];
  userReadOnly?: boolean;
}) {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState(initialMessages);

  // no sending in demo mode
  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg p-4 bg-gray-100 mb-4">
      {messages.map((m, i) => (
        <AgentMessage key={i} sender={m.sender} text={m.text} />
      ))}
      {userReadOnly && (
        <p className="text-sm italic text-gray-500 mt-2">Demo mode – chat disabled</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

20.1 Database Schema

CREATE TABLE faq_entries (
  id            UUID        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  question      TEXT        NOT NULL,

20.2.1 List FAQs
// pages/api/faq/index.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { category } = req.query as { category?: string };
  const where = category ? { category } : {};
  const faqs = await db.faq_entries.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: { display_order: 'asc' }
  });
  res.json({ faqs });
};

export default handler;

// components/FAQ/FAQList.tsx
'use client';
import useSWR from 'swr';
import { Disclosure } from '@headlessui/react';
import { ChevronUpIcon } from '@heroicons/react/20/solid';

export default function FAQList({ category = 'general' }: { category?: string }) {
  const { data } = useSWR(`/api/faq?category=${category}`, fetcher);
  if (!data) return <p>Loading FAQs…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {data.faqs.map((faq: any) => (
        <Disclosure key={faq.id}>
          {({ open }) => (
            <div className="border rounded-lg p-4">
              <Disclosure.Button className="flex justify-between w-full text-left">
                <span className="font-medium">{faq.question}</span>
                <ChevronUpIcon
                  className={`${
                    open ? 'transform rotate-180' : ''
                  } w-5 h-5 text-gray-500`}
                />
              </Disclosure.Button>
              <Disclosure.Panel className="mt-2 text-gray-700">
                {faq.answer}
              </Disclosure.Panel>
            </div>
          )}
        </Disclosure>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

Below is Step 19 – Final QA & Security Layer, fully detailed for your dev team.

19.0 Overview

Ensure the entire platform is production-hardened: pen-tested, rate-limited, input-sanitized, access-controlled, monitored, and audited.

19.1 Penetration Testing & Vulnerability Scans

19.1.1 OWASP ZAP Baseline Scan via GitHub Actions
# .github/workflows/security-scan.yml
name: Security Scan
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 2 * * 1'     # Weekly on Monday at 02:00 UTC
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  zap-scan:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      - name: Start application
        run: npm install && npm run build && npm start &
      - name: Wait for app
        run: npx wait-on http://localhost:3000
      - name: Run OWASP ZAP Baseline Scan
        uses: zaproxy/action-baseline@v0.5.0
        with:
          zap_api_key: ${{ secrets.ZAP_API_KEY }}
          target: 'http://host.docker.internal:3000'
          rules_file_name: 'baseline-rules.yaml'
19.1.2 Dependency Vulnerability Scans
Enable Dependabot in .github/dependabot.yml:
version: 2
updates:
  - package-ecosystem: "npm"
    directory: "/"
    schedule:
      interval: "daily"
19.2 Rate Limiting Middleware

Create middleware.ts at project root:

// middleware.ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { Ratelimit } from '@upstash/ratelimit';
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';

const redis = new Redis({ url: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL!, token: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN! });
const rl = new Ratelimit({ redis, limiter: Ratelimit.slidingWindow(100, '1 m') });

export async function middleware(req: NextRequest) {
  const ip = req.ip ?? 'unknown';
  const { success } = await rl.limit(ip);
  if (!success) {
    return new NextResponse('Rate limit exceeded', { status: 429 });
  }
  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = { matcher: ['/api/:path*'] };
19.3 Prompt Injection & Input Sanitization

// lib/security/promptFilter.ts
import createDOMPurify from 'isomorphic-dompurify';

export function sanitizePrompt(input: string): string {
  // Strip out malicious patterns
  let clean = createDOMPurify.sanitize(input);
  // Basic injection heuristics
  clean = clean.replace(/#{2,}|<\/?script>/gi, '');
  return clean;
}
Use in API routes:

import { sanitizePrompt } from '@/lib/security/promptFilter';

const userPrompt = sanitizePrompt(req.body.prompt);
19.4 Authentication & Role Checks

19.4.1 JWT Validation Middleware
// lib/security/requireAuth.ts
import { getToken } from 'next-auth/jwt';
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next';

const secret = process.env.NEXTAUTH_SECRET!;

export async function requireAuth(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const token = await getToken({ req, secret });
  if (!token) {
    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
    throw 'Unauthorized';
  }
  return token;
}
Use at top of every API route:

import { requireAuth } from '@/lib/security/requireAuth';

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const token = await requireAuth(req, res);
  // ...rest of handler
}
19.5 Database Security & RLS (Supabase)

-- Enable row-level security
ALTER TABLE agent_instances ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Policy: owners can read/write their agent_instances
CREATE POLICY "Owners can manage own instances"
  ON agent_instances
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

-- Policy: members of team_agent_access can read
CREATE POLICY "Team members can read shared agents"
  ON agent_instances
  FOR SELECT
  USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM team_agent_access
      WHERE team_agent_access.agent_instance_id = agent_instances.id
        AND team_agent_access.team_id IN (
          SELECT team_id FROM team_members
          WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND accepted = true
        )
    )
  );

npm install @sentry/nextjs
// sentry.server.config.js
import * as Sentry from '@sentry/nextjs';

Sentry.init({
  dsn: process.env.SENTRY_DSN,
  tracesSampleRate: 0.1,
});
Wrap API handlers:

// pages/api/...ts

19.7 Audit & Access Logs

CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
  id           UUID       PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id      UUID       REFERENCES users(id),
  action       TEXT       NOT NULL,
  resource     TEXT,
  resource_id  UUID,
  ip_address   TEXT,
  user_agent   TEXT,
  created_at   TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);
Middleware to record:

// lib/security/audit.ts
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

export async function logAudit({ userId, action, resource, resourceId, req }) {
  await db.audit_logs.create({
    data:{
      user_id:userId,action,resource,resource_id:resourceId,
      ip_address:req.headers['x-forwarded-for'] || req.socket.remoteAddress,
      user_agent:req.headers['user-agent'] || ''
    }
  });
}
Call logAudit in critical routes (e.g., payments, agent edits).

– Exceeding 100 requests/min returns 429.

– Sentry captures ≥90% of uncaught exceptions.

– 95th percentile latency < 300ms at 1k concurrent users.

Below is Step 22 – CRM & KPI Connector Modules, fully detailed and copy-/paste-ready for your dev team.

22.1 Database Schema

-- Table of connected CRM integrations per user
CREATE TABLE crm_integrations (
  id            UUID        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id       UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  provider      TEXT        NOT NULL,             -- 'hubspot','highlevel','salesforce','pipedrive'
  access_token  TEXT        NOT NULL,             -- encrypted or in Vault
  refresh_token TEXT,
  scope         TEXT[],
  connected_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Logs of sync operations
CREATE TABLE crm_sync_logs (
  id                  UUID        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  integration_id      UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES crm_integrations(id),
  entity              TEXT        NOT NULL,         -- 'contacts','deals','tasks'
  direction           TEXT        NOT NULL,         -- 'inbound'|'outbound'
  status              TEXT        NOT NULL,         -- 'pending'|'success'|'error'
  details             JSONB,
  executed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
22.2 OAuth Connect API Routes

22.2.1 HubSpot Connect
// pages/api/integrations/hubspot/connect.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  // Step 1: redirect user to HubSpot OAuth consent
  const redirectUri = process.env.HUBSPOT_REDIRECT_URI;
  const clientId   = process.env.HUBSPOT_CLIENT_ID;
  const url = `https://app.hubspot.com/oauth/authorize?client_id=${clientId}`
            + `&redirect_uri=${encodeURIComponent(redirectUri)}&scope=contacts%20crm.objects.deals.read`;
  res.redirect(url);
};

export default handler;
22.2.2 HubSpot OAuth Callback
// pages/api/integrations/hubspot/callback.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import axios from 'axios';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { code } = req.query as { code: string };
  // Exchange code for tokens
  const tokenRes = await axios.post('https://api.hubapi.com/oauth/v1/token', null, {
    params: {
      grant_type: 'authorization_code',
      client_id: process.env.HUBSPOT_CLIENT_ID,
      client_secret: process.env.HUBSPOT_CLIENT_SECRET,
      redirect_uri: process.env.HUBSPOT_REDIRECT_URI,
      code
    },
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }
  });
  const { access_token, refresh_token, scope } = tokenRes.data;

  // Store in DB
  await db.crm_integrations.create({
    data: {
      user_id: session.user.id,
      provider: 'hubspot',
      access_token,
      refresh_token,
      scope: scope.split(' ')
    }
  });

  res.redirect('/settings/integrations');
};

export default handler;
Repeat analogous routes for HighLevel, Salesforce, Pipedrive — swapping URLs, scopes, and env vars.
22.3 Webhook Endpoints

22.3.1 HubSpot Webhook Receiver
// pages/api/webhooks/hubspot.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import crypto from 'crypto';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  // Validate signature (if set up)
  const sig = req.headers['x-hubspot-signature'] as string;
  // ...validate HMAC with your SECRET...
  const events = req.body; // array of change events

  for (const ev of events) {
    // e.g., ev.subscriptionType = 'contact.creation'
    await db.crm_sync_logs.create({
      data: {
        integration_id: /* lookup hubspot integration id */,
        entity: ev.subscriptionType.split('.')[0],
        direction: 'inbound',
        status: 'pending',
        details: ev
      }
    });
  }
  res.status(200).json({ received: true });
};

export default handler;
Setup each CRM to publish webhooks here.
22.4 Sync Workers (Serverless)

// lib/crm/hubspotSync.ts
import axios from 'axios';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

export async function inboundContactSync(integrationId: string) {
  const integration = await db.crm_integrations.findUnique({ where: { id: integrationId } });
  const res = await axios.get('https://api.hubapi.com/crm/v3/objects/contacts', {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${integration.access_token}` }
  });
  for (const contact of res.data.results) {
    await db.crm_sync_logs.create({
      data: {
        integration_id: integrationId,
        entity: 'contacts',
        direction: 'inbound',
        status: 'success',
        details: contact
      }
    });
    // Optionally: upsert into local contacts table
  }
}
Schedule with Cron (GitHub Actions or Next.js Cron).
22.5 Front-End Integration Manager

// components/IntegrationManager.tsx
'use client';
import useSWR from 'swr';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function IntegrationManager() {
  const { data } = useSWR('/api/integrations/list', fetcher);
  if (!data) return <p>Loading…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {['hubspot','highlevel','salesforce','pipedrive'].map(provider => {
        const connected = data.integrations.some((i:any)=>i.provider===provider);
        return (
          <div key={provider} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border rounded-lg">
            <span className="capitalize">{provider}</span>
            {connected ? (
              <button className="btn-outline" onClick={()=>disconnect(provider)}>Disconnect</button>
            ) : (
              <Link href={`/api/integrations/${provider}/connect`}>
                <a className="btn-primary">Connect</a>
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
22.5.1 List Integrations API
// pages/api/integrations/list.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const integrations = await db.crm_integrations.findMany({
    where: { user_id: session.user.id },
    select: { provider: true, connected_at: true }
  });
  res.json({ integrations });
};

export default handler;

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 6 seconds
Below is Step 23 – Time-Saved & ROI Calculator, fully scoped for your devs. Copy/paste into your repo or ClickUp.

23.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma
model ImpactSetting {
  id               String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId           String
  agentId          String
  taskName         String
  manualSeconds    Int      // expected time if done manually
  agentSeconds     Int      // actual time via agent
  freqPerWeek      Int      // how often/week this task runs
  hourlyRateCents  Int      // user’s billing rate in ¢
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([agentId])
}
23.2 API Routes

23.2.1 Get Impact Metrics
// pages/api/impact/index.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });

  const settings = await prisma.impactSetting.findMany({
    where: { userId: session.user.id }
  });

  // compute for each setting
  const metrics = settings.map(s => {
    const secSavedPerWeek = (s.manualSeconds - s.agentSeconds) * s.freqPerWeek;
    const dollarsPerWeek = (secSavedPerWeek / 3600) * (s.hourlyRateCents / 100);
    return {
      id: s.id,
      agentId: s.agentId,
      taskName: s.taskName,
      secSavedPerWeek,
      dollarsPerWeek,
      dollarsPerMonth: dollarsPerWeek * 4,
      dollarsPerYear: dollarsPerWeek * 52
    };
  });

  const totalWeekly = metrics.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.dollarsPerWeek, 0);
  res.json({ metrics, totalWeekly, totalMonthly: totalWeekly * 4, totalYearly: totalWeekly * 52 });
};

export default handler;
23.2.2 Create/Update Impact Setting
// pages/api/impact/[id].ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const userId = session.user.id;
  const { id } = req.query as { id: string };
  const body = req.body as {
    agentId: string;
    taskName: string;
    manualSeconds: number;
    agentSeconds: number;
    freqPerWeek: number;
    hourlyRateCents: number;
  };

  const upserted = await prisma.impactSetting.upsert({
    where: { id: id || '' },
    create: { ...body, userId },
    update: { ...body }
  });

  res.json(upserted);
};

export default handler;
23.3 Front-End Hook

// hooks/useImpact.ts
import useSWR from 'swr';

export function useImpact() {
  const { data, mutate, error } = useSWR('/api/impact', fetcher);
  return {
    metrics: data?.metrics || [],
    totalWeekly: data?.totalWeekly || 0,
    totalMonthly: data?.totalMonthly || 0,
    totalYearly: data?.totalYearly || 0,
    isLoading: !data && !error,
    error,
    refresh: mutate
  };
}
23.4 Impact Calculator UI

// components/Impact/ImpactCalculator.tsx
'use client';
import { useImpact } from '@/hooks/useImpact';

export default function ImpactCalculator() {
  const { metrics, totalWeekly, totalMonthly, totalYearly, isLoading } = useImpact();
  if (isLoading) return <p>Loading impact…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Time Saved & ROI</h2>

      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Agent / Task</th>
            <th>Hours Saved /Wk</th>
            <th>Value $/Wk</th>
            <th>$/Mo</th>
            <th>$/Yr</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {metrics.map(m => (
            <tr key={m.id}>
              <td>{m.taskName}</td>
              <td>{(m.secSavedPerWeek/3600).toFixed(2)}</td>
              <td>${m.dollarsPerWeek.toFixed(2)}</td>
              <td>${m.dollarsPerMonth.toFixed(2)}</td>
              <td>${m.dollarsPerYear.toFixed(2)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg">
        <p className="font-medium">Total Saved:</p>
        <p>Weekly: <strong>${totalWeekly.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
        <p>Monthly: <strong>${totalMonthly.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
        <p>Yearly: <strong>${totalYearly.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

23.5 Seed/Default Settings

On agent install, seed one ImpactSetting per agent with your best-guess values:

// lib/impact/seedDefaults.ts
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const defaults = [
  { agentId: 'cashflow', taskName: 'Invoice Follow-Up', manualSeconds: 900, agentSeconds: 60, freqPerWeek: 5 },
  { agentId: 'iris',     taskName: 'Social Engagement', manualSeconds: 1200, agentSeconds: 120, freqPerWeek: 7 },
  // … etc for all 12 agents
];

export async function seedImpactForUser(userId: string, hourlyRateCents = 5000) {
  for (const d of defaults) {
    await prisma.impactSetting.upsert({
      where: { userId_agentId: { userId, agentId: d.agentId } },
      create: { userId, hourlyRateCents, ...d },
      update: { hourlyRateCents, ...d }
    });
  }
}
Call seedImpactForUser(session.user.id) after signup or first login.

24.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

model AgentDraft {
  id            String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String
  name          String?
  description   String?
  category      String?         // e.g. “Sales”, “Support”
  pricing       Json?           // { creditCost, nftFloor, royaltyPct }
  tools         String[]        // e.g. ['crm','email','sms']
  toneSettings  Json?           // { tone: string, formality: string, … }
  isPublished   Boolean  @default(false)
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime @updatedAt

  AgentTraining AgentTraining[]
}

model AgentTraining {
  id            String   @id @default(cuid())
  draftId       String
  fileUrl       String         // Supabase/S3 path
  promptExample String?        // sample chat snippet
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now())

  draft         AgentDraft     @relation(fields: [draftId], references: [id])
}
24.2 API Routes

24.2.1 Create / Update Draft
// pages/api/agents/builder/draft.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const userId = session.user.id;

  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    // Create new draft
    const draft = await prisma.agentDraft.create({ data: { userId } });
    return res.json(draft);
  }

  if (req.method === 'PUT') {
    // Update fields
    const { id, ...data } = req.body;
    const updated = await prisma.agentDraft.update({
      where: { id },
      data,
    });
    return res.json(updated);
  }

  res.status(405).end();
};

export default handler;
24.2.2 Upload Training File
// pages/api/agents/builder/upload.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import formidable from 'formidable';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { uploadToSupabase } from '@/lib/storage';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export const config = { api: { bodyParser: false } };

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const form = new formidable.IncomingForm();
  form.parse(req, async (err, fields, files) => {
    const file = files.file as formidable.File;
    const fileUrl = await uploadToSupabase(file.filepath, file.originalFilename!);
    const training = await prisma.agentTraining.create({
      data: { draftId: fields.draftId as string, fileUrl },
    });
    res.json(training);
  });
};

export default handler;
24.2.3 Finalize & Publish
// pages/api/agents/builder/publish.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const { id } = req.body;   // draft ID
  // Validate required fields...
  await prisma.agentDraft.update({ where:{ id }, data:{ isPublished: true } });
  // Optionally: copy to production Agent table...
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;

24.4 Data Flow & State

Draft Creation: on entering builder, POST draft → get draftId.
Per-Step Save: each step PUTs changed fields on that draftId.
Storage: uploaded files go to Supabase/S3; URL stored in AgentTraining.
Preview: review step fetches full draft + trainings.
Publish: final step flips isPublished → moves draft to live Agent table via background job or on-the-fly.
24.5 Acceptance Criteria

Wizard Steps
– 6 steps render in order; cannot skip.
Persistence
– Refresh retains draft state.
Uploads
– Files ≤ 10 MB accepted; show spinner & error on fail.
Pricing
– Credit cost ≥ 0; NFT floor ≥ 0; royalty 0–100%.
Publish
– Required fields (name, tools, pricing) validated; error otherwise.
Redirects
– On publish, user lands on “Complete” and can access /agents.
Security
– All builder APIs require auth.
Tests
– Unit tests for draft CRUD, upload, publish.
– Integration test for full wizard flow.

25.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

model AgentLicense {
  id             String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String   @index
  agentId        String
  licenseType    String   // "credit" | "nft"
  creditsUsed    Int      @default(0)    // for credit-based licenses
  nftTokenId     String?                  // on-chain token ID
  nftContract    String?                  // contract address
  royaltyPct     Int      @default(0)    // if user resells (for creator)
  validUntil     DateTime?               // expiry for subscriptions
  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([userId, agentId, licenseType])
}

model Transaction {
  id             String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String   @index
  agentId        String?
  type           String   // "credit_purchase"|"nft_mint"
  amountCents    Int?
  nftTokenId     String?
  nftContract    String?
  status         String   @default("pending") // "pending"|"success"|"failed"
  metadata       Json?
  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt
}
25.2 Stripe Credit Purchase Flow

25.2.1 Create Checkout Session
// pages/api/stripe/create-checkout.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import Stripe from 'stripe';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET!, { apiVersion: '2022-11-15' });

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });

  const { agentId, packCents } = req.body as { agentId?: string, packCents: number };

  // Create a Transaction record
  const tx = await prisma.transaction.create({
    data: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      agentId: agentId || null,
      type: 'credit_purchase',
      amountCents: packCents,
      status: 'pending'
    }
  });

  const stripeSession = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types: ['card'],
    mode: 'payment',
    line_items: [{
      price_data: {
        currency: 'usd',
        product_data: { name: agentId ? `Credits for Agent ${agentId}` : 'Harmony Credits' },
        unit_amount: packCents,
      },
      quantity: 1,
    }],
    success_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/settings/billing?success=1&txId=${tx.id}`,
    cancel_url: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/settings/billing?canceled=1`,
    metadata: { txId: tx.id }
  });

  res.json({ url: stripeSession.url });
};

export default handler;
25.2.2 Stripe Webhook
// pages/api/stripe/webhook.ts
import { buffer } from 'micro';
import Stripe from 'stripe';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export const config = { api: { bodyParser: false } };

const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET!, { apiVersion: '2022-11-15' });
const endpointSecret = process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!;

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const buf = await buffer(req);
  const sig = req.headers['stripe-signature']!;

  let event: Stripe.Event;
  try { event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(buf, sig, endpointSecret); }
  catch (err) { return res.status(400).send(`Webhook Error: ${(err as Error).message}`); }

  if (event.type === 'checkout.session.completed') {
    const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;
    const txId = session.metadata?.txId as string;

    // Mark transaction success
    await prisma.transaction.update({
      where: { id: txId },
      data: { status: 'success' }
    });

    // Credit user
    const tx = await prisma.transaction.findUnique({ where: { id: txId } });
    await prisma.agentLicense.upsert({
      where: { userId_agentId_licenseType: { userId: tx!.userId, agentId: tx!.agentId ?? '', licenseType: 'credit' } },
      create: {
        userId: tx!.userId,
        agentId: tx!.agentId ?? '',
        licenseType: 'credit',
        creditsUsed: 0
      },
      update: {}
    });
  }

  res.status(200).json({ received: true });
}
25.3 NFT Minting Flow

25.3.1 Mint Endpoint
// pages/api/licenses/mint-nft.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { ThirdwebSDK } from '@thirdweb-dev/sdk';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const sdk = new ThirdwebSDK('polygon'); // or 'ethereum'

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { agentId } = req.body as { agentId: string };
  const contract = await sdk.getContract(process.env.THIRDWEB_CONTRACT_ADDRESS!);

  // Mint to user's wallet
  const userAddress = session.user.walletAddress;
  const metadata = { name: `Harmony Agent:${agentId}`, agentId, mintedAt: new Date() };
  const tx = await contract.erc721.mintTo(userAddress, metadata);

  // Record transaction & license
  const tokenId = tx.id.toString();
  await prisma.transaction.create({
    data: { userId: session.user.id, agentId, type: 'nft_mint', nftContract: contract.getAddress(), nftTokenId: tokenId, status: 'success' }
  });
  await prisma.agentLicense.upsert({
    where: { userId_agentId_licenseType: { userId: session.user.id, agentId, licenseType: 'nft' } },
    create: { userId: session.user.id, agentId, licenseType: 'nft', nftContract: contract.getAddress(), nftTokenId: tokenId },
    update: { nftTokenId: tokenId }
  });

  res.json({ tokenId });
};

export default handler;
25.3.2 License Check
// pages/api/licenses/check.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { agentId } = req.query as { agentId: string };
  const lic = await prisma.agentLicense.findFirst({
    where: { userId: session.user.id, agentId, licenseType: { in: ['nft','credit'] } }
  });

  res.json({ licensed: !!lic, license: lic });
};

export default handler;
25.4 Front-End Components

25.4.1 PurchaseModal.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { mintNft, buyCredits } from '@/lib/purchase';
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react';

export default function PurchaseModal({ agentId, onClose, onSuccess }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleCredits = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/stripe/create-checkout', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ agentId, packCents: 5000 })
    });
    const { url } = await res.json();
    window.location.href = url;
  };

  const handleMint = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/licenses/mint-nft', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({ agentId })
    });
    const { tokenId } = await res.json();
    setLoading(false);
    onSuccess();
  };

  return (
    <div className="modal">
      <h3>Activate Agent</h3>
      <button onClick={handleCredits} disabled={loading}>Buy 5000¢ Credits</button>
      <button onClick={handleMint} disabled={loading}>Mint License NFT</button>
      <button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
    </div>
  );
}
25.4.2 LicenseGate.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function LicenseGate({ agentId, children }) {
  const [licensed, setLicensed] = useState<boolean|null>(null);
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/licenses/check?agentId=${agentId}`)
      .then(r=>r.json()).then(data=>setLicensed(data.licensed));
  }, [agentId]);

  if (licensed === null) return <p>Loading…</p>;
  if (!licensed) return <button onClick={()=>setShowModal(true)}>Activate Agent</button>;

  return (
    <>
      {children}
      {showModal && <PurchaseModal agentId={agentId} onClose={()=>setShowModal(false)} onSuccess={()=>location.reload()} />}
    </>
  );
}

Below is Step 26 – Launchpad Onboarding & Integration Wizard, end-to-end, copy-paste ready for your dev team.

26.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Stores each user’s connected services and their status
model IntegrationSetting {
  id             String     @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String     @index
  provider       String     // e.g. "stripe","hubspot","google_calendar"
  config         Json       // stores provider-specific config (tokens, scopes, flags)
  status         String     @default("pending") // "pending"|"connected"|"error"
  required       Boolean    @default(false)     // true if mandatory for core functionality
  createdAt      DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime   @updatedAt

  @@unique([userId, provider])
}

/// Log sync health for each integration
model IntegrationLog {
  id             String     @id @default(cuid())
  settingId      String     @index
  event          String     // e.g. "oauth_callback","sync_success"
  details        Json?
  createdAt      DateTime   @default(now())

  setting        IntegrationSetting @relation(fields:[settingId], references:[id])
}
26.2 Supported Providers

Category	Provider	Key Scopes / APIs
Payments	Stripe, PayPal	Charges, Subscriptions, Webhooks
CRM	HubSpot, HighLevel, Salesforce, Pipedrive, Zoho	Contacts, Deals, Tasks
Email	Gmail, Outlook	Send, Read, Labels
Calendar	Google Calendar, Office365 Calendar	Events, Free/Busy
PM Tools	ClickUp, Asana, Monday.com, Notion	Tasks, Projects, Comments
Social Media	Twitter, Facebook, LinkedIn, Instagram, YouTube	Posts, Comments, DMs
Chat APIs	OpenAI, Anthropic, Azure OpenAI	Chat Completions
Storage	Google Drive, Dropbox	File upload/download, folders
Comms	Slack, Discord, Microsoft Teams	Webhook, chat, channels
E-comm	Shopify	Products, Orders
Analytics	Google Analytics, Mixpanel	Events, Reports
26.3 OAuth & API Routes

26.3.1 Initiate OAuth
// pages/api/onboarding/[provider]/connect.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';

const providerConfig = {
  gmail: {
    authUrl: 'https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth',
    clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
    scopes: ['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send'],
    redirectUri: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/api/onboarding/gmail/callback`
  },
  clickup: {
    authUrl: 'https://app.clickup.com/api',
    clientId: process.env.CLICKUP_CLIENT_ID,
    scopes: ['team:read','task:write'],
    redirectUri: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/api/onboarding/clickup/callback`
  },
  // …add each provider here
};

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const { provider } = req.query as { provider: string };
  const cfg = providerConfig[provider];
  if (!cfg) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown provider' });

  const url = `${cfg.authUrl}?client_id=${cfg.clientId}` +
    `&redirect_uri=${encodeURIComponent(cfg.redirectUri)}` +
    `&response_type=code&scope=${encodeURIComponent(cfg.scopes.join(' '))}`;
  res.redirect(url);
};

export default handler;
26.3.2 OAuth Callback & Save Tokens
// pages/api/onboarding/[provider]/callback.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import axios from 'axios';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const tokenUrls: Record<string,string> = {
  gmail: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',
  clickup: 'https://api.clickup.com/api/v2/oauth/token',
  // …
};

const providerConfig = {
  gmail: { clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET },
  clickup: { clientId: process.env.CLICKUP_CLIENT_ID, clientSecret: process.env.CLICKUP_CLIENT_SECRET },
  // …
};

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.redirect('/login');
  const { provider, code } = req.query as { provider: string; code: string };
  const url = tokenUrls[provider];
  const cfg = providerConfig[provider];
  if (!url || !cfg) return res.status(400).end();

  // Exchange code for tokens
  const tokenRes = await axios.post(url, {
    grant_type: 'authorization_code',
    client_id: cfg.clientId,
    client_secret: cfg.clientSecret,
    redirect_uri: `${process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL}/api/onboarding/${provider}/callback`,
    code
  }, { headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' } });

  // Upsert IntegrationSetting
  await prisma.integrationSetting.upsert({
    where: { userId_provider: { userId: session.user.id, provider } },
    create: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      provider,
      config: tokenRes.data,
      status: 'connected'
    },
    update: {
      config: tokenRes.data,
      status: 'connected',
      updatedAt: new Date()
    }
  });

  // Log success
  await prisma.integrationLog.create({
    data: {
      settingId: session.user.id + '_' + provider, // adjust to actual PK lookup
      event: 'oauth_callback',
      details: tokenRes.data
    }
  });

  res.redirect('/launchpad/onboarding?provider=' + provider);
};

export default handler;
Repeat analogous callbacks for each provider.
26.4 Token Refresh Worker

// lib/integrations/refreshTokens.ts

import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
import axios from 'axios';

export async function refreshIntegrationTokens() {
  const pendings = await prisma.integrationSetting.findMany({
    where: { status: 'connected' }
  });

  for (const setting of pendings) {
    try {
      const { provider, config } = setting;
      // e.g. Google
      if (provider === 'gmail') {
        const resp = await axios.post('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', {
          grant_type: 'refresh_token',
          client_id: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
          client_secret: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET,
          refresh_token: config.refresh_token
        });
        await prisma.integrationSetting.update({
          where: { id: setting.id },
          data: { config: { ...config, access_token: resp.data.access_token } }
        });
      }
      // …other providers
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Refresh failed for', setting.provider, e);
      await prisma.integrationSetting.update({
        where: { id: setting.id },
        data: { status: 'error' }
      });
    }
  }
}
Schedule via Cron daily.

26.5.2 Provider Step
// app/launchpad/[provider]/connect.tsx
'use client';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function ProviderConnect({ params }) {
  const { provider } = params;
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'pending'|'connected'|'error'>('pending');
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/integrations/list`)
      .then(r=>r.json())
      .then(data => {
        const s = data.integrations.find((i:any)=>i.provider===provider)?.status || 'pending';
        setStatus(s);
      });
  }, [provider]);

  if (status === 'connected') {
    return (
      <div className="text-center">
        <h2>{provider} Connected ✅</h2>
        <button onClick={()=>router.push('/launchpad/summary')} className="btn-primary">Next</button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="text-center space-y-4">
      <h2>Connect {provider}</h2>
      <a href={`/api/onboarding/${provider}/connect`} className="btn-outline">Start OAuth</a>
      {status==='error' && <p className="text-red-500">Connection failed. Try again.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

27.1 Database Schema Changes

// schema.prisma
