27.2 NextAuth Configuration

// pages/api/auth/[...nextauth].ts

import NextAuth from "next-auth";
import GoogleProvider from "next-auth/providers/google";
import EmailProvider  from "next-auth/providers/email";
import CredentialsProvider from "next-auth/providers/credentials";
import { PrismaAdapter } from "@next-auth/prisma-adapter";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyTotp, generateTotpSecret } from "@/lib/totp";
import { signInWithEthereum } from "next-auth/providers/ethereum";

export default NextAuth({
  adapter: PrismaAdapter(prisma),
  session: { strategy: "jwt" },
  providers: [
    GoogleProvider({
      clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_ID!,
      clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_SECRET!,
    }),
    EmailProvider({
      server: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER!,
      from: process.env.EMAIL_FROM!,
    }),
    CredentialsProvider({
      id: "phone-otp",
      name: "Phone OTP",
      credentials: {
        phone: { label: "Phone", type: "text" },
        code:  { label: "OTP",   type: "text" },
      },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { phoneNumber: credentials!.phone }});
        if (user && await verifySmsOtp(user.phoneNumber!, credentials!.code!)) {
          return user;
        }
        return null;
      }
    }),
    CredentialsProvider({
      id: "totp",
      name: "TOTP",
      credentials: { code: { label: "2FA Code", type: "text" } },
      async authorize(credentials, req) {
        const session = req.body; 
        const email = session.user?.email;
        if (!email) return null;
        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
        if (user && user.twoFactorEnabled && await verifyTotp(user.twoFactorSecret!, credentials!.code!)) {
          return user;
        }
        return null;
      }
    }),
    signInWithEthereum({
      clientId: process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL!, 
      // auto-configures SIWE flow
    }),
  ],
  callbacks: {
    async jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) token.id = user.id;
      return token;
    },
    async session({ session, token }) {
      session.user!.id = token.id as string;
      return session;
    },
    // Enforce 2FA: after OAuth/Email/Phone login, if user.twoFactorEnabled must redirect to TOTP step
    async redirect({ url, baseUrl }) {
      // if token needs 2FA, push to /auth/totp
      // implement custom logic here
      return url;
    }
  }
});
27.3 TOTP Utility & SMS OTP

27.3.1 TOTP (using speakeasy)
// lib/totp.ts
import speakeasy from 'speakeasy';

export function generateTotpSecret() {
  const secret = speakeasy.generateSecret({ length: 20 });
  return { base32: secret.base32, otpauth_url: secret.otpauth_url };
}

export function verifyTotp(secret: string, token: string) {
  return speakeasy.totp.verify({ secret, encoding: 'base32', token });
}
27.3.2 SMS OTP (using twilio)
// lib/sms.ts
import Twilio from 'twilio';
const client = new Twilio(process.env.TWILIO_SID, process.env.TWILIO_TOKEN);

const otpStore: Record<string,string> = {}; // replace with Redis in prod

export async function sendSmsOtp(phone: string) {
  const code = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random()*900000).toString();
  otpStore[phone] = code;
  await client.messages.create({
    to: phone,
    from: process.env.TWILIO_FROM!,
    body: `Your Harmony OTP is ${code}`
  });
}

export function verifySmsOtp(phone: string, code: string) {
  return otpStore[phone] === code;
}
27.3.3 API Routes
// pages/api/auth/otp/send.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { sendSmsOtp } from '@/lib/sms';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const { phone } = req.body as { phone: string };
  await sendSmsOtp(phone);
  res.json({ success: true });
};
export default handler;
// pages/api/auth/otp/enable-totp.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
import { generateTotpSecret } from '@/lib/totp';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const { base32, otpauth_url } = generateTotpSecret();
  await prisma.user.update({
    where:{ id: session.user.id },
    data:{ twoFactorSecret: base32 }
  });
  res.json({ otpauth_url });
};
export default handler;
// pages/api/auth/otp/confirm-totp.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
import { verifyTotp } from '@/lib/totp';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const { token } = req.body as { token: string };
  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: session.user.id } });
  if (user && user.twoFactorSecret && verifyTotp(user.twoFactorSecret, token)) {
    await prisma.user.update({ where:{ id: user.id }, data:{ twoFactorEnabled: true }});
    return res.json({ success: true });
  }
  res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid code' });
};
export default handler;
27.4 Front-End Components

27.4.1 Settings UI for 2FA
// components/Settings/TwoFactorSettings.tsx
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import QRCode from 'qrcode.react';

export default function TwoFactorSettings() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'init'|'qr'|'confirm'|'done'>('init');
  const [qrUrl, setQrUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');

  async function initTOTP() {
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/otp/enable-totp', { method:'POST' });
    const { otpauth_url } = await res.json();
    setQrUrl(otpauth_url);
    setStep('qr');
  }
  async function confirm() {
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/otp/confirm-totp', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({ token:code })
    });
    if (res.ok) setStep('done');
    else alert('Invalid code');
  }

  if (step==='init') {
    return <button onClick={initTOTP}>Enable 2FA</button>;
  }
  if (step==='qr') {
    return (
      <div>
        <QRCode value={qrUrl} />
        <p>Scan with Authenticator app, then enter code:</p>
        <input value={code} onChange={e=>setCode(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={confirm}>Confirm</button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (step==='done') {
    return <p>Two-factor authentication enabled ✅</p>;
  }
  return null;
}
27.4.2 Phone OTP Login
// app/auth/phone.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { signIn } from 'next-auth/react';

export default function PhoneLogin() {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(''); 
  const [code, setCode]   = useState('');
  const [step, setStep]   = useState<'send'|'verify'>('send');

  async function send() {
    await fetch('/api/auth/otp/send', { method:'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }), headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} });
    setStep('verify');
  }
  async function verify() {
    await signIn('phone-otp', { phone, code, callbackUrl: '/dashboard' });
  }

  return step==='send' ? (
    <>
      <input placeholder="Phone #" onChange={e=>setPhone(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={send}>Send OTP</button>
    </>
  ) : (
    <>
      <input placeholder="Enter OTP" onChange={e=>setCode(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={verify}>Verify & Sign In</button>
    </>
  );
}
27.4.3 Crypto-Wallet Login
// components/Auth/WalletLogin.tsx
'use client';
import { signIn } from 'next-auth/react';
import { useAccount, useSignMessage } from 'wagmi';

export default function WalletLogin() {
  const { address } = useAccount();
  const { signMessageAsync } = useSignMessage();

  const login = async () => {
    const message = `Sign in to Harmony at ${new Date().toISOString()}`;
    const signature = await signMessageAsync({ message });
    await signIn('ethereum', { message, signature, redirect: false });
    window.location.href = '/dashboard';
  };

  return <button onClick={login}>Connect Wallet</button>;
}

Below is Step 28 – Master Learning Orchestrator & Governance Panel, copy-paste ready for your devs.

28.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Stores aggregated “master” learning from all users’ agents
model MasterMemory {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  agentId       String    // e.g. "CashflowAgent"
  userId        String?   // null if global feed
  context       Json      // embeddings / raw data
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())
}

/// User proposals for new features / agent updates
model Suggestion {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String    @index
  title         String
  description   String
  agentId       String?   // optional, if suggestion targets one agent
  status        String    @default("pending")  // "pending","approved","rejected"
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime  @updatedAt
}

/// Admin & community votes on suggestions
model SuggestionVote {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  suggestionId  String    @index
  userId        String
  vote          Int       // +1 or -1
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())

  suggestion    Suggestion @relation(fields:[suggestionId], references:[id])
}

/// Admin veto overrides
model Veto {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  suggestionId  String    @unique
  adminId       String
  reason        String
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())
}
28.2 Ingesting Agent Logs into MasterMemory

28.2.1 API Route
// pages/api/master-memory/ingest.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session || session.user.role !== 'admin') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
  }

  const { agentId, entries } = req.body as {
    agentId: string;
    entries: Array<{ userId?: string; context: any }>;
  };

  // Bulk write
  const writes = entries.map(e => prisma.masterMemory.create({
    data: { agentId, userId: e.userId || null, context: e.context }
  }));
  await Promise.all(writes);

  res.json({ ok: true, ingested: entries.length });
};

export default handler;
Trigger this after each agent run: fan-out each user’s context to both per-user memory and a global feed.
28.3 Periodic Embedding & Pruning Worker

// lib/masterMemory/processQueue.ts

import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
import { upsertEmbedding } from '@/lib/embeddings';

export async function processMasterMemory() {
  // Fetch all new entries
  const entries = await prisma.masterMemory.findMany({ where: { /* add a flag if needed */ } });
  for (const entry of entries) {
    // transform context → embedding & store in vector DB
    await upsertEmbedding({ id: entry.id, vector: entry.context });
    // Optionally mark as processed
  }
}
Schedule via cron hourly.
28.4 Governance API

28.4.1 Propose
// pages/api/suggestions/create.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { title, description, agentId } = req.body;
  const suggestion = await prisma.suggestion.create({
    data: { userId: session.user.id, title, description, agentId }
  });
  res.json(suggestion);
};

export default handler;
28.4.2 Vote
// pages/api/suggestions/vote.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { suggestionId, vote } = req.body as { suggestionId: string; vote: 1 | -1 };
  const existing = await prisma.suggestionVote.findUnique({
    where: { suggestionId_userId: { suggestionId, userId: session.user.id } }
  });
  if (existing) {
    // update
    await prisma.suggestionVote.update({
      where: { id: existing.id },
      data: { vote }
    });
  } else {
    // create
    await prisma.suggestionVote.create({
      data: { suggestionId, userId: session.user.id, vote }
    });
  }
  res.json({ ok: true });
};

export default handler;
28.4.3 Admin Veto
// pages/api/suggestions/veto.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession } from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const sess = await getSession({ req });
  if (!sess || !['admin','founder'].includes(sess.user.role!)) {
    return res.status(403).end();
  }
  const { suggestionId, reason } = req.body;
  await prisma.veto.create({ data: { suggestionId, adminId: sess.user.id, reason } });
  await prisma.suggestion.update({ where:{ id: suggestionId }, data:{ status:'rejected' } });
  res.json({ ok: true });
};

export default handler;

28.5.1 SuggestionCard & Voting
// components/SuggestionCard.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { signIn, useSession } from 'next-auth/react';

export default function SuggestionCard({ sug }) {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [score, setScore] = useState(sug.upvotes - sug.downvotes);

  const vote = async (v: 1 | -1) => {
    if (!session) return signIn();
    await fetch('/api/suggestions/vote', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ suggestionId: sug.id, vote: v })
    });
    setScore(score + v);
  };

  return (
    <div className="card">
      <h3>{sug.title}</h3>
      <p>{sug.description}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button onClick={() => vote(1)}>👍</button>
        <button onClick={() => vote(-1)}>👎</button>
        <span>{score}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
28.5.2 Veto Form (Admin Only)
// components/VetoForm.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react';

export default function VetoForm({ suggestionId }) {
  const { data: sess } = useSession();
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!sess || !['admin','founder'].includes(sess.user.role!)) return;
    await fetch('/api/suggestions/veto', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ suggestionId, reason })
    });
    alert('Veto submitted');
  };

  if (!sess || !['admin','founder'].includes(sess.user.role!)) return null;
  return (
    <div className="p-4 bg-red-50">
      <h4>Veto Suggestion</h4>
      <textarea value={reason} onChange={e=>setReason(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={submit} className="btn-red">Submit Veto</button>
    </div>
  );
}

– POST /api/master-memory/ingest accepts bulk entries only by admin.
Embedding Worker
– Periodic job upserts embeddings of all MasterMemory to vector DB.
Suggestion Flow
– Any authenticated user can POST /api/suggestions/create.
Voting
– Users can up/down-vote once per suggestion; real-time score updates.
Admin Veto
– Admins/founders can veto; veto marks status = "rejected".
Governance UI
– Lists suggestions, voting buttons, admin veto forms where applicable.
Data Integrity
– Suggestion statuses update automatically when enough votes or veto.
Tests
– Unit tests for all APIs; mock sessions & roles.

29.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// A workspace or “team” owned by a user
model Team {
  id           String       @id @default(cuid())
  name         String
  ownerId      String       @index
  createdAt    DateTime     @default(now())

  owner        User         @relation(fields:[ownerId], references:[id])
  members      TeamMember[]
}

/// Each user’s membership in a team, with role
model TeamMember {
  id           String       @id @default(cuid())
  teamId       String       @index
  userId       String       @index
  role         String       @default("member") // "member"|"admin"
  joinedAt     DateTime     @default(now())

  team         Team         @relation(fields:[teamId], references:[id])
  user         User         @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id])
  permissions  TeamAgentAccess[]
}

/// Which agents a team member can use
model TeamAgentAccess {
  id            String      @id @default(cuid())
  memberId      String      @index
  agentId       String      // e.g. "CashflowAgent"
  canUse        Boolean     @default(true)

  member        TeamMember  @relation(fields:[memberId], references:[id])
}
29.2 API Routes

29.2.1 Create Team
// pages/api/team/create.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { name } = req.body as { name: string };
  const team = await prisma.team.create({
    data: {
      name,
      ownerId: session.user.id,
      members: {
        create: [{ userId: session.user.id, role: 'admin' }]
      }
    }
  });
  res.json(team);
};

export default handler;
29.2.2 Invite Member
// pages/api/team/[teamId]/invite.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { teamId } = req.query as { teamId: string };
  const { email, role } = req.body as { email: string; role: 'member' | 'admin' };

  // Only owner or admin can invite
  const membership = await prisma.teamMember.findFirst({
    where: { teamId, userId: session.user.id, role: 'admin' }
  });
  if (!membership) return res.status(403).end();

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
  if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });

  const member = await prisma.teamMember.create({
    data: { teamId, userId: user.id, role }
  });
  res.json(member);
};

export default handler;
29.2.3 List Team & Members
// pages/api/team/[teamId]/members.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { teamId } = req.query as { teamId: string };
  // Ensure session.user is a member
  const me = await prisma.teamMember.findFirst({
    where: { teamId, userId: session.user.id }
  });
  if (!me) return res.status(403).end();

  const members = await prisma.teamMember.findMany({
    where: { teamId },
    include: { user: true, permissions: true }
  });
  res.json(members);
};

export default handler;
29.2.4 Update Member Role
// pages/api/team/[teamId]/member/[memberId]/role.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { teamId, memberId } = req.query as { teamId: string; memberId: string };
  const { role } = req.body as { role: 'member' | 'admin' };

  // Only team admin can change roles
  const me = await prisma.teamMember.findFirst({
    where: { teamId, userId: session.user.id, role: 'admin' }
  });
  if (!me) return res.status(403).end();

  const updated = await prisma.teamMember.update({
    where: { id: memberId },
    data: { role }
  });
  res.json(updated);
};

export default handler;
29.2.5 Assign Agent Access
// pages/api/team/[teamId]/member/[memberId]/access.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { teamId, memberId } = req.query as { teamId: string; memberId: string };
  const { agentId, canUse } = req.body as { agentId: string; canUse: boolean };

  // Only team admin
  const me = await prisma.teamMember.findFirst({
    where: { teamId, userId: session.user.id, role: 'admin' }
  });
  if (!me) return res.status(403).end();

  const access = await prisma.teamAgentAccess.upsert({
    where: { memberId_agentId: { memberId, agentId } },
    create: { memberId, agentId, canUse },
    update: { canUse }
  });
  res.json(access);
};

export default handler;
29.2.6 Remove Member
// pages/api/team/[teamId]/member/[memberId]/remove.ts

import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }           from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { teamId, memberId } = req.query as { teamId: string; memberId: string };

  const me = await prisma.teamMember.findFirst({
    where: { teamId, userId: session.user.id, role: 'admin' }
  });
  if (!me) return res.status(403).end();

  await prisma.teamMember.delete({ where: { id: memberId } });
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;

// components/Team/TeamManager.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import InviteForm      from './InviteForm';
import MemberCard      from './MemberCard';
import AgentAccessPanel from './AgentAccessPanel';

type Member = {
  id: string;
  role: string;
  user: { id: string; name: string; email: string };
  permissions: { agentId: string; canUse: boolean }[];
};

export default function TeamManager({ teamId }: { teamId: string }) {
  const [members, setMembers] = useState<Member[]>([]);

  async function loadMembers() {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/team/${teamId}/members`);
    setMembers(await res.json());
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadMembers(); }, []);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Team & Permissions</h2>
      <InviteForm teamId={teamId} onInvited={loadMembers} />
      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {members.map(m => (
          <MemberCard key={m.id} member={m} onChange={loadMembers} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
29.3.2 InviteForm.tsx
// components/Team/InviteForm.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function InviteForm({
  teamId,
  onInvited
}: {
  teamId: string;
  onInvited: () => void;
}) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole]   = useState<'member'|'admin'>('member');

  async function invite() {
    await fetch(`/api/team/${teamId}/invite`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, role })
    });
    setEmail('');
    onInvited();
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded">
      <h3 className="font-semibold">Invite a Member</h3>
      <input
        type="email" placeholder="Email" value={email}
        onChange={e=>setEmail(e.target.value)}
        className="border p-2 w-full rounded mb-2"
      />
      <select
        value={role}
        onChange={e=>setRole(e.target.value as any)}
        className="border p-2 rounded mb-2"
      >
        <option value="member">Member</option>
        <option value="admin">Admin</option>
      </select>
      <button onClick={invite} className="btn-primary w-full">Invite</button>
    </div>
  );
}
29.3.3 MemberCard.tsx
// components/Team/MemberCard.tsx
'use client';
import AgentAccessPanel from './AgentAccessPanel';

export default function MemberCard({
  member,
  onChange
}: {
  member: any;
  onChange: () => void;
}) {
  const changeRole = async (newRole: string) => {
    await fetch(
      `/api/team/${member.teamId}/member/${member.id}/role`,
      { method: 'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({ role: newRole }) }
    );
    onChange();
  };

  const remove = async () => {
    await fetch(
      `/api/team/${member.teamId}/member/${member.id}/remove`,
      { method: 'POST' }
    );
    onChange();
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 border rounded">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <div>
          <p className="font-semibold">{member.user.name}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{member.user.email}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <select
            value={member.role}
            onChange={e=>changeRole(e.target.value)}
            className="border p-1 rounded"
          >
            <option value="member">Member</option>
            <option value="admin">Admin</option>
          </select>
          <button onClick={remove} className="ml-2 text-red-500">Remove</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <AgentAccessPanel member={member} onChange={onChange} />
    </div>
  );
}
29.3.4 AgentAccessPanel.tsx
// components/Team/AgentAccessPanel.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function AgentAccessPanel({
  member,
  onChange
}: {
  member: { id: string; permissions: { agentId: string; canUse: boolean }[] };
  onChange: () => void;
}) {
  const [perms, setPerms] = useState(member.permissions);

  const toggle = async (agentId: string, canUse: boolean) => {
    await fetch(
      `/api/team/${member.teamId}/member/${member.id}/access`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ agentId, canUse: !canUse })
      }
    );
    onChange();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <h4 className="text-sm font-medium">Agent Access</h4>
      {perms.map(p => (
        <div key={p.agentId} className="flex items-center">
          <label className="flex-1">{p.agentId}</label>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={p.canUse}
            onChange={()=>toggle(p.agentId, p.canUse)}
          />
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

Below is Step 30 – Time‐Save ROI Calculator, fully specified and copy/paste‐ready:

30.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Stores per‐agent default time‐savings and per‐user overrides
model AgentTimeConfig {
  id             String   @id @default(cuid())
  agentId        String   @unique
  defaultMinutes Int      // e.g. 120 (minutes saved per week)
  hourlyRate     Float    // e.g. 75.00 USD/hour
}

/// Logs each user's ROI calculation events
model R oiCalculation {
  id             String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String    @index
  agentIds       String[]  // ["CashflowAgent","IrisAgent",…]
  totalMinutes   Int       // sum of minutes saved
  totalValue     Float     // computed USD savings
  createdAt      DateTime  @default(now())
}
30.2 API Routes

30.2.1 Fetch Agent Time Configs
// pages/api/roi/agent-configs.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const configs = await prisma.agentTimeConfig.findMany();
  res.json(configs);
};

export default handler;
30.2.2 Calculate & Log ROI
// pages/api/roi/calculate.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';

interface Body {
  agentIds: string[];
  userHourlyRate?: number; // optional override
}

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { agentIds, userHourlyRate } = req.body as Body;
  // Fetch configs
  const configs = await prisma.agentTimeConfig.findMany({
    where: { agentId: { in: agentIds } }
  });
  // Sum minutes and compute value
  const totalMinutes = configs.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.defaultMinutes, 0);
  // use override if provided, else average of configs
  const rate = userHourlyRate ?? (
    configs.reduce((s, c) => s + c.hourlyRate, 0) / configs.length
  );
  const totalValue = (totalMinutes / 60) * rate;

  // Log it
  await prisma.roiCalculation.create({
    data: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      agentIds,
      totalMinutes,
      totalValue
    }
  });

  res.json({ totalMinutes, totalValue });
};

export default handler;
30.3 Front-End Components

30.3.1 ROI Form & Results Display
// components/ROI/ROICalculator.tsx
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

type Config = { agentId: string; defaultMinutes: number; hourlyRate: number };

export default function ROICalculator() {
  const [configs, setConfigs] = useState<Config[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [overrideRate, setOverrideRate] = useState<number>();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ totalMinutes: number; totalValue: number }>();

  // load configs
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/roi/agent-configs')
      .then(r=>r.json())
      .then(setConfigs);
  }, []);

  const toggleAgent = (agentId: string) => {
    setSelected(sel =>
      sel.includes(agentId)
        ? sel.filter(a => a !== agentId)
        : [...sel, agentId]
    );
  };

  const calculate = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/roi/calculate', {
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ agentIds: selected, userHourlyRate: overrideRate })
    });
    setResult(await res.json());
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg shadow">
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Time-Save ROI Calculator</h3>
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="font-medium">Select Agents:</label>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-2">
          {configs.map(c => (
            <button
              key={c.agentId}
              onClick={()=>toggleAgent(c.agentId)}
              className={`p-2 border rounded ${
                selected.includes(c.agentId) ? 'bg-green-200' : ''
              }`}
            >
              {c.agentId} (saves {c.defaultMinutes} min)
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="font-medium">Hourly Rate Override (USD):</label>
        <input
          type="number"
          value={overrideRate ?? ''}
          onChange={e=>setOverrideRate(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
          className="border p-2 rounded w-32 ml-2"
        />
      </div>
      <button
        onClick={calculate}
        className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
        disabled={selected.length === 0}
      >
        Calculate ROI
      </button>

      {result && (
        <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-white border rounded">
          <p>Total Time Saved: <strong>{result.totalMinutes}</strong> minutes</p>
          <p>Equivalent Value: <strong>${result.totalValue.toFixed(2)}</strong> USD</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

31.1 HTTP Hardening & Middleware

31.1.1 Install Dependencies
npm install helmet cors express-rate-limit xss-clean
31.1.2 Configure in Next.js Custom Server
// server.js (if using a custom Express server)

import express from 'express';
import helmet from 'helmet';
import cors from 'cors';
import xss from 'xss-clean';
import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';
import next from 'next';

const dev = process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production';
const app = next({ dev });
const handle = app.getRequestHandler();

// global rate limiter: max 100 requests per 15min per IP
const globalLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 100,
  message: 'Too many requests from this IP, please try again later.'
});

app.prepare().then(() => {
  const server = express();

  // 1. Security headers
  server.use(helmet());

  // 2. CORS (adjust origin to your domains)
  server.use(cors({ origin: ['https://yourdomain.com'], credentials: true }));

  // 3. XSS protection
  server.use(xss());

  // 4. Rate limiting
  server.use(globalLimiter);

  // 5. JSON parsing
  server.use(express.json());

  // Next.js handlers
  server.all('*', (req, res) => handle(req, res));

  server.listen(3000, () => {
    console.log('> Ready on http://localhost:3000');
  });
});
If you’re not using a custom server, wrap your API routes with a rate-limit helper (see 31.2).
31.2 Rate-Limit Middleware for API Routes

// lib/middleware/rateLimit.ts
import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';
import slowDown from 'express-slow-down';
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next';

// Slow-down: after 20 requests, delay 1s per request
const speedLimiter = slowDown({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000, // 1 minute
  delayAfter: 20,
  delayMs: 1000
});

// Hard limit: 100 requests per 15 minutes
const ipLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 100,
  handler: (_req, res) => res.status(429).json({ error: 'Too many requests' })
});

// Helper to apply both
export function withRateLimit(
  handler: (req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) => void
) {
  return async (req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) => {
    await speedLimiter(req as any, res as any, () => {});
    await ipLimiter(req as any, res as any, () => {});
    return handler(req, res);
  };
}
Usage in any API route:

// pages/api/some-secure-route.ts
import { withRateLimit } from '@/lib/middleware/rateLimit';

const handler = async (req, res) => {
  // your logic
  res.json({ ok: true });
};

export default withRateLimit(handler);
31.3 Prompt-Injection Sanitization

31.3.1 XSS & Control-Char Cleaner
// lib/security/sanitizePrompt.ts

/**
 * Remove unprintable/control characters and HTML tags from user inputs.
 */
export function sanitizePrompt(input: string): string {
  // strip HTML
  const noHTML = input.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
  // remove control chars except newline/tab
  return noHTML.replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, '');
}
Apply before sending to LLM:

import { sanitizePrompt } from '@/lib/security/sanitizePrompt';

const safePrompt = sanitizePrompt(userPrompt);
const response = await callLLM(safePrompt, history);
31.4 Audit & Activity Logging

31.4.1 Prisma Model
model AuditLog {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String?
  route      String
  method     String
  ip         String
  statusCode Int
  timestamp  DateTime @default(now())
}
31.4.2 Logging Middleware
// lib/middleware/auditLog.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export function withAuditLog(
  handler: (req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) => void
) {
  return async (req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) => {
    const start = Date.now();
    await handler(req, res);
    await prisma.auditLog.create({
      data: {
        userId: (req as any).session?.user?.id,
        route: req.url!,
        method: req.method!,
        ip: req.headers['x-forwarded-for'] as string || req.socket.remoteAddress!,
        statusCode: res.statusCode,
      }
    });
  };
}
Compose with rate limit & audit:

export default withRateLimit(withAuditLog(async (req, res) => {
  // your handler
}));
31.5 Automated Security Scans

31.5.1 OWASP ZAP Baseline Scan
Create a script in package.json:

// package.json
{
  "scripts": {
    "zap-scan": "zap-baseline.py -t http://localhost:3000 -r zap_report.html"
  }
}
Install ZAP CLI:
pip install python-owasp-zap-v2.4
Run scan (dev server must be running):
npm run zap-scan
Review zap_report.html for critical/high issues; remediate before production.
31.6 Content Security Policy (CSP)

// next.config.js
module.exports = {
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: "/(.*)",
        headers: [
          {
            key: "Content-Security-Policy",
            value: [
              "default-src 'self'",
              "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
              "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
              "img-src 'self' data:",
              "font-src 'self' data:",
              "connect-src 'self' https://api.openai.com https://your-supabase.io",
            ].join('; ')
          }
        ]
      }
    ];
  }
};

32.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// A demo/tutorial unit (video, interactive, forum prompt)
model DemoItem {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  slug        String    @unique                   // e.g. "invoice-demo"
  title       String
  description String
  type        String    // "video" | "interactive" | "forum"
  contentUrl  String?   // video URL or embed link
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt
}

/// Tracks user progress on interactive tutorials
model DemoProgress {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String   @index
  demoItemId String   @index
  completed  Boolean  @default(false)
  updatedAt  DateTime @default(now()) @updatedAt

  user       User     @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id])
  demoItem   DemoItem @relation(fields:[demoItemId], references:[id])
}

/// Forum posts/questions tied to a demo
model DemoForumPost {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  demoItemId  String   @index
  authorId    String
  content     String
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  demoItem    DemoItem @relation(fields:[demoItemId], references:[id])
  author      User     @relation(fields:[authorId], references:[id])
}
32.2 API Routes

32.2.1 List Demo Items
// pages/api/demo/items.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const items = await prisma.demoItem.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' } });
  res.json(items);
};

export default handler;
32.2.2 Get Single Demo Item & Progress
// pages/api/demo/[slug].ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { slug } = req.query as { slug: string };
  const item = await prisma.demoItem.findUnique({ where: { slug } });
  if (!item) return res.status(404).end();

  const session = await getSession({ req });
  let progress = null;
  if (session) {
    progress = await prisma.demoProgress.findUnique({
      where: { userId_demoItemId: { userId: session.user.id, demoItemId: item.id } }
    });
  }

  res.json({ item, progress });
};

export default handler;
32.2.3 Mark Tutorial Complete
// pages/api/demo/[slug]/complete.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { slug } = req.query as { slug: string };
  const item = await prisma.demoItem.findUnique({ where: { slug } });
  if (!item) return res.status(404).end();

  const record = await prisma.demoProgress.upsert({
    where: { userId_demoItemId: { userId: session.user.id, demoItemId: item.id } },
    create: { userId: session.user.id, demoItemId: item.id, completed: true },
    update: { completed: true }
  });
  res.json(record);
};

export default handler;
32.2.4 Forum – List & Post
// pages/api/demo/[slug]/posts.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

export const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { slug } = req.query as { slug: string };
  const item = await prisma.demoItem.findUnique({ where: { slug } });
  if (!item) return res.status(404).end();

  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    const posts = await prisma.demoForumPost.findMany({
      where: { demoItemId: item.id },
      include: { author: { select: { id: true, name: true } } },
      orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' }
    });
    res.json(posts);

  } else if (req.method === 'POST') {
    const session = await getSession({ req });
    if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
    const { content } = req.body as { content: string };
    const post = await prisma.demoForumPost.create({
      data: {
        demoItemId: item.id,
        authorId: session.user.id,
        content
      },
      include: { author: { select: { id: true, name: true } } }
    });
    res.status(201).json(post);
  } else {
    res.status(405).end();
  }
};

export default handler;

// components/Demo/DemoItemPage.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import VideoPlayer      from './VideoPlayer';
import InteractiveConsole from './InteractiveConsole';
import DemoForum        from './DemoForum';

export default function DemoItemPage({ item, progress }: any) {
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(progress?.completed);

  const markComplete = async () => {
    await fetch(`/api/demo/${item.slug}/complete`, { method: 'POST' });
    setCompleted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{item.title}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-700">{item.description}</p>

      {item.type === 'video' && <VideoPlayer src={item.contentUrl} />}

      {item.type === 'interactive' && (
        <InteractiveConsole demoSlug={item.slug} />
      )}

      {!completed && (
        <button
          onClick={markComplete}
          className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
        >
          Mark as Complete
        </button>
      )}
      {completed && <span className="text-green-700">✔ Completed</span>}

      {/* Forum */}
      <DemoForum demoSlug={item.slug} />
    </div>
  );
}

32.3.4 InteractiveConsole.tsx
// components/Demo/InteractiveConsole.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function InteractiveConsole({ demoSlug }: { demoSlug: string }) {
  const [log, setLog] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');

  const runCommand = () => {
    // stub: echo back input
    setLog((l) => [...l, `> ${input}`, `Response: [simulated]`]);
    setInput('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-gray-900 text-green-200 p-4 rounded">
      <div className="h-48 overflow-y-auto mb-2">
        {log.map((line, i) => (
          <pre key={i}>{line}</pre>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          value={input}
          onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)}
          className="flex-1 bg-gray-800 p-2 rounded"
          placeholder="Type command…"
        />
        <button onClick={runCommand} className="bg-indigo-600 px-3 rounded">
          ▶
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
32.3.5 DemoForum.tsx
// components/Demo/DemoForum.tsx
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function DemoForum({ demoSlug }: { demoSlug: string }) {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [content, setContent] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/demo/${demoSlug}/posts`).then(r=>r.json()).then(setPosts);
  }, [demoSlug]);

  const submit = async () => {
    await fetch(`/api/demo/${demoSlug}/posts`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ content })
    });
    setContent('');
    const updated = await fetch(`/api/demo/${demoSlug}/posts`).then(r=>r.json());
    setPosts(updated);
  };

  return (
    <div className="mt-8">
      <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Discussion</h3>
      <div className="space-y-2 mb-4">
        {posts.map(p => (
          <div key={p.id} className="p-2 border rounded">
            <p className="text-sm"><strong>{p.author.name}</strong> says:</p>
            <p>{p.content}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{new Date(p.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <textarea
        value={content}
        onChange={e => setContent(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full border p-2 rounded mb-2"
        placeholder="Ask a question…"
      />
      <button onClick={submit} className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded">
        Post
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

33.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// FAQ categories (group similar questions)
model FAQCategory {
  id        String @id @default(cuid())
  name      String @unique   // e.g. "General", "Billing", "Security"
  order     Int    @default(0)
}

/// Individual FAQ entries
model FAQItem {
  id          String       @id @default(cuid())
  categoryId  String
  question    String
  answer      String       // Markdown or HTML
  order       Int          @default(0)
  createdAt   DateTime     @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime     @updatedAt

  category    FAQCategory  @relation(fields:[categoryId], references:[id])
}
33.2 API Routes

// pages/api/faq/index.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const categories = await prisma.fAQCategory.findMany({
    orderBy: { order: 'asc' },
    include: {
      items: { orderBy: { order: 'asc' } }
    }
  });
  res.json(categories);

33.2.2 Get Single FAQ
// pages/api/faq/[id].ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { prisma }          from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.query as { id: string };
  const item = await prisma.fAQItem.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: { category: true }
  });
  if (!item) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });
  res.json(item);
};

export default handler;
33.2.3 Admin: Create / Update / Delete
// pages/api/faq/admin.ts
import { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }     from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }         from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session?.user?.role?.includes('admin')) {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
  }

  const { method } = req;
  if (method === 'POST') {
    const { categoryId, question, answer, order } = req.body;
    const item = await prisma.fAQItem.create({ data: { categoryId, question, answer, order } });
    return res.status(201).json(item);
  }
  if (method === 'PUT') {
    const { id, question, answer, order, categoryId } = req.body;
    const item = await prisma.fAQItem.update({ where: { id }, data: { question, answer, order, categoryId } });
    return res.json(item);
  }
  if (method === 'DELETE') {
    const { id } = req.body;
    await prisma.fAQItem.delete({ where: { id } });
    return res.status(204).end();
  }
  res.setHeader('Allow', ['POST','PUT','DELETE']);
  res.status(405).end();
};

export default handler;

'use client';

type FAQItem = { id: string; question: string; answer: string };
type FAQCategory = { id: string; name: string; items: FAQItem[] };

export default function FAQPage() {
  const [cats, setCats] = useState<FAQCategory[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/faq').then(r=>r.json()).then(setCats);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto p-6 space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Frequently Asked Questions</h1>
      {cats.map(cat => (
        <section key={cat.id}>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mb-4">{cat.name}</h2>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {cat.items.map(item => (
              <details key={item.id} className="border rounded p-3">
                <summary className="cursor-pointer font-medium">{item.question}</summary>
                <div className="mt-2 prose" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: item.answer }} />
              </details>

33.3.2 FAQEditor.tsx (Admin)
// components/FAQ/Admin/FAQEditor.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

type Category = { id: string; name: string };
type FAQItem   = { id: string; question: string; answer: string; order: number; categoryId: string };

export default function FAQEditor() {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [items, setItems]           = useState<FAQItem[]>([]);
  const [form, setForm]             = useState<Partial<FAQItem>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/faq').then(r=>r.json()).then(data => {
      setCategories(data.map((c: any)=>({ id:c.id, name:c.name })));
      setItems(data.flatMap((c: any)=>c.items));
    });
  }, []);

  const save = async () => {
    const method = form.id ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
    await fetch('/api/faq/admin', {
      method, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify(form)
    });
    // refresh
    const data = await fetch('/api/faq').then(r=>r.json());
    setItems(data.flatMap((c: any)=>c.items));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Edit FAQs</h2>

      <select
        value={form.categoryId||''}
        onChange={e=>setForm(f=>({ ...f, categoryId:e.target.value }))}
      >
        <option value="">Select Category</option>
        {categories.map(c=>(
          <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <input
        placeholder="Question"
        value={form.question||''}
        onChange={e=>setForm(f=>({ ...f, question:e.target.value }))}
        className="w-full border p-2"
      />

      <textarea
        placeholder="Answer (HTML allowed)"
        value={form.answer||''}
        onChange={e=>setForm(f=>({ ...f, answer:e.target.value }))}
        className="w-full border p-2 h-40"
      />

      <input
        type="number"
        placeholder="Order"
        value={form.order||0}
        onChange={e=>setForm(f=>({ ...f, order:parseInt(e.target.value) }))}
        className="w-24 border p-2"
      />

      <button onClick={save} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded">
        {form.id ? 'Update' : 'Create'}
      </button>

      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mt-6">Existing Items</h3>
      <ul className="space-y-2">
        {items.map(i=>(
          <li key={i.id} className="flex justify-between items-center border p-2 rounded">
            <span>{i.question}</span>
            <button
              onClick={()=>setForm(i)}
              className="text-blue-600 underline"
            >
              Edit
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

      </button>

Below is Step 34 – Onboarding Launchpad & Integration Checklist, fully detailed and copy-paste ready:

34.1 Database Schema (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// What integrations we support
enum IntegrationType {
  GOOGLE
  GOOGLE_CALENDAR
  HUBSPOT
  GOHIGHLEVEL
  STRIPE
  META_FACEBOOK
  X_TWITTER
  LINKEDIN
  SLACK
  TWILIO
  SENDGRID
  GOOGLE_DRIVE
  DROPBOX
  CLICKUP
  NOTION
  ASANA
  MONDAY
}

/// Tracks a user’s connected integrations
model ConnectedIntegration {
  id             String           @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String           @index
  type           IntegrationType
  status         String           // "connected", "error", "pending"
  credentials    Json?            // tokens, webhook URLs, etc.
  connectedAt    DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime         @updatedAt

  user           User             @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id])
}

/// Tracks overall onboarding steps progress
model OnboardingProgress {
  id         String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String    @unique
  step       Int       @default(1)   // 1–N
  updatedAt  DateTime  @updatedAt

  user       User      @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id])
}
34.2 API Routes

34.2.1 Get Launchpad Status
// pages/api/launchpad/status.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const userId = session.user.id;
  const progress = await prisma.onboardingProgress.findUnique({ where: { userId } });
  const integrations = await prisma.connectedIntegration.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    orderBy: { type: 'asc' }
  });

  res.json({ step: progress?.step || 1, integrations });
};

export default handler;
34.2.2 Initiate OAuth / Connect Integration
// pages/api/launchpad/connect/[type].ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { getOAuthUrl }       from '@/lib/integrations/oauth';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { type } = req.query as { type: string };
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  // e.g. getOAuthUrl('GOOGLE') returns authorization URL
  const url = getOAuthUrl(type as any, session.user.id);
  res.json({ url });
};

export default handler;
34.2.3 Save Integration Callback
// pages/api/launchpad/callback/[type].ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { parse }              from 'url';
import { prisma }             from '@/lib/prisma';
import { exchangeOAuthCode }  from '@/lib/integrations/oauth';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { type } = req.query as { type: string };
  const { code, state } = parse(req.url!, true).query as any;
  const userId = state; // we passed userId in state
  const creds  = await exchangeOAuthCode(type as any, code as string);

  await prisma.connectedIntegration.upsert({
    where: { userId_type: { userId, type: type as any } },
    create: { userId, type: type as any, status: 'connected', credentials: creds },
    update: { status: 'connected', credentials: creds }
  });

  // advance onboarding step
  await prisma.onboardingProgress.upsert({
    where: { userId },
    create: { userId, step: 2 },
    update: { step: { increment: 1 } }
  });

  res.redirect('/launchpad'); // back to Launchpad UI
};

export default handler;

// components/Launchpad/LaunchpadPage.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import IntegrationCard from './IntegrationCard';
import ProgressStepper from './ProgressStepper';

type Integration = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
};

export default function LaunchpadPage() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [integrations, setIntegrations] = useState<Integration[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/launchpad/status')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        setStep(data.step);
        setIntegrations(data.integrations);
      });

    'GOOGLE', 'GOOGLE_CALENDAR', 'HUBSPOT', 'GOHIGHLEVEL',
    'STRIPE', 'META_FACEBOOK', 'X_TWITTER', 'LINKEDIN',

    'CLICKUP', 'NOTION', 'ASANA', 'MONDAY'
  ];

// components/Launchpad/OAuthButton.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function OAuthButton({ integration, disabled }: { integration: string; disabled: boolean }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const connect = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/launchpad/connect/${integration}`);
    const { url } = await res.json();
    window.location.href = url;
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={connect}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      className={`w-full px-4 py-2 rounded text-white ${
        disabled ? 'bg-gray-400' : 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700'
      }`}
    >
      {loading ? 'Launching...' : disabled ? 'Connected' : `Connect ${integration.replace('_',' ')}`}
    </button>
  );
}

// lib/integrations/oauth.ts

export function getOAuthUrl(type: string, userId: string): string {
  switch (type) {
    case 'GOOGLE':
      return `https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth?...&state=${userId}`;
    // other cases...
    default:
      throw new Error('Unsupported integration');
  }
}

export async function exchangeOAuthCode(type: string, code: string) {
  // hit the provider’s token endpoint, return JSON credentials
  // e.g., for Google:
  // const tokens = await googleOAuthClient.getToken(code);
  // return tokens;
  return { accessToken: 'x', refreshToken: 'y' };
}

35.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Extend your existing Agent model
model Agent {
  id                 String   @id @default(cuid())
  name               String
  // … other fields …
  defaultTimeSaved   Int      @default(15)   // minutes saved PER use
}

/// Log each agent invocation
model PromptLog {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String   @index
  agentId      String
  prompt       String
  response     String
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  // no change needed if you already have this model
}
35.2 API Route: Compute Usage & Time Saved

// pages/api/roi.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }           from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }               from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const userId = session.user.id;

  // 1) Count uses per agent
  const usage = await prisma.promptLog.groupBy({
    by: ['agentId'],
    where: { userId },
    _count: { _all: true },
  });

  // 2) Fetch defaultTimeSaved per agent
  const agentIds = usage.map(u => u.agentId);
  const agents   = await prisma.agent.findMany({
    where: { id: { in: agentIds } },
    select: { id: true, name: true, defaultTimeSaved: true }
  });

  // 3) Build results
  let totalMinutes = 0;
  const byAgent = usage.map(u => {
    const agent = agents.find(a => a.id === u.agentId)!;
    const minutes = agent.defaultTimeSaved * u._count._all;
    totalMinutes += minutes;
    return {
      agentId: u.agentId,
      name: agent.name,
      uses: u._count._all,
      timeSavedMinutes: minutes
    };
  });

  return res.json({ byAgent, totalMinutes });
};

export default handler;

35.3.1 ROIPage.tsx
// components/ROI/ROIPage.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import ROICard     from './ROICard';
import ROIStats    from './ROIStats';

type AgentUsage = {
  agentId: string;
  name: string;
  uses: number;
  timeSavedMinutes: number;
};

export default function ROIPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{
    byAgent: AgentUsage[];
    totalMinutes: number;
  }>({ byAgent: [], totalMinutes: 0 });

  const [hourlyRate, setHourlyRate] = useState(100); // default

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/roi')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setData);
  }, []);

  const totalHours = data.totalMinutes / 60;
  const roi        = totalHours * hourlyRate;
  const weeklyROI  = (roi / 7) * 7;     // same as total for current period
  const monthlyROI = (roi / 7) * 30;    // projection
  const yearlyROI  = (roi / 7) * 365;   // projection

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto p-6 space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Time-Savings & ROI Calculator</h1>

      <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
        <label className="font-medium">Your Hourly Rate ($):</label>
        <input
          type="number"
          value={hourlyRate}
          onChange={e => setHourlyRate(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
          className="border p-2 rounded w-24"
        />
      </div>

      <ROIStats
        totalMinutes={data.totalMinutes}
        hourlyRate={hourlyRate}
        weeklyROI={weeklyROI}
        monthlyROI={monthlyROI}
        yearlyROI={yearlyROI}
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {data.byAgent.map(agent => (
          <ROICard
            key={agent.agentId}
            name={agent.name}
            uses={agent.uses}
            timeSavedMinutes={agent.timeSavedMinutes}
            hourlyRate={hourlyRate}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
35.3.2 ROIStats.tsx
// components/ROI/ROIStats.tsx
'use client';
import React from 'react';

type Props = {
  totalMinutes: number;
  hourlyRate: number;
  weeklyROI: number;
  monthlyROI: number;
  yearlyROI: number;
};

export default function ROIStats({
  totalMinutes, hourlyRate,
  weeklyROI, monthlyROI, yearlyROI
}: Props) {
  const totalHours = (totalMinutes / 60).toFixed(1);
  const totalROI   = (totalHours * hourlyRate).toFixed(2);

  return (
    <div className="bg-gray-100 p-4 rounded grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-4 text-center">
      <div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">{totalHours}</div>
        <div className="text-sm">Hours Saved</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">${totalROI}</div>
        <div className="text-sm">ROI</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">${weeklyROI.toFixed(2)}</div>
        <div className="text-sm">This Week</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">${monthlyROI.toFixed(2)}</div>
        <div className="text-sm">This Month</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">${yearlyROI.toFixed(2)}</div>
        <div className="text-sm">This Year</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

}

36.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Supported CRMs / KPI sources
enum CRMType {
  HUBSPOT
  GOHIGHLEVEL
  SALESFORCE
  ZOHO
  PIPEDRIVE
}

/// Stores per-user CRM connections
model CRMConnection {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String   @unique
  type         CRMType
  credentials  Json     // OAuth tokens, API keys, etc.
  status       String   // "connected", "error"
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime @updatedAt

  user         User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// Logs of metrics pulled (for history + comparison)
model CRMMetricsLog {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  connectionId String  @index
  pulledAt    DateTime @default(now())
  metrics     Json     // e.g. { leads: 120, deals: 5, pipelineValue: 45000 }
  
  connection  CRMConnection @relation(fields: [connectionId], references: [id])
}
36.2 API Routes

36.2.1 Connect / Update CRM Credentials
// pages/api/crm/connect/[type].ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { exchangeCRMCreds }  from '@/lib/integrations/crm';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { type } = req.query as { type: CRMType };
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const creds = await exchangeCRMCreds(type, req.body.code);
  const conn  = await prisma.cRMConnection.upsert({
    where: { userId: session.user.id },
    create: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      type,
      credentials: creds,
      status: 'connected'
    },
    update: {
      credentials: creds,
      status: 'connected'
    }
  });
  res.json(conn);
};
export default handler;
36.2.2 Pull Latest Metrics
// pages/api/crm/metrics.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { fetchCRMMetrics }   from '@/lib/integrations/crm';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const conn = await prisma.cRMConnection.findUnique({
    where: { userId: session.user.id }
  });
  if (!conn) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No CRM connected' });

  const metrics = await fetchCRMMetrics(conn.type, conn.credentials);
  await prisma.cRMMetricsLog.create({
    data: { connectionId: conn.id, metrics }
  });

  res.json(metrics);
};
export default handler;

// components/CRM/CRMConnectPage.tsx
'use client';

type Metrics      = Record<string, number>;

export default function CRMConnectPage() {
  const [conn, setConn]     = useState<Connection | null>(null);
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<Metrics | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/crm/metrics')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setMetrics);
    fetch('/api/crm/connect/HUBSPOT', { method: 'GET' }) // or a status endpoint
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setConn);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto p-6 space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">CRM & KPI Integrations</h1>
      <IntegrationCard type="HUBSPOT" connection={conn} />
      <IntegrationCard type="GOHIGHLEVEL" connection={conn} />
      {/* repeat for each CRMType */}
      {metrics && <KPIStats data={metrics} />}
    </div>
  );
}
36.3.2 IntegrationCard.tsx
// components/CRM/IntegrationCard.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function IntegrationCard({
  type,
  connection
}: {
  type: string;
  connection: { status: string } | null;
}) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const status = connection?.type === type ? connection.status : 'not connected';

  const connect = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    // redirect to OAuth or API key input flow
    const res = await fetch(`/api/crm/connect/${type}`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ code: '...' }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    // refresh UI
    window.location.reload();
  };

  return (
    <div className="border rounded p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-semibold">{type}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm">Status: <span className={status==='connected'?'text-green-600':'text-gray-600'}>{status}</span></p>
      </div>
      <button
        onClick={connect}
        disabled={loading || status==='connected'}
        className={`px-4 py-2 rounded ${status==='connected'?'bg-gray-400':'bg-blue-600 text-white'}`}
      >
        {status === 'connected' ? 'Connected' : (loading ? 'Connecting…' : 'Connect')}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

Below is Step 37 – Two-Factor Authentication & Secure Login Flows, fully specified and copy-paste ready.

37.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

model User {
  id                   String    @id @default(cuid())
  name                 String?
  email                String?   @unique
  // … other fields …
  twoFactorEnabled     Boolean   @default(false)
  twoFactorSecret      String?   // base32 for TOTP
  phoneNumber          String?   // E.164 format for SMS 2FA
  phone2FAEnabled      Boolean   @default(false)
  // wallet login nonce, etc., lives elsewhere
}
37.2 Helper Library (TOTP & QR)

// lib/2fa.ts
import speakeasy from 'speakeasy';
import qrcode from 'qrcode';

export function generate2FASecret(userEmail: string) {
  const secret = speakeasy.generateSecret({
    name: `Harmony(${userEmail})`,
    length: 20
  });
  return { otpauth_url: secret.otpauth_url!, base32: secret.base32 };
}

export async function generateQRCode(otpauthUrl: string) {
  return qrcode.toDataURL(otpauthUrl);
}

export function verify2FACode(secret: string, token: string) {
  return speakeasy.totp.verify({
    secret,
    encoding: 'base32',
    token,
    window: 1
  });
}
37.3 API Routes

37.3.1 Setup 2FA
// pages/api/2fa/setup.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { generate2FASecret, generateQRCode } from '@/lib/2fa';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session?.user?.email) return res.status(401).end();

  const { otpauth_url, base32 } = generate2FASecret(session.user.email);
  const qr = await generateQRCode(otpauth_url);

  // store secret temporarily; only enable after verify
  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    data: { twoFactorSecret: base32 }
  });

  res.json({ qr, secret: base32 });
};

export default handler;
37.3.2 Verify & Enable 2FA
// pages/api/2fa/verify.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { verify2FACode }     from '@/lib/2fa';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session?.user?.email) return res.status(401).end();

  const { token } = req.body;
  const user      = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: session.user.id } });
  if (!user?.twoFactorSecret) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No secret' });

  const valid = verify2FACode(user.twoFactorSecret, token);
  if (!valid) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid code' });

  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    data: { twoFactorEnabled: true }
  });

  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;
37.3.3 Disable 2FA
// pages/api/2fa/disable.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session?.user?.email) return res.status(401).end();

  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    data: { twoFactorEnabled: false, twoFactorSecret: null }
  });

  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;
37.3.4 Login TOTP Verification
// pages/api/2fa/login.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { verify2FACode }     from '@/lib/2fa';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { userId, token } = req.body;
  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId } });
  if (!user?.twoFactorSecret) return res.status(400).json({ error: '2FA not setup' });

  const valid = verify2FACode(user.twoFactorSecret, token);
  if (!valid) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid code' });

  // issue a session cookie or JWT claim for 2FA passed
  // e.g. set a NextAuth flag in session
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;
37.4 NextAuth Configuration

// pages/api/auth/[...nextauth].ts
import NextAuth from 'next-auth';
import CredentialsProvider from 'next-auth/providers/credentials';
import GoogleProvider      from 'next-auth/providers/google';
import { prisma }          from '@/lib/prisma';
import { verifyPassword }  from '@/lib/auth';

export default NextAuth({
  providers: [
    GoogleProvider({ clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_ID!, clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_SECRET! }),
    CredentialsProvider({
      name: 'Email / Password',
      credentials: { email: {}, password: {} },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: credentials!.email } });
        if (!user || !(await verifyPassword(credentials!.password, user.passwordHash))) {
          throw new Error('Invalid credentials');
        }
        return { id: user.id, email: user.email, twoFactorEnabled: user.twoFactorEnabled };
      }
    }),
    // Wallet login can be added here as a CredentialsProvider or custom provider
  ],
  callbacks: {
    async signIn({ user }) {
      // If 2FA enabled, redirect to /verify-2fa
      if (user.twoFactorEnabled) {
        return `/verify-2fa?userId=${user.id}`;
      }
      return true;
    },
    async session({ session, user }) {
      session.user.id = user.id;
      session.user.twoFactorEnabled = user.twoFactorEnabled;
      // also include 2FA-verified flag from cookie/JWT if implemented
      return session;
    }
  },
  pages: {
    signIn: '/auth/signin',
    // after credential sign-in, NextAuth will redirect to /verify-2fa if needed
  }
});
37.5 Front-End Pages

37.5.1 2FA Setup Page
// pages/2fa-setup.tsx
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function TwoFASetup() {
  const [qr, setQr]       = useState('');
  const [token, setToken] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/2fa/setup')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => setQr(data.qr));
  }, []);

  const verify = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/2fa/verify', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ token })
    });
    if (res.ok) setSuccess(true);
    else {
      const { error } = await res.json();
      setError(error);
    }
  };

  if (success) return <p>2FA Enabled! 🎉</p>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Set Up Two-Factor Authentication</h1>
      {qr && <img src={qr} alt="2FA QR Code" className="mx-auto mb-4" />}
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder="Enter code from your Authenticator"
        value={token}
        onChange={e => setToken(e.target.value)}
        className="border p-2 w-full mb-2"
      />
      {error && <p className="text-red-600">{error}</p>}
      <button onClick={verify} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded">
        Verify & Enable
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
37.5.2 2FA Verify on Login
// pages/verify-2fa.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Verify2FA() {
  const [token, setToken]   = useState('');
  const [error, setError]   = useState('');
  const router              = useRouter();
  const params              = useSearchParams();
  const userId              = params.get('userId');

  const submit = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/2fa/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ userId, token })
    });
    if (res.ok) router.push('/');
    else {
      const { error } = await res.json();
      setError(error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Enter 2FA Code</h1>
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder="123456"
        value={token}
        onChange={e => setToken(e.target.value)}
        className="border p-2 w-full mb-2"
      />
      {error && <p className="text-red-600">{error}</p>}
      <button onClick={submit} className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded">
        Verify & Sign In
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
37.6 Acceptance Criteria

Schema
– User.twoFactorEnabled and twoFactorSecret fields exist.
API
– /api/2fa/setup returns QR + secret.
– /api/2fa/verify enables 2FA after correct TOTP.
– /api/2fa/disable turns it off.
– /api/2fa/login validates TOTP at sign-in.
NextAuth
– Users with 2FA enabled are redirected to /verify-2fa.
– Session callback includes twoFactorEnabled.
UI
– 2FA setup page shows QR, accepts code, handles errors.
– 2FA verify page on login blocks until correct code.
Security
– Secrets stored only in DB; not exposed client-side.
– All 2FA endpoints require an active session.
Tests
– Unit tests for generate2FASecret, verify2FACode.
– Integration tests for setup → verify → login flows.
