38.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// A reusable template for an agent
model AgentTemplate {
  id             String   @id @default(cuid())
  creatorId      String
  name           String
  description    String
  defaultPrompt  String
  capabilities   String[]      // e.g. ["crmAccess", "email"]
  pricingCredits Int           // how many credits per use
  pricingETH     Float?        // optional ETH price for NFT mint
  isPublished    Boolean @default(false)
  nftContract    String?        // on-chain address once minted
  nftTokenId     String?
  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt

  creator        User     @relation(fields: [creatorId], references: [id])
}

/// A marketplace listing for a template
model MarketplaceListing {
  id            String   @id @default(cuid())
  templateId    String   @unique
  priceCredits  Int
  priceETH      Float?
  isActive      Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime @updatedAt

  template      AgentTemplate @relation(fields: [templateId], references: [id])
}

/// A user’s purchased/custom agent instance
model UserAgent {
  id            String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String
  templateId    String
  name          String
  metadata      Json      // merged template + user custom props
  purchasedAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  expiresAt     DateTime? // optional subscription expiry

  user          User        @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  template      AgentTemplate @relation(fields: [templateId], references: [id])
}
38.2 API Routes

38.2.1 Create / Update Agent Template
// pages/api/agent-builder/create.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { name, description, defaultPrompt, capabilities, pricingCredits, pricingETH } = req.body;
  const template = await prisma.agentTemplate.create({
    data: {
      creatorId: session.user.id,
      name, description, defaultPrompt,
      capabilities, pricingCredits, pricingETH
    }
  });
  res.json(template);
};

export default handler;
// pages/api/agent-builder/update.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { id, ...data } = req.body;
  const tpl = await prisma.agentTemplate.update({
    where: { id },
    data,
  });
  res.json(tpl);
};

export default handler;
38.2.2 Publish to Marketplace
// pages/api/agent-builder/publish.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { templateId, priceCredits, priceETH } = req.body;
  // mark template published
  await prisma.agentTemplate.update({
    where: { id: templateId },
    data: { isPublished: true, pricingCredits: priceCredits, pricingETH }
  });
  const listing = await prisma.marketplaceListing.upsert({
    where: { templateId },
    create: { templateId, priceCredits, priceETH },
    update: { priceCredits, priceETH, isActive: true }
  });
  res.json(listing);
};

export default handler;
38.2.3 Fetch Marketplace Listings
// pages/api/marketplace/list.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const listings = await prisma.marketplaceListing.findMany({
    where: { isActive: true },
    include: { template: true }
  });
  res.json(listings);
};

export default handler;
38.2.4 Purchase / Mint Agent
// pages/api/marketplace/buy.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { mintAgentNFT }      from '@/lib/nft';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { listingId, paymentMethod } = req.body;
  const listing = await prisma.marketplaceListing.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: listingId }, include: { template: true }
  });

  // 1) charge credits or ETH off-chain
  // 2) mint NFT if priceETH
  let nftResult = null;
  if (listing.priceETH) {
    nftResult = await mintAgentNFT(session.user.id, listing.template.id);
  }

  // 3) record user’s purchase
  const userAgent = await prisma.userAgent.create({
    data: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      templateId: listing.template.id,
      name: listing.template.name,
      metadata: listing.template, 
    }
  });
  res.json({ userAgent, nftResult });
};

export default handler;

// components/Builder/AgentBuilderForm.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function AgentBuilderForm() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: '', description: '', defaultPrompt: '',
    capabilities: [] as string[],
    pricingCredits: 10, pricingETH: 0
  });

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/agent-builder/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify(form)
    });
    if (res.ok) alert('Template Created!');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <input
        placeholder="Agent Name"
        value={form.name}
        onChange={e => setForm({...form, name: e.target.value})}
        className="border p-2 w-full"
      />
      <textarea
        placeholder="Description"
        value={form.description}
        onChange={e => setForm({...form, description: e.target.value})}
        className="border p-2 w-full"
      />
      <textarea
        placeholder="Default Prompt"
        value={form.defaultPrompt}
        onChange={e => setForm({...form, defaultPrompt: e.target.value})}
        className="border p-2 w-full"
      />
      <input
        type="number"
        placeholder="Credits per Use"
        value={form.pricingCredits}
        onChange={e => setForm({...form, pricingCredits: parseInt(e.target.value)})}
        className="border p-2 w-32"
      />
      <input
        type="number"
        placeholder="ETH Price (opt.)"
        value={form.pricingETH}
        onChange={e => setForm({...form, pricingETH: parseFloat(e.target.value)})}
        className="border p-2 w-32"
      />
      {/* capabilities tags input omitted for brevity */}
      <button
        onClick={handleSubmit}
        className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
      >
        Save Template
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

// components/Marketplace/MarketplacePage.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import AgentCard      from './AgentCard';
import PurchaseModal  from './PurchaseModal';

export default function MarketplacePage() {
  const [listings, setListings] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<any|null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/marketplace/list')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setListings);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6">Agent Marketplace</h1>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {listings.map(l => (
          <AgentCard key={l.id} listing={l} onBuy={() => setSelected(l)} />
        ))}
      </div>
      {selected && (
        <PurchaseModal
          listing={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onSuccess={() => window.location.reload()}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

38.3.5 PurchaseModal.tsx
// components/Marketplace/PurchaseModal.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function PurchaseModal({
  listing, onClose, onSuccess
}: {
  listing: any;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const buy = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/marketplace/buy', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ listingId: listing.id })
    });
    if (res.ok) onSuccess();
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded shadow max-w-sm w-full">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-2">Confirm Purchase</h2>
        <p className="mb-4">Buy <strong>{listing.template.name}</strong> for {listing.priceCredits} credits{listing.priceETH && ` or ${listing.priceETH} ETH`}?</p>
        <div className="flex justify-end space-x-2">
          <button onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2">Cancel</button>
          <button
            onClick={buy}
            disabled={loading}
            className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
          >
            {loading ? 'Processing…' : 'Confirm'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
38.4 NFT Mint Helper

// lib/nft.ts
import { ThirdwebSDK } from '@thirdweb-dev/sdk';

const sdk = new ThirdwebSDK('polygon');

export async function mintAgentNFT(walletAddress: string, templateId: string) {
  const contract = await sdk.getContract(process.env.AGENT_NFT_CONTRACT!);
  const metadata = {
    name: `Agent: ${templateId}`,
    description: `License NFT for agent template ${templateId}`,
    image: '', // optional image URL
    properties: { templateId }
  };
  const tx = await contract.erc721.mintTo(walletAddress, metadata);
  return tx;
}

39.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Supported third-party integration types
enum IntegrationType {
  GOOGLE_OAUTH
  MICROSOFT_OAUTH
  CRM_API
  CALENDAR_API
  EMAIL_SMTP
  SMS_TWILIO
  STRIPE
  SOCIAL_FACEBOOK
  SOCIAL_TWITTER
  SOCIAL_LINKEDIN
  DRIVE_GOOGLE
  DRIVE_ONEDRIVE
  ZAPIER
}

/// Persist each user’s connected integrations
model UserIntegration {
  id             String          @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String
  type           IntegrationType
  credentials    Json            // tokens, API keys
  status         String          // "connected","error"
  requiredFor    String[]        // e.g. ["Cashflow","Grace"]
  createdAt      DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime        @updatedAt

  user           User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}
39.2 Integration Helper Library

// lib/integrations/launchpad.ts

import axios from 'axios';
import { OAuth2Client } from 'google-auth-library';

export async function getOAuthUrl(type: IntegrationType, userId: string): Promise<string> {
  switch(type) {
    case 'GOOGLE_OAUTH':
      const client = new OAuth2Client(
        process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
        process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET,
        `${process.env.APP_URL}/api/integrations/google/callback`
      );
      return client.generateAuthUrl({
        access_type: 'offline',
        scope: ['openid','email','profile','https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive','https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar']
      });
    // add other providers similarly…
    default:
      throw new Error('Unsupported integration type');
  }
}

export async function exchangeToken(type: IntegrationType, code: string): Promise<any> {
  switch(type) {
    case 'GOOGLE_OAUTH':
      const client = new OAuth2Client(
        process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
        process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET,
        `${process.env.APP_URL}/api/integrations/google/callback`
      );
      const { tokens } = await client.getToken(code);
      return tokens;
    // other flows…
    default:
      throw new Error('Unsupported integration type');
  }
}

// pages/api/integrations/required.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';

const REQUIRED: Record<string,string[]> = {
  Cashflow:   ['CRM_API','STRIPE'],
  Grace:      ['CALENDAR_API','EMAIL_SMTP'],
  IRIS:       ['SOCIAL_TWITTER','SOCIAL_LINKEDIN'],
  Atlas:      ['CRM_API'],
  SŌL:        [],
  // etc.
};

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const { agent } = req.query as { agent: string };
  res.json({ required: REQUIRED[agent] || [] });
};

export default handler;
39.3.2 Get OAuth URL
// pages/api/integrations/[type]/url.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { getOAuthUrl }       from '@/lib/integrations/launchpad';
import { IntegrationType }   from '@prisma/client';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const type = req.query.type as IntegrationType;
  const url  = await getOAuthUrl(type, session.user.id);
  res.json({ url });
};

export default handler;
39.3.3 OAuth Callback & Store Credentials
// pages/api/integrations/google/callback.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { exchangeToken }     from '@/lib/integrations/launchpad';
import { IntegrationType }   from '@prisma/client';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { code } = req.query as { code: string };
  const tokens   = await exchangeToken(IntegrationType.GOOGLE_OAUTH, code);

  await prisma.userIntegration.upsert({
    where: { userId_type: { userId: session.user.id, type: IntegrationType.GOOGLE_OAUTH } },
    create: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      type: IntegrationType.GOOGLE_OAUTH,
      credentials: tokens,
      status: 'connected',
      requiredFor: []
    },
    update: {
      credentials: tokens,
      status: 'connected'
    }
  });

  // redirect back to launchpad
  res.redirect('/launchpad');
};

export default handler;

39.4.1 LaunchpadPage.tsx
// components/Launchpad/LaunchpadPage.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import ProgressStepper        from './ProgressStepper';
import IntegrationStatusCard  from './IntegrationStatusCard';
import ConnectButton          from './ConnectButton';

export default function LaunchpadPage() {
  const [required, setRequired] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [connected, setConnected] = useState<Record<string,string>>({});
  const AGENTS = ['Cashflow','Grace','IRIS','Atlas']; // etc.

  // 1) Determine which integrations user still needs
  useEffect(() => {
    // flatten all required integrations for core agents
    Promise.all(AGENTS.map(a => fetch(`/api/integrations/required?agent=${a}`).then(r=>r.json())))
      .then(arr => {
        const req = Array.from(new Set(arr.flatMap(x=>x.required)));
        setRequired(req);
      });
    // 2) fetch user's existing integrations
    fetch('/api/integrations/status') // next step route: /status returns all userIntegration rows
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        const map: Record<string,string> = {};
        data.forEach((u: any) => { map[u.type] = u.status; });
        setConnected(map);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto p-6 space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Launchpad Onboarding</h1>
      <ProgressStepper required={required} connected={connected} />
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {required.map(type => (
          <IntegrationStatusCard
            key={type}
            type={type}
            status={connected[type] || 'not connected'}
          >
            <ConnectButton type={type} />
          </IntegrationStatusCard>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
39.4.2 ProgressStepper.tsx
// components/Launchpad/ProgressStepper.tsx
'use client';

export default function ProgressStepper({
  required, connected
}: {
  required: string[];
  connected: Record<string,string>;
}) {
  const done = required.filter(t => connected[t] === 'connected').length;
  const percent = Math.round((done / required.length) * 100 || 0);

  return (
    <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-4">
      <div
        className="bg-green-600 h-4 rounded-full"
        style={{ width: `${percent}%` }}
      />
      <p className="text-sm mt-1">{percent}% complete</p>
    </div>
  );
}
39.4.3 IntegrationStatusCard.tsx
// components/Launchpad/IntegrationStatusCard.tsx

export default function IntegrationStatusCard({
  type, status, children
}: {
  type: string;
  status: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {

  return (
    <div className={`${color} border p-4 rounded flex justify-between items-center`}>
      <div>
        <h3 className="font-semibold">{type}</h3>
        <p className="text-sm">Status: <strong>{status}</strong></p>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}
39.4.4 ConnectButton.tsx
// components/Launchpad/ConnectButton.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function ConnectButton({ type }: { type: string }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handle = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/integrations/${type}/url`);
    const { url } = await res.json();
    window.location.href = url;
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handle}
      disabled={loading}
      className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
    >
      {loading ? 'Loading…' : 'Connect'}
    </button>
  );
}

// schema.prisma

/// A demo asset (video, interactive sample, code snippet)
model Demo {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  title       String
  description String
  type        String    // e.g. "video", "interactive", "snippet"
  url         String    // embed link or file path
  tags        String[]  // e.g. ["Cashflow","Training"]
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt
}

/// A frequently asked question entry
model FAQ {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  question   String
  answer     String
  category   String   // e.g. "MarketPlace", "Builder", "Billing"
  sortOrder  Int      @default(0)
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt  DateTime @updatedAt
}

// pages/api/faq/create.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session || session.user.role !== 'admin') return res.status(403).end();

  const { question, answer, category, sortOrder } = req.body;
  const faq = await prisma.fAQ.create({ data: { question, answer, category, sortOrder } });
  res.json(faq);
};

export default handler;
// pages/api/faq/update.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session || session.user.role !== 'admin') return res.status(403).end();

  const { id, question, answer, category, sortOrder } = req.body;
  const faq = await prisma.fAQ.update({
    where: { id },
    data: { question, answer, category, sortOrder }
  });
  res.json(faq);
};

export default handler;

export default function DemoLibraryPage() {
  const [demos, setDemos] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<any|null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/demos/list')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setDemos);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4">Interactive Demo Library</h1>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {demos.map(d => (
          <DemoCard key={d.id} demo={d} onSelect={() => setSelected(d)} />
        ))}
      </div>
      {selected && (
        <DemoModal demo={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

41.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Track each agent invocation and estimated time saved
model AgentUsage {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String
  agentId       String
  invokedAt     DateTime  @default(now())
  estimatedMinutesSaved Int  // minutes saved per invocation
  // link back to user
  user          User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// Store user’s personal billing settings for ROI conversion
model UserSettings {
  id            String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String    @unique
  hourlyRate    Float     @default(50.0)  // $/hour
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime  @updatedAt

  user          User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}
41.2 API Routes

41.2.1 Log Agent Usage
// pages/api/usage/log.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { agentId, minutesSaved } = req.body as { agentId: string; minutesSaved: number };
  const record = await prisma.agentUsage.create({
    data: {
      userId: session.user.id,
      agentId,
      estimatedMinutesSaved: minutesSaved
    }
  });
  res.json(record);
};

export default handler;
41.2.2 Fetch ROI Summary
// pages/api/usage/summary.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const userId = session.user.id;
  // total minutes saved
  const result = await prisma.agentUsage.aggregate({
    where: { userId },
    _sum: { estimatedMinutesSaved: true },
    _count: { id: true }
  });
  // fetch user's hourly rate
  const settings = await prisma.userSettings.findUnique({ where: { userId } });
  const minutes = result._sum.estimatedMinutesSaved || 0;
  const hours = minutes / 60;
  const rate  = settings?.hourlyRate || 50;
  const value = hours * rate;

  res.json({
    totalInvocations: result._count.id,
    totalMinutesSaved: minutes,
    totalHoursSaved: hours,
    hourlyRate: rate,
    dollarValueSaved: value
  });
};

export default handler;
41.2.3 Update Hourly Rate
// pages/api/settings/hourlyRate.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { hourlyRate } = req.body as { hourlyRate: number };
  const userId = session.user.id;

  const settings = await prisma.userSettings.upsert({
    where: { userId },
    create: { userId, hourlyRate },
    update: { hourlyRate }
  });
  res.json(settings);
};

export default handler;

41.3.1 ROICalculatorPage.tsx
// components/ROICalculator/ROICalculatorPage.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import RateInput       from './RateInput';
import SummaryCard     from './SummaryCard';
import ProjectionChart from './ProjectionChart';

type Summary = {
  totalInvocations: number;
  totalMinutesSaved: number;
  totalHoursSaved: number;
  hourlyRate: number;
  dollarValueSaved: number;
};

export default function ROICalculatorPage() {
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<Summary|null>(null);

  // fetch summary on load
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/usage/summary')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setSummary);
  }, []);

  if (!summary) return <p>Loading...</p>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Time-Save ROI Calculator</h1>
      <RateInput initialRate={summary.hourlyRate} onUpdate={() => {
        // refetch summary after rate change
        fetch('/api/usage/summary')
          .then(r => r.json())
          .then(setSummary);
      }} />
      <SummaryCard summary={summary} />
      <ProjectionChart
        hoursSaved={summary.totalHoursSaved}
        rate={summary.hourlyRate}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
41.3.2 RateInput.tsx
// components/ROICalculator/RateInput.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function RateInput({
  initialRate, onUpdate
}: {
  initialRate: number;
  onUpdate: () => void;
}) {
  const [rate, setRate] = useState<number>(initialRate);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const save = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    await fetch('/api/settings/hourlyRate', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ hourlyRate: rate })
    });
    setLoading(false);
    onUpdate();
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
      <label className="font-medium">Your Hourly Rate ($):</label>
      <input
        type="number"
        value={rate}
        onChange={e => setRate(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
        className="border px-2 py-1 rounded w-24"
      />
      <button
        onClick={save}
        disabled={loading}
        className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-1 rounded"
      >
        {loading ? 'Saving…' : 'Save'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

      <h2 className="font-semibold mb-2">Your Impact so far</h2>

        <li>Hourly Rate: <strong>${hourlyRate.toFixed(2)}/hr</strong></li>

41.3.4 ProjectionChart.tsx
// components/ROICalculator/ProjectionChart.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function ProjectionChart({
  hoursSaved, rate
}: { hoursSaved: number; rate: number }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{ month: string; value: number }[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // simple linear projection over next 6 months
    const months = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun'];
    const arr = months.map((m, i) => ({
      month: m,
      value: (hoursSaved * (i+1)) * rate
    }));
    setData(arr);
  }, [hoursSaved, rate]);

  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg p-4 bg-white">
      <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">6-Month Projection ($)</h3>
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {data.map(d => <th key={d.month}>{d.month}</th>)}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              {data.map(d => <td key={d.month} className="text-center">${d.value.toFixed(0)}</td>)}
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

42.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

/// Supported CRM & analytics sources
enum CRMType {
  HUBSPOT
  HIGHLEVEL
  CLICKUP
  ZAPIER
}

/// Persist each user’s CRM integration credentials
model CRMIntegration {
  id           String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String
  type         CRMType
  credentials  Json      // e.g. API keys, OAuth tokens
  status       String    // "connected" | "error"
  createdAt    DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime  @updatedAt

  user         User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// Store KPI metrics pulled from CRMs
model KPIRecord {
  id           String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String
  source       CRMType   // which CRM
  metric       String    // e.g. "dealsClosed", "pipelineValue", "tasksCompleted"
  value        Float
  timestamp    DateTime  @default(now())

  user         User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}
42.2 CRM Helper Library

// lib/crm/index.ts

import axios from 'axios';
import { CRMType } from '@prisma/client';

export async function fetchHubSpotMetrics(apiKey: string) {
  const url = `https://api.hubapi.com/deals/v1/deal/paged?hapikey=${apiKey}`;
  const resp = await axios.get(url);
  const deals = resp.data.deals || [];
  return {
    dealsClosed: deals.filter((d: any) => d.properties.dealstage.value === 'closedwon').length,
    pipelineValue: deals.reduce((sum: number, d: any) => sum + parseFloat(d.properties.amount.value || 0), 0)
  };
}

export async function fetchHighLevelMetrics(token: string) {
  const resp = await axios.get('https://api.gohighlevel.com/v1/contacts', {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
  });
  const contacts = resp.data.contacts || [];
  return {
    contactsCount: contacts.length
    // add more endpoints as needed
  };
}

export async function fetchClickUpMetrics(token: string) {
  const resp = await axios.get('https://api.clickup.com/api/v2/team', {
    headers: { Authorization: token }
  });
  const teams = resp.data.teams || [];
  // for demo, return count of teams as tasksCompleted
  return { tasksCompleted: teams.length };
}

42.3.1 List CRM Integrations
// pages/api/crm/list.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req: _req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const list = await prisma.cRMIntegration.findMany({
    where: { userId: session.user.id }
  });
  res.json(list);
};

export default handler;
42.3.2 Sync KPI
// pages/api/kpi/sync.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { fetchHubSpotMetrics, fetchHighLevelMetrics, fetchClickUpMetrics } from '@/lib/crm';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req: _req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const userId = session.user.id;

  const integrations = await prisma.cRMIntegration.findMany({ where: { userId, status: 'connected' } });
  const records: any[] = [];

  for (const integ of integrations) {
    let metrics: Record<string, number> = {};
    try {
      switch (integ.type) {
        case 'HUBSPOT':
          metrics = await fetchHubSpotMetrics(integ.credentials.apiKey);
          break;
        case 'HIGHLEVEL':
          metrics = await fetchHighLevelMetrics(integ.credentials.token);
          break;
        case 'CLICKUP':
          metrics = await fetchClickUpMetrics(integ.credentials.token);
          break;
      }
      for (const [metric, value] of Object.entries(metrics)) {
        records.push(await prisma.kPIRecord.create({
          data: { userId, source: integ.type, metric, value: value as number }
        }));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // mark integration error if needed
      await prisma.cRMIntegration.update({
        where: { id: integ.id },
        data: { status: 'error' }
      });
    }
  }

  res.json({ synced: records.length });
};

export default handler;
42.3.3 Fetch KPI Summary
// pages/api/kpi/summary.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req: _req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();
  const userId = session.user.id;

  // aggregate by metric
  const aggs = await prisma.kPIRecord.groupBy({
    by: ['metric'],
    where: { userId },
    _sum: { value: true }
  });

  return res.json(aggs.map(a => ({
    metric: a.metric,
    total: a._sum.value || 0
  })));
};

export default handler;

43.1 Schema Updates (Prisma)

// schema.prisma

model User {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  email           String    @unique
  name            String?
  // existing fields…
  twoFA           TwoFA?    @relation(fields: [twoFAId], references: [id])
  twoFAId         String?
  phoneAuth       PhoneAuth?@relation(fields: [phoneAuthId], references: [id])
  phoneAuthId     String?
  wallets         Wallet[]  // for crypto-login
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt
}

/// TOTP-based 2FA
model TwoFA {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String   @unique
  secret     String   // base32 TOTP secret
  enabled    Boolean  @default(false)
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt  DateTime @updatedAt

  user       User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// SMS-based 2FA
model PhoneAuth {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String   @unique
  phoneNumber  String
  code         String?  // last sent OTP
  codeExpires  DateTime?
  verified     Boolean  @default(false)
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime @updatedAt

  user         User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// Wallet-based login
model Wallet {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String
  address      String   @unique
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())

  user         User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}
43.2 NextAuth Configuration

// pages/api/auth/[...nextauth].ts
import NextAuth from 'next-auth';
import EmailProvider from 'next-auth/providers/email';
import GoogleProvider from 'next-auth/providers/google';
import AppleProvider   from 'next-auth/providers/apple';
import CredentialsProvider from 'next-auth/providers/credentials';
import { PrismaAdapter } from '@next-auth/prisma-adapter';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
// for Ethereum.sig verification
import { verifyMessage } from 'ethers/lib/utils';

export default NextAuth({
  adapter: PrismaAdapter(prisma),
  session: { strategy: 'jwt' },
  providers: [
    EmailProvider({ /* SMTP config via env */ }),
    GoogleProvider({ clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_ID!, clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_SECRET! }),
    AppleProvider({ clientId: process.env.APPLE_ID!, clientSecret: process.env.APPLE_SECRET! }),
    CredentialsProvider({
      id: 'ethereum',
      name: 'Ethereum',
      credentials: {
        message: { label: 'Message', type: 'text' },
        signature: { label: 'Signature', type: 'text' }
      },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        try {
          const { message, signature } = credentials!;
          const address = verifyMessage(message, signature);
          let wallet = await prisma.wallet.findUnique({ where: { address } });
          if (!wallet) {
            // No associated user, create new user
            const user = await prisma.user.create({ data: { email: `${address}@wallet.local` } });
            wallet = await prisma.wallet.create({ data: { address, userId: user.id } });
          }
          return { id: wallet.userId, email: `${address}@wallet.local` };
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }
    })
  ],
  callbacks: {
    async jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) token.userId = user.id;
      return token;
    },
    async session({ session, token }) {
      if (token.userId) session.user.id = token.userId as string;
      return session;
    }
  },
  pages: {
    signIn: '/auth/signin'
  },
  secret: process.env.NEXTAUTH_SECRET
});
43.3 2FA API Routes

43.3.1 Generate TOTP Secret & QR
// pages/api/auth/2fa/generate.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { authenticator }     from 'otplib';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const secret = authenticator.generateSecret();
  const otpauth = authenticator.keyuri(session.user.email!, 'Harmony', secret);

  // upsert TwoFA
  await prisma.twoFA.upsert({
    where:   { userId: session.user.id },
    create:  { userId: session.user.id, secret },
    update:  { secret }
  });

  res.json({ secret, otpauth });
};

export default handler;
43.3.2 Verify TOTP Code
// pages/api/auth/2fa/verify.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { authenticator }     from 'otplib';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { code } = req.body as { code: string };
  const record = await prisma.twoFA.findUnique({ where: { userId: session.user.id } });
  if (!record) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Not initialized' });

  const valid = authenticator.check(code, record.secret);
  if (valid) {
    await prisma.twoFA.update({ where: { userId: session.user.id }, data: { enabled: true } });
    return res.json({ success: true });
  } else {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid code' });
  }
};

export default handler;
43.3.3 Disable TOTP
// pages/api/auth/2fa/disable.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  await prisma.twoFA.update({ where: { userId: session.user.id }, data: { enabled: false } });
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;
43.4 SMS 2FA API Routes

43.4.1 Send Code
// pages/api/auth/phone/send.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { randomInt }         from 'crypto';
import { addMinutes }        from 'date-fns';
import { sendSMS }           from '@/lib/sms'; // your SMS util

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { phoneNumber } = req.body as { phoneNumber: string };
  const code = String(randomInt(100000, 999999));
  const expires = addMinutes(new Date(), 10);

  await prisma.phoneAuth.upsert({
    where: { userId: session.user.id },
    create: { userId: session.user.id, phoneNumber, code, codeExpires: expires },
    update: { phoneNumber, code, codeExpires: expires, verified: false }
  });

  await sendSMS(phoneNumber, `Your Harmony login code is ${code}`);
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;
43.4.2 Verify Code
// pages/api/auth/phone/verify.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { code } = req.body as { code: string };
  const record = await prisma.phoneAuth.findUnique({ where: { userId: session.user.id } });
  if (!record) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Not initialized' });
  if (record.code !== code || record.codeExpires! < new Date()) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid or expired code' });
  }

  await prisma.phoneAuth.update({ where: { userId: session.user.id }, data: { verified: true } });
  res.json({ success: true });
};

export default handler;

43.5.2 TOTPSetup.tsx
// components/Security/TOTPSetup.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import QRCode from 'qrcode.react';

export default function TOTPSetup({
  enabled, onStatusChange
}: {
  enabled: boolean;
  onStatusChange: (s: {enabled:boolean}) => void;
}) {
  const [otpauth, setOtpauth] = useState<string|null>(null);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'view'|'verify'>('view');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');

  const init = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/2fa/generate');
    const { otpauth: url } = await res.json();
    setOtpauth(url);
    setStep('verify');
  };

  const verify = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/2fa/verify', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    if (res.ok) onStatusChange({ enabled: true });
  };

  const disable = async () => {
    await fetch('/api/auth/2fa/disable');
    onStatusChange({ enabled: false });
    setOtpauth(null);
    setStep('view');
  };

  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg p-4">
      <h2 className="font-semibold">Authenticator App (TOTP)</h2>
      {enabled ? (
        <button onClick={disable} className="mt-2 text-red-600">Disable 2FA</button>
      ) : (
        <>
          <button onClick={init} className="mt-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
            Set up 2FA
          </button>
          {step === 'verify' && otpauth && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <QRCode value={otpauth} />
              <input
                className="border px-2 py-1 mt-2"
                placeholder="Enter code"
                value={code}
                onChange={e => setCode(e.target.value)}
              />
              <button onClick={verify} className="ml-2 bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
                Verify
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
43.5.3 Phone2FASetup.tsx
// components/Security/Phone2FASetup.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Phone2FASetup() {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode]   = useState('');
  const [step, setStep]   = useState<'input'|'verify'>('input');

  const send = async () => {
    await fetch('/api/auth/phone/send', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({ phoneNumber: phone })
    });
    setStep('verify');
  };
  const verify = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/phone/verify', {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({ code })
    });
    if (res.ok) setStep('done');
  };

  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg p-4">
      <h2 className="font-semibold">SMS Two-Factor</h2>
      {step === 'input' ? (
        <>
          <input
            type="tel"
            placeholder="+1-234-567-8901"
            value={phone}
            onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)}
            className="border px-2 py-1"
          />
          <button onClick={send} className="ml-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">Send Code</button>
        </>
      ) : step === 'verify' ? (
        <>
          <input
            placeholder="Enter code"
            value={code}
            onChange={e => setCode(e.target.value)}
            className="border px-2 py-1"
          />
          <button onClick={verify} className="ml-2 bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">Verify</button>
        </>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-green-700">Phone verified ✅</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

44.1 Prisma Schema Additions

// schema.prisma

/// Logs every user–agent interaction
model AgentInteraction {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  agentId     String
  input       String
  output      String
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  user        User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

/// Tracks each batch ingestion into the master knowledge store
model IngestionLog {
  id               String    @id @default(cuid())
  lastInteraction  DateTime  // timestamp up to which we ingested
  ingestedAt       DateTime  @default(now())
}
44.2 Vector Store & Embedding Helpers

// lib/learning/embeddings.ts

import OpenAI from 'openai';
import { PineconeClient } from '@pinecone-database/pinecone';

const openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY! });
const pinecone = new PineconeClient();

export async function initPinecone() {
  await pinecone.init({
    apiKey: process.env.PINECONE_API_KEY!,
    environment: process.env.PINECONE_ENV!,
  });
  return pinecone.Index(process.env.PINECONE_INDEX!);
}

export async function ingestInteractions(interactions: {
  id: string; input: string; output: string;
}[]) {
  const index = await initPinecone();
  const upserts = [];

  for (const { id, input, output } of interactions) {
    // generate embedding from combined text
    const text = `${input}\n\n${output}`;
    const { data } = await openai.embeddings.create({
      model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
      input: text
    });
    const vector = data[0].embedding;

    upserts.push({
      id,
      values: vector,
      metadata: { text }
    });
  }
  await index.upsert({ upsertRequest: { vectors: upserts } });
}
44.3 API Routes

44.3.1 Ingest New Interactions
// pages/api/learning/ingest.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';
import { ingestInteractions } from '@/lib/learning/embeddings';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (_, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req: _ });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  // find last ingestion
  const lastLog = await prisma.ingestionLog.findFirst({ orderBy: { ingestedAt: 'desc' } });
  const cutoff   = lastLog?.lastInteraction || new Date(0);

  // fetch new interactions
  const interactions = await prisma.agentInteraction.findMany({
    where: { createdAt: { gt: cutoff } },
    select: { id: true, input: true, output: true }
  });
  if (interactions.length === 0) {
    return res.json({ ingested: 0 });
  }

  // ingest into vector store
  await ingestInteractions(interactions);

  // record ingestion
  await prisma.ingestionLog.create({
    data: { lastInteraction: interactions[interactions.length - 1].createdAt }
  });

  res.json({ ingested: interactions.length });
};

export default handler;
44.3.2 Query Master Knowledge
// pages/api/learning/query.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { initPinecone }      from '@/lib/learning/embeddings';
import OpenAI                from 'openai';

const openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY! });

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const { question } = req.body as { question: string };
  const index = await initPinecone();

  // embed question
  const embeddingResp = await openai.embeddings.create({
    model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
    input: question
  });
  const vector = embeddingResp.data[0].embedding;

  // query top 5 contexts
  const queryResp = await index.query({
    queryRequest: { vector, topK: 5, includeMetadata: true }
  });

  // extract metadata.text
  const contexts = queryResp.matches?.map(m => m.metadata.text).join('\n\n---\n\n');

  // ask GPT for answer with context
  const chat = await openai.chat.completions.create({
    model: 'gpt-4o',
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: 'You are the Harmony Master Agent, synthesizing past interactions.' },
      { role: 'user', content: `Context:\n${contexts}\n\nQuestion:\n${question}` }
    ]
  });

  res.json({ answer: chat.choices[0].message.content });
};

export default handler;

44.4.1 IngestButton.tsx
// components/Learning/IngestButton.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function IngestButton() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [count, setCount]     = useState<number|null>(null);

  const handle = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/learning/ingest', { method: 'POST' });
    const data = await res.json();
    setCount(data.ingested);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <button
        onClick={handle}
        disabled={loading}
        className="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
      >
        {loading ? 'Ingesting…' : 'Ingest New Interactions'}
      </button>
      {count !== null && <p className="mt-2">{count} interactions ingested.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
44.4.2 QueryMaster.tsx
// components/Learning/QueryMaster.tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function QueryMaster() {
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('');
  const [answer, setAnswer]     = useState<string|null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(false);

  const ask = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/learning/query', {
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ question })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    setAnswer(data.answer);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <textarea
        className="w-full border p-2"
        rows={3}
        placeholder="Ask the Master Agent anything…"
        value={question}
        onChange={e => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
      />
      <button
        onClick={ask}
        disabled={loading}
        className="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded"
      >
        {loading ? 'Thinking…' : 'Ask Master Agent'}
      </button>
      {answer && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Answer:</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">{answer}</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

44.5 Cron Job Trigger

Configure your host (e.g. Vercel Cron, GitHub Actions) to call /api/learning/ingest daily.
Example (GitHub Actions):

# .github/workflows/ingest.yml
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 2 * * *'  # every day at 2 AM UTC
jobs:
  ingest:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      - name: Ingest Interactions
        run: |
          curl -X POST https://your-domain.com/api/learning/ingest \
            -H "Authorization: Bearer ${{ secrets.NEXTAUTH_SECRET }}"

Below is Step 45: ROI & Time-Saving Calculator, fully detailed and copy/paste ready for your dev team.

45.1 Prisma Schema Additions

// schema.prisma

/// Tracks time estimates per agent action
model AgentTimeEstimate {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  agentId     String   @unique
  minutesPer  Float    // estimated minutes saved per action
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime @updatedAt
}

/// Logs each agent usage event
model AgentUsage {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  agentId     String
  actions     Int      @default(1)   // number of actions invoked
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  user        User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}
45.2 Backend: ROI Calculation API

// pages/api/metrics/timeSavings.ts
import type { NextApiHandler } from 'next';
import { getSession }        from 'next-auth/react';
import { prisma }            from '@/lib/prisma';

const handler: NextApiHandler = async (req, res) => {
  const session = await getSession({ req });
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end();

  const userId = session.user.id;

  // fetch usage and time estimates
  const usages = await prisma.agentUsage.groupBy({
    by: ['agentId'],
    where: { userId },
    _sum: { actions: true }
  });

  const estimates = await prisma.agentTimeEstimate.findMany();

  // calculate savings per agent
  const results = usages.map(u => {
    const estimate = estimates.find(e => e.agentId === u.agentId);
    const sumActions = u._sum.actions || 0;
    const minutesPer = estimate?.minutesPer || 0;
    const minutesSaved = sumActions * minutesPer;
    return {
      agentId: u.agentId,
      actions: sumActions,
      minutesSaved,
    };
  });

  // total savings
  const totalMinutes = results.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.minutesSaved, 0);
  const totalHours   = totalMinutes / 60;
  const hourlyRate   = Number(process.env.AVERAGE_HOURLY_RATE || 50); // default $50/hr
  const dollarsSaved = totalHours * hourlyRate;

  res.json({ results, totalMinutes, totalHours, dollarsSaved });
};

export default handler;

45.3.2 TimeSavingsCard.tsx
// components/Metrics/TimeSavingsCard.tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

type AgentResult = {
  agentId: string;
  actions: number;
  minutesSaved: number;
};

export default function TimeSavingsCard() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{
    results: AgentResult[];
    totalMinutes: number;
    totalHours: number;
    dollarsSaved: number;
  } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/metrics/timeSavings')
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(setData);
  }, []);

  if (!data) return <p>Loading savings…</p>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 bg-white rounded shadow max-w-lg mx-auto">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-2">Time-Saving & ROI</h2>
      <ul className="space-y-2 mb-4">
        {data.results.map(r => (
          <li key={r.agentId} className="flex justify-between">
            <span>{r.agentId}</span>
            <span>{Math.round(r.minutesSaved)} min saved</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className="border-t pt-4">
        <p>Total time saved: <strong>{data.totalHours.toFixed(1)} hrs</strong></p>
        <p>Value at ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_HOURLY_RATE || '50'}/hr: <strong>${data.dollarsSaved.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
